{{Top}}
{{Update|[[Reign of the Empire: The Mask of Fear]], [[Star Wars 100 Objects]], [[The Art of Star Wars: The High Republic: Volume I]]}}
{{Quote|We must keep our faith in the Republic. The day we stop believing democracy can work is the day we lose it.''"<br />"''Let's pray that day never comes.|Queen Jamillia and Padmé Amidala, discussing the Separatist Crisis|<ref name="AOTC">{{Film|II}}</ref>}}
[[File:2000_meeting.png|thumb|right|350px|Senators demanded Chancellor Palpatine to return to democratic principles in the last days of the Republic.]]
'''Democracy''' was a form of social organization that involved mass [[Politics|political]] participation in a state's decision-making processes and individual freedoms. The [[Galactic Republic]] maintained a [[Republic Era|thousand years of democracy]] across sovereign [[star system]]s through the [[Galactic Senate]], but its democratic processes were eroded by personal and corporate interests that placed greed above common interests throughout [[the galaxy]]. Such corruption was exploited by the [[Dark Lord of the Sith|Sith Lord]] [[Darth Sidious]], who undermined the Senate by engineering the [[Invasion of Naboo|Naboo Crisis]] and was secretly Sheev Palpatine, who was elected the [[Supreme Chancellor]]. Despite the democratic principles of [[senator]]s such as [[Padmé Amidala Naberrie|Padmé Amidala]], Palpatine gained increasing power over the Senate during the [[Separatist Crisis]], which spiraled into the [[Clone Wars]] against the [[Confederacy of Independent Systems|Separatists]]. At the end of the war, he ordered the [[Great Jedi Purge|destruction]] of the [[Jedi Order]], paving way for the Republic's full transition into the [[Fascism|fascist]] [[dictatorship]] of the [[Galactic Empire]].

[[Galactic Emperor|Emperor]] Palpatine's loyal bureaucrats and the [[Imperial Military|Imperial war machine]] suppressed the people of the [[galaxy]] as the [[Imperial Senate|Senate]] became decreasingly relevant to governmental affairs, and the Empire was eventually overthrown by [[Alliance to Restore the Republic|the Rebellion]], giving way to the [[New Republic]]. However, its system of representative democracy was weakened by growing divides in the aftermath of the [[Galactic Civil War]]. Due to its complacency and the machinations of [[Darth]] Sidious and his [[Sith Eternal|Sith cult]], the New Republic collapsed as the [[Neo-Imperialism|neo-Imperial]] [[First Order]] [[First Order-Resistance War|conquered]] the galaxy. However, [[Leia Skywalker Organa Solo|Leia Organa]]'s [[Resistance]] movement mobilized hopes for freedom. Among them was a new generation of freedom fighters led by [[Rey Skywalker]], [[Finn]], and [[Poe Dameron]], and they eventually rallied a [[Citizens' Fleet|fleet of galactic citizens]] to [[Battle of Exegol|defeat]] Sidious' plans for a new [[Sith Empire]], liberating the galaxy.

==Description==
===Peace and prosperity===
{{Quote|I don't think the system works.''"<br />"''How would you have it work?''"<br />"''We need a system where the politicians sit down and discuss the problem, agree what's in the interest of all the people, and then do it.''"<br />"''That's exactly what we do. The&hellip; the trouble is that people don't always agree.''"<br />"''Well, then they should be made to.|Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala exchange political views while on vacation|<ref name="AOTC" />}}
[[File:Millennial_Celebration_Invitation.png|thumb|left|240px|The Republic supported peace and democracy for a thousand years.]]
Democracy was a form of social organization that involved free [[Politics|political]] participation in a state's decision-making processes. [[Timeline of galactic history|Galactic history]] alternated between a mixture of democracies and [[dictatorship]]s, and the [[Galactic Republic]] was founded after the fall of the [[Sith Empire]] around [[1032 BBY]]. The Republic was a democratic union of thousands of worlds in much of [[the galaxy]]<ref name="TSWB">''[[The Star Wars Book]]''</ref> that maintained a [[Republic Era|thousand years of democracy]], symbolized and upheld by the [[Galactic Senate]].<ref name="TPM">{{Film|I}}</ref> The Senate was a representative democracy<ref name="Kempshall">''[[Star Wars: The Rise and Fall of the Galactic Empire]]''</ref> comprised of [[senator]]s and [[Junior Representative|representatives]]<ref name="AOTC" /> of their respective [[homeworld]]s,<ref name="Galactic Republic">{{Databank|galactic-republic|Galactic Republic}}</ref> [[star systems]],<ref name="AOTC" /> and [[sector]]s<ref name="Shadow">''[[Queen's Shadow]]''</ref>&mdash;with each member of the Republic being recognized as sovereign in their own right<ref name="TPM" />&mdash;who were elected to cooperate extensively through discussion and debate in the best interest of all the<ref name="AOTC" /> trillions of beings in the Republic,<ref name="TSWB" /> passing legislation to solve the Republic's issues.<ref name="AOTC" />

Under Republic law, personal freedoms and civil liberties were guaranteed,<ref name="Ever Been">{{Andor|Ever Been to Ghorman?}}</ref> such as the freedom from [[slavery]],<ref name="TPM" /> the freedom to form sovereign communities with self-determination,<ref name="Liberty">{{TCW|Liberty on Ryloth}}</ref> the freedom to access basic social services, education, healthcare, and infrastructure, and the freedom of expression without being subject to violence.<ref name="Pursuit TCW">{{TCW|Pursuit of Peace}}</ref> These rights for citizens of the Republic were protected by government agencies, and the extent to which the state played a role in protecting such rights could be changed by the Senate.<ref name="TSWB" /> Each senator was responsible for representing both their constituents and the government as a whole, and these politicians needed to reconcile their conflicting responsibilities, which were especially frequent in wartime.<ref name="Front">{{FantasyFlightGames|url=en/news/2019/5/22/soldiers-and-politicians|text=On the Front Lines|date=2019-05-22}}</ref>

The Senate convened in the [[Federal District]]'s [[Galactic Senate Chamber|Senate Rotunda]] of the Republic [[capital]] [[planet]] [[Coruscant]].<ref name="TSWB" /> Aside from making law, the legislature could elect the [[Supreme Chancellor]] to lead the Republic government and hold the government to account, with senators' work including attending committees and establishing commissions of inquiry to scrutinize particular issues.<ref name="TPM" /> The [[Office of the Chancellor]] had limited unilateral executive powers over the Senate,<ref name="AOTC" /> though the system was subject to [[bureaucrat]]ic procedures<ref name="TPM" /> that could be influenced by special-interest lobbies.<ref name="Tarkin">[[Tarkin (novel)|''Tarkin'']]</ref> Also within the Federal District sat the [[Galactic Courts of Justice]], the [[Imperial Executive Building|Senate Office Building]], and the [[Imperial Palace|Jedi Temple]]<ref name="CL2016">[[Star Wars: Complete Locations (2016)|''Star Wars: Complete Locations'']]</ref>&mdash;the Republic's [[Millennium|one thousand years]] of democracy coexisted with one thousand years of peace<ref name="AOTC" /> due to political stability and the effectiveness of the [[Jedi Order]].<ref name="TSWB" />

===Monopoly of violence===
{{Quote|If you offer the Separatists violence, they can only show us violence in return! Many will lose their lives. All will lose their freedom.|Padmé Amidala|<ref name="AOTC" />}}
[[File:Political_Doubletalk.png|thumb|right|260px|''Political Doubletalk'' poster campaigning against Republic militarization]]
The Republic was supported by the [[Jedi]], peacekeepers trained to use their strong connections with the mystical energy field known as [[the Force]]. The Jedi were committed to [[Light side of the Force|light]] and [[Living Force|life]], safeguarding peace and justice throughout the [[galaxy]] and sometimes being tasked by the Senate to act on its behalf as negotiators.<ref name="THRCE">''[[Star Wars: The High Republic Character Encyclopedia]]''</ref> Jedi had a responsibility to uphold the [[Jedi Code]] and protect all people of the galaxy, providing a moral and spiritual foundation to their work in a galaxy that enjoyed an absence of large-scale warfare for a thousand years.<ref name="Front" /> The Republic did not maintain a standing military until its final years, though [[Republic Defense Coalition treaties|treaties]] allowed some member worlds to contribute their local military assets to an overarching [[Republic Defense Coalition]] for the duration of specific emergencies, such as the [[Nihil conflict]].<ref name="TSWB" /> The [[Jedi High Council]] made key decisions for members of the Jedi Order to follow and operated based on consensus among its twelve members. The council included respected [[Grand Master]]s, with one, [[Ry Ki-Sakka]], believing that after a member [[Death|died]] and [[Oneness|passed into]] [[Cosmic Force|the Force]], the council did not replace their seat but instead expanded by inviting new Jedi.<ref name="Temptation">''[[The High Republic: Temptation of the Force]]''</ref>

In the years leading up to the [[Separatist Crisis]], the Senate's [[Core Faction]] advocated for increasing the centralization of power, including funding expansion programs to outlying star systems and organizing them in the model of the [[Core Worlds]]. They were opposed by the [[Rim Faction]], which favored greater rights for individual star systems in the Outer Rim, such as [[Ryloth]]. The Core Faction was often supported by the [[Militarists]], who wanted a centralized Republic military to counter criminal gangs instead of relying on a patchwork of [[planetary defense forces]] and the [[Judicial Department]], which included the [[Sector Ranger]]s who were supported by Jedi.<ref name="Rise">''[[Rise of the Separatists]]''</ref>

The Republic's increasing investment in warfare and executive powers of the Supreme Chancellor were exploited by [[Darth Sidious|Sheev Palpatine]] to create a [[Grand Army of the Republic]] during the [[Clone Wars]] and become [[Galactic Emperor]]; the increasingly corrupt Senate supported the increasingly oppressive government of the Republic, which rebranded itself as the [[Galactic Empire]].<ref name="TSWB" /> The [[Chancellor (New Republic)|Chancellor]] of the [[New Republic]] that overthrew Palpatine's dictatorship was therefore established as a position with limited authority by [[Mon Mothma]], who refused to let the office maintain the emergency powers granted in the old [[charter of Chancellor]].<ref name="Aftermath">[[Aftermath (novel)|''Aftermath'']]</ref> The new democratic state therefore lacking an executive despite an attempt by [[Centrists|Centrist faction]] senators to create a new position named [[First Senator]] that would have executive power over economic and military affairs. They were opposed by the [[Populists]], who favored the sovereignty of individual member states and were led by [[Leia Skywalker Organa Solo|Leia Organa]]. Organa believed that the fledgling democracy was left vulnerable to various Imperial remnants due to its own demilitarization, and she left the New Republic Senate to form the [[Resistance]], an armed movement that vowed to protect the freedom of people throghout the galaxy.<ref name="Bloodline">[[Bloodline (novel)|''Bloodline'']]</ref>

===Economy===
{{Quote|It is my experience that senators focus only on pleasing those who fund their campaigns&hellip;and they are by no means scared of forgetting the niceties of democracies in order to get those funds.|Obi-Wan Kenobi|<ref name="AOTC" />}}
Wealth was created with the exchange of material and services, and exchange values were variable depending on the satisfaction of the trading parties. Treaties could be legally binding under the Republic's democratic system, while haggling was a common practice in parts of the [[Outer Rim Territories]] that were beyond the Republic's jurisdiction, such as the planet [[Tatooine]]. Enterprising businesspeople capitalized on the mass organization of supply shipments and the labor of people to make use of material resources, such as the [[tibanna]] gas industry on the world of [[Bespin]], with the extracted and processed tibanna being valued for fuel. The Senate's laws governed how business was conducted, and ordinary people in the galaxy sold their labor for wages with which they could purchase their own personal property.<ref name="CL2016" />
 
The Republic's democracy moderated the galactic economy through a mixture of taxation, regulation and exemptions for the business of private companies, and public services.<ref name="TPM" /> While the [[Republic Treasury]] was responsible for printing Republic [[Galactic Credit Standard|credits]], the Senate's [[Finance Committee]] oversaw annual budgets, revenue generation, government grants, and emergency relief fund appropriations.<ref name="Rise" /> The Senate and the government jointly determined the state budget, with the state funding basic social services, education, healthcare, and infrastructure.<ref name="Pursuit TCW" /> Interplanetary cooperation and trade created the prosperity of the [[High Republic Era]], though that age of prosperity and hope was disrupted by destructive violence caused by [[Nihil]] raiders.<ref name="TSWB" />

Having long abandoned the [[aurodium standard]], the Republic partnered with the [[InterGalactic Banking Clan]] (IGBC) and [[Bank of the Core]] to maintain its credit reserves and ensure the stability of the Republic's [[currency]],<ref name="Rise" /> which was recognized throughout the Republic for having a fixed value but not necessarily beyond its boundaries in the Outer Rim.<ref name="TPM" /> The Republic also had a [[Republic Tax Collection Agency|tax collection agency]],<ref name="Rise" /> cut other areas of public spending,<ref name="Pursuit TCW" /> and issued [[war bond]]s to fund militarization during the Clone Wars.<ref name="Propaganda">''[[Star Wars Propaganda: A History of Persuasive Art in the Galaxy]]''</ref> As one of the most powerful corporations in the Outer Rim,<ref name="GA">''[[Star Wars: Galactic Atlas]]''</ref> the IGBC issued loans to the Republic at interest rates of their own discretion.<ref name="Crisis at the Heart">{{TCW|Crisis at the Heart}}</ref>

Economics was the study of resource allocation, people, and their choices. While some economic policies promoted rapid, short-term growth at the expense of beings, other policies allowed for incremental and constructive long-term economic growth. Although some people believed that economics had no morality and that free markets would correct themselves, some economists resolved moral dilemmas through the guidance of the Force. Listening to the Force could uncover deeper truths even when economics was popularly seen as patterns of doom and gloom; supply and demand. Some Jedi adopted monastic lives of austerity,<ref name="Unlimited">''[[Unlimited Power]]''</ref> and the only personal possession allowed within the Jedi Order's membership was the [[lightsaber]].<ref name="TSWB" />

[[File:SanHill-SWM43.png|thumb|left|320px|Corporate interests corrupted the Republic's democracy and profited from war.]]
In the latter years of the Republic, peace fostered comfort and comfort in turn begot complacency, encouraging corruption. Several commercial corporation giants&mdash;such as the IGBC, the [[Trade Federation]], the [[Commerce Guild]], the [[Corporate Alliance]],<ref name="TSWB" /> and the Techno Union,<ref name="Rise" /> all of which stood as examples of unbridled [[capitalism]]<ref name="Prelude to the Clone Wars">{{EncyclopediaCite|Prologue to the Clone Wars and the First Battle of Geonosis}}</ref>&mdash;gained their own seats in the Senate. They exploited disagreements among politicians to stall solutions and loopholes in the bureaucracy in pursuit of profit at the expense of representative democracy, corrupting more senators on their payroll.<ref name="TSWB" />

Although the corporations were in turn restricted by the levying of taxes, they decided to lead the [[Confederacy of Independent Systems]], a secessionist faction motivated by the frustration of many senators of Outer Rim star systems with what they perceived as an ineffectual Republic that favored the Core Worlds elite while being burdened with heavy taxation. The former [[Jedi Master]] [[Count of Serenno|Count]] [[Dooku]] of [[Serenno]] had a reputation of being a political idealist frustrated with the Republic's inefficiency, and he promised freedom and prosperity as the leader of the Separatist Alliance. While the [[Separatist Senate|Separatist Parliament]] was a representative democratic legislature akin to the Galactic Senate, it did not have the Republic's layers of bureaucracy, existing treaties, and interference from corporations.<ref name="TSWB" />

However, the Confederacy was a democracy only in name.<ref name="CIS history 8">{{Databank|url=confederacy-of-independent-systems-history-gallery|title=Confederacy of Independent Systems History Gallery}} {{C|Slide 8}}</ref> The Parliament was largely sidelined by Dooku's secret [[Executive Separatist Council]], which consisted mainly of corporations such as the Trade Federation, Commerce Guild, Corporate Alliance, InterGalactic Banking Clan, [[Techno Union]], and the [[Retail Caucus]] that funded the war against the Republic.<ref name="TSWB" /> When the Confederacy was defeated, the trade corporations were absorbed by the Galactic Empire dictatorship<ref name="CIS DB">{{Databank|confederacy-of-independent-systems|Confederacy of Independent Systems}}</ref> or simply pledged themselves to the new government.<ref name="MoF">''[[Reign of the Empire: The Mask of Fear]]''</ref>

===Justice, rights, and the rule of law===
{{Quote|It's every citizen's duty to challenge their leaders, to keep them honest, and hold them accountable if they're not.|Ahsoka Tano|<ref name="Academy">{{TCW|The Academy}}</ref>}}
[[File:LightAndLife-2021THR1.jpg|thumb|right|300px|The Jedi safeguarded the Republic's democracy as guardians of peace and justice.]]
The Galactic Republic valued the rule of law,<ref name="Timelines" /> as did planetary representative democracies such as [[Devaron]].<ref name="Nexus">''[[Nexus of Power]]''</ref> The Alliance to Restore the Republic likewise emphasized its belief that galactic governance required just laws that were fairly implemented by the "consent of the governed."<ref name="Propaganda" /> The Rebellion<ref name="TFAn">[[Star Wars: The Force Awakens (novelization)|''Star Wars: The Force Awakens'' novelization]]</ref> and the New Republic<ref name="Alphabet">[[Alphabet Squadron (novel)|''Alphabet Squadron'']]</ref> were sometimes denounced as forces of "anarchy," and the First Order purported to reclaim "stability" and "progress" that were granted by Imperial "civilization" and "rule of law,"<ref name="TFAn" /> yet without democracy, the Imperial and First Order regimes ruled by decree. They arbitrarily made oppressive laws and committed [[Terrorist|acts of terror]] that could not be challenged in peace,<ref name="TSWB" /> [[Imperial Military|militarily]] quashing disagreement rather than protecting their citizens.<ref name="Rix Road">{{Andor|Rix Road (episode)}}</ref> [[Soldier]]s' obedience to superior orders ensured the birth of the Empire from a corrupted Republic; whereas the plague of corruption and ineffectual governance undermined the state's legal systems, the subsequent dictatorships were ultimately toppled by popular defiance.<ref name="TSWB" />

Republic laws were enforced by various [[Republic courts|courts]] and agencies of the Judicial Department,<ref name="Rise" /> with the Old Republic [[Judicial Forces]] having worked alongside Jedi to dismantle the [[Zygerrian Slave Empire|Zygerrian slave trade]]<ref name="Falcon 91">{{BuildFalconCite|91|Guide to the Galaxy}}</ref> as slavery was outlawed in the Republic by the [[Galactic Constitution]].<ref name="Solo">''[[Solo: A Star Wars Story: Tales from Vandor]]''</ref> However, many outlying [[star system]]s that were not part of the Republic continued to experience slavery, including the planet Tatooine in [[Hutt Space]].<ref name="TPM" /> The Republic valued the right to a fair trial, holding due process even for war crimes.<ref name="Trials">''[[The High Republic: Trials of the Jedi]]''</ref> The legal concept of a war crime still existed in the Empire, though Imperial forces committed atrocities throughout the galaxy with minimal legal consequence.<ref name="Kempshall" />

In the Republic's twilight years, its courts were overwhelmed<ref name="TSWB" /> and the Jedi's ability to predict the future diminished, encapsulated by their involvement as military leaders rather than their traditional role as peacekeepers in the Clone Wars.<ref name="AOTC" /> The slave trade on [[Zygerria]] revived under Separatist influence during the war.<ref name="Slaves of the Republic">{{TCW|Slaves of the Republic}}</ref> The Republic Senate's [[Ethics Committee]] had the power to investigate senators and government officials, including to order witnesses for court hearings, and the [[Judiciary Committee]]'s [[Internal Activities Committee]] could recommend criminal charges on relevant individuals.<ref name="Rise" /> Under the undemocratic Imperial Senate, unjust laws were passed at the directive of the government. Imperial court trials were a sham, being staffed by dismissive judges, violent [[bailiff]]s, lacking a jury, and processing cases that were brought forward by regime officials and military personnel against innocent individuals.<ref name="Announcement">{{Andor|Announcement}}</ref>

==History==
===Knights of the Old Republic===
{{Quote|The Republic victory was so definitive that it reinvented itself, resettling its capital on Coruscant after a devastating ouster. History reset its chrono on this date, and from that moment, the modern Galactic Republic was born.|The artist [[Janyor]] of Bith|<ref name="Propaganda" />}}
[[File:Unityarc.png|thumb|left|320px|The Republic was a democratic union of worlds throughout the galaxy.]]
The "Old Republic" was initially founded by [[Core Founder|twenty-two]] Core Worlds<ref name="Storm">''[[The High Republic: The Rising Storm]]''</ref> around [[25,000 BBY]].<ref name="Old Republic date">''[[The Star Wars Book]]'' dates the founding of the [[Galactic Republic]] to around 25,000 [[Standard year|years]] before the events of {{Film|IV}}, which include the [[Battle of Yavin]], corresponding to c. [[25,000 BBY]].</ref> As time progressed, members of the Republic included a growing quantity of sovereign signatories of star systems and worlds that sent their representatives to the Galactic Senate,<ref name="TSWB" /> which met in the [[Imperial Senate Building|Senate Building]] in the Federal District of Coruscant, the Core World [[ecumenopolis]] that Serving as the political capital of the Republic. The congress of the Republic elected Chancellors who led the Republic government and also chaired Senate meetings.<ref name="CL2016" /> Given differences in [[orbit]]al cycles and [[species]] lifespans, members of the Republic defined their own electoral procedures and term limits for their own senators.<ref name="Kempshall" />

Coruscant attracted migrants from thousands of other worlds, including from both Republic member worlds and foreign allies. Although [[Troithe]] in the [[Deep Core]] rivalled Coruscant as the cosmopolitan jewel of the Republic with its aristo-mercantile families, Coruscant's growing workforce bolstered its industrial capacity and outpaced production on Troithe.<ref name="Shadow Fall">''[[Shadow Fall: An Alphabet Squadron Novel]]''</ref> [[The Works]] of Coruscant thrived and were later vacated as the costs of manufacturing in the ecumenopolis rose, though its population remained in the trillions.<ref name="CL2016" /> The [[Expansion Region]]'s [[Thisspias]] had a constitutional monarchy that was culturally led by the hereditary [[Blood Monarchy]] but otherwise governed by a [[Thisspiasian parliament|parliament]]. The [[Blood Monarch]] had successfully convinced the world's fighters to serve in the military of the Republic during its initial establishment, securing her reign and expanding Thisspias' economy while her people defended galactic democracy despite their generally conservative and pro-independence stance. Thisspias was involved in many Republic conflicts during the [[Great Manifest Period]] and had great influence in the Galactic Senate, connecting the Core Worlds and the Outer Rim.<ref name="Unlimited" />

The Jedi Order emerged as the Republic's protectors, but the [[Force-sensitive|Force users]] suffered a schism<ref name="Eras">{{SW|url=eras|text=Star Wars Eras}}</ref> during the [[Hundred-Year Darkness]]<ref name="SW 2015 9">[[Star Wars (2015) 9|''Star Wars'' (2015) 9]]</ref> around [[5000 BBY]].<ref name="Timelines" /> The fallen Jedi turned against their compatriots and became tyrants known as [[Dark Lord of the Sith|Dark Lords of the Sith]], having been corrupted by the [[dark side of the Force]],<ref name="TSWB" /> which fueled desires to control others through the manipulation of fears and restrictive laws of punishment rather than the Jedi way of serving political communities with compassion.<ref name="Unlimited" /> The [[Sith Wars]] plagued the Old Republic.<ref name="TSWB" /> According to the [[Qel-Droma Epics]], the Dark Lord [[Naga Sadow]]'s [[Great Hyperspace War]] was foiled by [[Empress]] [[Teta]] working together with the Jedi, but later the [[Krath]] took control of the [[Empress Teta system]] in the Deep Core and were led by fallen Jedi who commanded [[Mandalorian crusaders]].<ref name="Nexus" /> The Sith were also led by a [[Unidentified Sith Emperor|Sith Emperor]]<ref name="Coruscant">{{EncyclopediaCite|Coruscant}}</ref> and devastated entire worlds, [[Liberation of Coruscant|including Coruscant]]. The Sith Order was ostensibly eliminated by [[Jedi Knight]]s at the [[defeat of the Sith|end]] of the [[Jedi-Sith War]]<ref name="Propaganda" /> on [[Ruusan]],<ref name="HC29">{{HelmetCollectionCite|29|Databank A-Z}}</ref> and the Galactic Republic was democratically founded around 1032 BBY following the Sith Empire's collapse.<ref name="TSWB" /> However, the sole Sith survivor, [[Darth Bane]], conjured a [[Grand Plan]] whereby the Sith would corrupt the Republic from within and take revenge against the Jedi.<ref name="HC14">{{HelmetCollectionCite|14|Weapons & Uniforms}}</ref>

[[Tarsus Valorum]] served as the first Supreme Chancellor of the modern Republic state, which disbanded its military<ref name="Tarkin" /> in favor of the Jedi Knights, who were no longer burdened by an existential threat such as the Sith.<ref name="TSWB" /> The Republic tried to suppress the history of the Sith's oppression and their obsession with genetic sciences and [[immortality]].<ref name="TROS VD">''[[Star Wars: The Rise of Skywalker: The Visual Dictionary]]''</ref> The Galactic Constitution<ref name="Propaganda" /> was influenced by the quartet of controversial [[philosopher]]s known as the [[Four Sages of Dwartii]].<ref name="CL2016" /> While the constitution included a declaration that all [[Sentience|sentient]] beings were equal and outlawed slavery, [[droid rights]] remained a contentious topic.<ref name="Solo">''[[Solo: A Star Wars Story: Tales from Vandor]]''</ref>

===Expansion of the High Republic===
{{Quote|Perhaps it's just the cost of peace. That it's hard won. That it hurts because it's worthwhile. Worth fighting for. Precious.''"<br />"''We need to be better, Aida. All of us. We need to recognize what we have and help the galaxy cling on to it.''"<br />"''We will. The Jedi Order, the Republic&mdash;we'll find a way. One day, we'll be the beacon of peace that the galaxy needs. But we're not there yet. In the meantime, we have to do all we can and hope it's enough.|Jedi [[Aida Forte]] and [[Creighton Sun]]|audio=CreightonAidaCostOfPeace-TBoJ.flac|<ref name="Battle of Jedha">''[[The High Republic: The Battle of Jedha]]''</ref>}}
The Republic was the dominant galactic-scale state that included sovereign signatories of star systems across the Core Worlds, [[Colonies]], [[Inner Rim Territories]], Expansion Region, and [[Mid Rim Territories]]. Republic explorers extended [[hyperspace route]]s across the Outer Rim Territories, leaving the largely uncharted [[Unknown Regions]] and the unpredictable [[Wild Space]] out of the authorities' reach. Cooperation between much of the galaxy's star systems ensured that the Republic prospered in the golden age of the High Republic<ref name="TSWB" /> between around [[500 BBY]] and [[100 BBY]].<ref name="Timelines">''[[Star Wars: Timelines]]''</ref>

[[File:OrlenMolloKyongGreylark-TheChancellors.png|thumb|right|330px|The Republic executive's powers were limited and depended on collaboration.]]
In [[382 BBY]],<ref name="THRCE" /> the Republic was led by the Co-Chancellors [[Kyong Greylark]] and [[Orlen Mollo]], who sought to develop a more equitable and connected union<ref name="Co-Chancellors">{{SWYouTube|series=Characters of Star Wars: The High Republic|text=The Chancellors|video=w54C6NHFasc|sw_url=video/the-chancellors-characters-of-the-high-republic}}</ref> during the [[Great Hyperspace Rush]]. [[Republic Pathfinder]]s and Jedi led expeditions further into the Outer Rim. Mollo was a [[Quarren]] who had experience of conflict on [[Mon Cala]], the shared homeworld of the Quarren and [[Mon Calamari]] peoples, and effectively worked with Greylark until the latter's resignation following her [[Axel Greylark|son's]] arrest.<ref name="Convergence">''[[The High Republic: Convergence]]''</ref> While Co-Chancellor Greylark specialized in the Senate on Coruscant, Co-Chancellor Mollo traveled extensively and, while he believed the Republic should not exert undue influence on non-member worlds, he and Jedi negotiators pleaded for a ceasefire to the [[Eiram and E'ronoh War]].<ref name="THRCE" /> Around that [[time]], the Republic [[Republic-Togruta conflict|clashed]] with the sovereign and independent [[Togruta]] people of<ref name="Storm" /> the Expansion Region planet [[Shili]].<ref name="TFA BG">[[Star Wars: The Force Awakens Beginner Game|''Star Wars: The Force Awakens'' Beginner Game]]</ref>

Jedi Masters and found-siblings [[Porter Engle]] and [[Barash Silvain]] meanwhile negotiated between the [[Bethune army]] and the [[Siege of Firevale|besieged]] [[Firevale]] on [[Gansevor]], allowing humanitarian supplies to enter Firevale and establishing a ceasefire.<ref name="Blade 2">[[The High Republic — The Blade 2|''The High Republic — The Blade'' 2]]</ref> Silvain was quick to believe the Princess [[Sicatra]] of [[Bethune]] that the [[Bethunian]]s wanted to kill her due to her pregnancy with Prince [[Colden]] of Firevale, given that Silvain herself had strong childhood longings for being part of a family, and entrusted Engle with demanding the army's departure. However, the army had allied with [[General]] [[Abediah Viess]]' [[Viess's army|mercenary army]] that insisted on targeting Firevale's city center, forcing Engle to battle Viess.<ref name="Blade 3">[[The High Republic — The Blade 3|''The High Republic — The Blade'' 3]]</ref> Princess Sicatra then revealed that the siege had been a ruse intended to usurp the [[Firevale's royal family|Firevale throne]] and ordered the Bethunians to turn against Viess' army. Although the mercenaries retreated, Silvain felt responsible for her misjudgement and the resulting bloodshed, [[Barash Vow|vowing]] to exile herself to reconnect with the Force.<ref name="Blade 4">[[The High Republic — The Blade 4|''The High Republic — The Blade'' 4]]</ref>

===The Nihil crisis===
===="We are all the Republic"====
{{Quote|Some have traveled far, some are close to home. But we all are one. We are all the Republic, and this fair is for all of us. ''This day'' is for all of us. Together we will experience new things. Together we will witness marvels from across the sectors. Art. Music. Drama. Innovation. They belong to us and they bring us together. This is a chance to understand one another; to realize once again what we all bring to the Republic, each and every one. This is our time. And it is only the beginning.|Chancellor Lina Soh|<ref name="Storm" />}}
At some point on the Outer Rim planet Ryloth, the [[Clan Assembly]] signed the [[articles of membership]] for the [[Twi'lek]] homeworld and their colony worlds to join the Republic. However, one of the colonies, [[Aaloth]], was struck by famines that spurred a [[Insurrection on Aaloth|coup d'état]]&mdash;organized by the [[Zygerrian Alliance]], it executed members of Aaloth's [[Lourna Dee's family|ruling family]] and forced its sole survivor, [[Lourna Dee]], into an [[Unidentified Zygerrian camp|enslavement camp]] on Zygerria until it was [[Attack on the Zygerrian camp|liberated]] by the Jedi<ref name="Tempest">''[[The High Republic: Tempest Runner]]''</ref> around [[255 BBY]].<ref name="Timelines" />

[[File:SohStarlightSpeech-YJA-PrincePirate.png|thumb|left|350px|Chancellor Lina Soh was committed to the Republic's ideals of unity and compassion.]]
Following her [[Supreme Chancellor election, 234 BBY|election]] as Chancellor around [[234 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> [[Lina Soh]] launched the [[Great Works]] projects to strengthen the Republic's [[outreach program]]s and expand the democratic union's influence in the Outer Rim. Among the projects were the negotiation of a treaty between the Quarren and Mon Calamari,<ref name="LOTJ">''[[The High Republic: Light of the Jedi]]''</ref> the [[Grand Renewal|renewal]] of [[Kwenn]]'s [[Gem Cities of Kwenn|Gem Cities]],<ref name="Living Force">''[[The Living Force]]''</ref> and technological innovations such as developing [[bacta]] cultivation, enhancing a network of [[communications relay]]s, and building [[Starlight Beacon]] as both a symbol of the Republic and an effort to boost interconnectivity in the [[Galactic Frontier]]. Chancellor Soh used the phrase "we are all the Republic" to emphasise the value of its democracy,<ref name="LOTJ" /> and Jedi such as [[Avar Kriss]] used the phrase "for light and life" to highlight the Jedi's commitment to all beings in the galaxy.<ref name="THR 1">[[The High Republic (2021) 1|''The High Republic'' (2021) 1]]</ref>

When the anarchistic Nihil marauders instigated the [[Great Hyperspace Disaster]] and subsequent [[Emergences]] in [[232 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> the Chancellor halted [[hyperspace]] travel in the Outer Rim via the [[Republic Transport Bureau]] under [[Secretary of Transportation]] [[Jeffo Lorillia]] to ensure spacefarers' safety. Senator [[Izzet Noor]] of Serenno spoke in protest of the order on behalf of the majority of the Outer Rim, though [[Jeni Wataro|Noor's aide]] was a covert Nihil agent.<ref name="THRCE" /> Chancellor Soh also asked the Jedi Order to work with the Republic Defense Coalition against the Nihil. The Jedi High Council debated and voted on the issue, with six members in support and five opposing; [[Ephru Shinn]] argued against Jedi military involvement on the principle of peace while [[Yarael Poof]] countered that Jedi must also intervene to maintain justice.<ref name="LOTJ" />

Senator [[Tia Toon]] of the [[Sullust system]] was not satisfied with the Republic's reliance on the Jedi and was concerned about the Republic Defense Coalition's limitations, and he had been proposing a [[Defense Force Program]] that would boost the Republic's military capabilities. While Toon did not consider the economic dominance of the [[SoroSuub Corporation]] in his star system to be a conflict of interest, his costly proposals had failed multiple rounds of voting in the Senate and were not welcomed by Chancellor Soh's pacifist ideals.<ref name="Storm" /> Around the same time, Republic authorities dissolved the [[Byne Guild]]<ref name="Timelines" /> after learning about the shipping company's abuse of employees working in [[indentured servitude]], a legal practice upon which the [[Hutt Clan]] capitalized to build industries.<ref name="Into">''[[The High Republic: Into the Dark]]''</ref>

[[File:TheRisingStorm-OOP-FullArt.jpg|thumb|right|320px|The Republic Fair on Valo was intended to be a demonstration of democracy and unity but was hijacked by the Nihil.]]
In [[231 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> the Chancellor hosted the [[Valo Republic Fair]] to celebrate the spirit of democracy, progress, and unity. The [[journalist]] [[Rhil Dairo]] was granted broad clearance to much of the Chancellor's work coordinating the event, as Lina Soh believed in the importance of free and transparent press coverage. The [[Republic Fair]] itself was hijacked by a vicious Nihil [[Valo Atrocity|attack]], but the Jedi and Republic forces protected many of the dignitaries and citizens of [[Valo]], including the Chancellor herself. As Soh recovered from her injuries, [[Vice Chair|Vice Chancellor]] [[Larep Reza]] served as acting chancellor. The Jedi's bravery convinced the fair's guest of honor, [[Regasa]] [[Elarec Yovet]] of the Togruta people, whose [[Togruta government|government]] had misgivings about the Republic, to argue for an alliance between the two states.<ref name="Storm" />

====Against surging storms====
{{Quote|We are all the Republic. What does that really mean? Are they just words? Is it merely a thoughtless motto we all toss around without considering its true intent? Perhaps to some, that's all the words have ever meant. To our enemies, certainly, they are a point of ridicule. But this is because they do not understand the strength that might be found in ''unity''. While we of the Republic share a common purpose, we share, too, common values. Compassion for our fellow beings. A desire to raise one another up, to help where help is needed, to grow. And to do it ''together''.|Chancellor Lina Soh, addressing the Senate|<ref name="Eye">''[[The High Republic: The Eye of Darkness]]''</ref>}}
In [[230 BBY]], the Nihil [[Infiltration on Corellia|infiltrated]] the government of Corellia, a Core World with significant isolationist sentiments along the taglines of "''[[Corellia for Corellians]]''." With help from the Jedi, [[Alys Ongwa]] and her [[Supreme Coronet City Diplomat Protection|diplomat security crew]] [[Battle of the Corellian Shipyards|rallied]] fellow [[Corellian]]s against the brutality of the Nihil regime. Despite failing to secure additional starships from the [[Santhe Shipyards|Corellian shipyards]],<ref name="Horizon">''[[The High Republic: Midnight Horizon]]''</ref> Nihil militants [[Destruction of Starlight Beacon|destroyed]] Starlight Beacon with the help of their "[[Shrikarai|Nameless]]" who which preyed on the Jedi's connection to the Force.<ref name="THRCE" />

[[File:Starlightbeaconexplodes.jpg|thumb|left|280px|The fall of the Starlight Beacon]]
The Nihil also forcibly claimed an area of the frontier as their own [[Occlusion Zone]], and the Jedi High Council informed the Senate that they were implementing the [[Guardian Protocols]], a series of policies limiting the Jedi Order's activities alongside the council's order for all Jedi to re-gather on Coruscant to evaluate the Nihil threat. The Chancellor vowed not to abandon the trapped worlds, and some Jedi were sent along with Republic forces to patrol the Occlusion Zone's seemingly impenetrable [[Stormwall]] border and attempt to break through it.<ref name="THRCE" /> In the [[229 BBY|following year]], Lina Soh faced an increasing number of senators who began to push for an accord with the Nihil to recognize their legitimacy and reopen trade routes. She nevertheless commanded the Senate's approval, emphasizing compassion and collaboration as core values of their democracy, including those beyond the state's borders who carried the Republic "in their [[heart]]s."<ref name="Eye" />

After the [[228 BBY|anniversary]] of the fall of Starlight, in lieu of a way to enter the Occlusion Zone, Jedi Master [[Elzar Mann]] petitioned Grand Master Ry Ki-Sakka about mounting an [[assault on the Stormwall]]. However, the Grand Master was concerned about prioritizing the Nihil conflict over the rest of the galaxy; most citizens' lives continued as normal and required governance and guardianship with everyday issues such as disputes, trade negotiations, and migrations. The Nihil [[Minister of Information (Nihil)|Minister of Information]] and former Republic senator [[Ghirra Starros]] sought to establish diplomatic relations with the Republic, and she was received as a guest by Chancellor Soh and Master Elzar Mann, but they agreed that the Senate and the Jedi would be divided on the issue of recognizing the Nihil as legitimate and refused to show that the Republic would capitulate to the Nihil.<ref name="Eye" />

[[File:JediSenate-THRSOS1.png|thumb|right|380px|The Jedi Order worked closely with the Senate of the Republic in response to crises such as the Nihil conflict.]]
The Jedi Council initially refused to work with Republic forces to attempt breaching the Stormwall. When Master Mann relayed a Republic report believing that a breach had been identified, the council agreed to help Republic forces in their assault. When it failed, the Nihil [[Expansion of the Stormwall|expanded]] the reach of the Stormwall, claiming more sectors of Republic Outer Rim space, up to the [[Seswenna sector]].<ref name="Eye" /> Although many citizens of Coruscant conducted their businesses as usual and were ignorant of the crisis in the frontier, including the consequent influx of [[refugee]]s, the Chancellor and the Jedi High Council refused to abandon the outlying systems and instead worked together closer than ever, with Master Mann serving as liaison, representing the Jedi Council.<ref name="Temptation" />

====Choosing and living====
{{Quote|I bring a message of hope, though it is difficult to hear. But listen, please. Even I am familiar with despair. I know it. That feeling when everything is too much, when the pressure of looking for anything inside of tomorrow is overwhelming. It hurts. I know it, too. In times like that just remember who you are&mdash;who you want to be. Who you can be. Focus on that. Fight with me, if that is who you can be. But if you cannot, you do not have to fight. I won't ask that of you. There are people already fighting for you. For all of us. All I am asking of you today is that you live. Live today, and try to find hope tomorrow. Help is coming.|Avar Kriss' promise to people trapped in Nihil space|<ref name="Temptation" />}}
Given the crisis, Chancellor Soh continued to have the confidence of senior senators from a rainbow of worlds, including Senator Toon.<ref name="Temptation" /> Grand Master [[Yoda]] suggested that the Nihil had overreached and would face uprisings from the billions of additional people trapped within their territory, assuring the Chancellor that the council fully committed to helping the Republic Defense Coalition breach the Stormwall and learn more about the dreadful Nameless horde. Indeed, Jedi Masters Avar Kriss and Porter Engle devised a way to exit and enter the Occlusion Zone,<ref name="Eye" /> with Kriss recording a message of hope broadcasted by resistance organizers in Nihil space.<ref name="Temptation" />

Ghirra Starros reached out to the Chancellor again. While Soh was open to the idea of the Republic coexisting with another nation, she believed the Nihil had proven to be incapable of doing so and refused to recognize the Nihil's sovereignty; the Chancellor cited the "[[Eye of the Nihil]]," [[Marchion Ro]]'s record of violent atrocities and hostile, expansionist agenda that trapped people within the Stormwall's borders against their will. When the Jedi Council invited Elzar Mann to join its ranks, Master Kriss, his lover, encouraged him in the belief that his strong voice would keep the council from stagnating.<ref name="Temptation" />

[[File:AvarElzar-TotF.png|thumb|left|380px|Avar Kriss and Elzar Mann played their parts in upholding the Republic and the Jedi's promise to serve the citizens of the galaxy.]]
Master Kriss believed that love was limitless, as opposed to attachment and possessiveness<ref name="Temptation" />&mdash;which had long been forbidden within the Jedi Order<ref name="THRCE" /> as they dangerously led to the dark side&mdash;and that the Jedi's love for people inspired them to find hope and fight for freedom, even beyond the Republic and at times of despair. Master Mann later discussed love with Grand Master Yoda, asking him if it was possible to be free of attachment while loving another individual, a place, or even the entire Republic. Mann realized that his parents must have let him go to be trained as a Jedi through their love; that despite Chancellor Soh's desperate desire for her [[Kitrep Soh|son]] to safely return from Nihil space, she was unwilling to compromise the billions of lives for which she was responsible to make her demand; the importance of choosing how to live and serve in every moment.<ref name="Temptation" />

====For light and life====
{{Quote|There will be times when it seems the fate of the galaxy hinges on the tiniest decision you make. Great and terrible times, indeed. In these moments, you will have to choose: will you dance with the flow of the Force, or will you become a boulder against the tide of history? You must ''dance'', even in the storm. To do this, you must hold within your body the ''memory'' of having danced in the calm. Only then, can you truly know how to let go, and rise.|Master Obratuk Glii, teaching Farzala Tarabal|<ref name="THRA 2023 14">[[The High Republic Adventures (2023) 14|''The High Republic Adventures'' (2023) 14]]</ref>}}
Within the Occlusion Zone, the Nihil [[Invasion of Eriadu|invaded]] the planet [[Eriadu]] and lay siege on [[Bri-Phrang City]], where Jedi and the Republic Defense Coalition remained. The Nihil wanted to secure the support of Eriadu's [[Tarkin family]] and combine their forces to defeat the Jedi. While the [[Quintad|Eriadu High Council]] debated ahead of their vote on whether or not to support the Nihil, [[Lula Mrala-Talisola|Lula Talisola]] and [[Zeen Mrala-Talisola|Zeen Mrala]]'s ally [[Sevran Tarkin]] exposed her [[Tragkul Tarkin|uncle]], who was inclined to welcome the Nihil, of dishonoring their family in the past.<ref name="THRA 2023 10">[[The High Republic Adventures (2023) 10|''The High Republic Adventures'' (2023) 10]]</ref> As the family continued deliberating among themselves, the Nihil traveled to recruit more fighters from Valo, which they had previously [[Occupation of Valo|occupied]]. The visiting Nihil were not aware that the Jedi had already freed the world, which remained in the Occlusion Zone but was the secret source of a pro-freedom and pro-Republic broadcast by the [[Jedi Initiate]] [[Gavi Takitaken]].<ref name="THRA 2023 11">[[The High Republic Adventures (2023) 11|''The High Republic Adventures'' (2023) 11]]</ref>

[[File:OcclusionZoneFateEriadu-Textless.jpg|thumb|right|320px|The urban warmth of Eriadu was set upon by the Nihil, but the banding together of not only Jedi but ordinary people ultimately proved the Nihil's downfall.]]
The Nihil attempted to re-conquer [[Lonisa City]] with lethal force, with some residents fighting back and others, wary of another war, were either subdued or tried fleeing from the violence. The Jedi Padawan [[Ram Jomaram]], having initially set up Valo's broadcast studio to spread hope and remind himself of freedom in the smallest moments, returned to his home city as Gavi Takitaken was forced to broadcast a plea to surrender. Each member of [[Tanana's crew|Jomaram's crew]] played their part as the Jedi used the broadcast to call on each person to "never shut up" and be a "light in the dark," banding together to transform from "a scattered constellation" to "the dawn," fighting against the occupation until every person, every world, and the entire galaxy was free.<ref name="THRA 2023 12">[[The High Republic Adventures (2023) 12|''The High Republic Adventures'' (2023) 12]]</ref> The people of Lonisa City indeed rose up and deposed the Nihil, who regathered on Eriadu to stomp out the resistance there. While the Nihil ignored their remaining [[Eriaduan]] collaborators' refusal to bombard Bri-Phrang City, a sweeping coalition of Jedi, pirates, and Eriaduans crossed the bay to Eriadu City under the leadership of [[Farzala Tarabal]]. Despite haunting visions of destruction throughout the war, Tarabal recalled his [[Obratuk Glii|Jedi Master's]] teachings to rise up together with the tide of history, fighting for the freedom of Eriaduans as the [[Battle of Eriadu]] began.<ref name="THRA 2023 14" />

While Marchion Ro kept nearly 10% of the Republic's members isolated behind his Stormwall and Nihil spies infiltrated the Republic, Ro could not be satisfied with his quest to have the power to choose for everyone else, and he therefore unleashed the all-consuming [[Blight]], threatening to destroy all life in the galaxy and thus, potentially, kill the Force itself. When he offered to remove the Blight from afflicted Republic worlds in return for public acknowledgment of his help, the Senate signaled that it would support the Supreme Chancellor whether she chose to accept or reject Ro's offer. With the decision left to Lina Soh herself, she asked her ministers to brief her on the state of the crisis and thought that even though she had been elected to the position and she tried to represent all the Republic, even the greatest elected leaders could ultimately only be themselves. When the Jedi discovered the Blight in the depths of their temple, they chose not to immediately alert the Chancellor&mdash;her Jedi liaison, Elzar Mann, reminded her that the Jedi Order was unelected and did not serve the Republic per se but rather helped the Republic, and they would not have helped if the government ordered over a trillion of Coruscant's residents to evacuate their world since doing so would lead to disastrous stampedes. Despite her initial anger at Mann's revelation, Lina Soh considered her desire for strengthening the Republic's unity and, trusting that the Jedi would do their best to stop the Blight, rejected Ro's offer.<ref name="Trials" />

[[File:OcclusionZone-TheAcolyteVG.png|thumb|left|350px|The Republic depended in large part upon the Jedi to dismantle the annexed "Occlusion Zone" (map pictured) and hold back the Blight.]]
With the war focusing on Eriadu while his Nihil were denied legitimacy as a separate state, Ro accelerated the spread of the Blight by killing more of the so-called Nameless on their [[Sophros|homeworld]], in doing so abandoning the Nihil and collapsing the Stormwall. He was ultimately foiled by the team of the [[Luminous Nine]], sent by the Jedi Council, who discovered but promised to keep secret the truth of the "Nameless" people&mdash;the Shrikarai&mdash;with Avar Kriss and Elzar Mann remaining on the world to restore balance on their world, each loving but not needing the other, both dedicated to defending light and life. With the loss of the Stormwall and a catastrophic defeat on Eriadu which took the life of General Viess, Ghirra Starros used the absence of the Eye and her fellow ministers to assume leadership of the so-called Nihil Empire, contacting the Chancellor with terms for conditional surrender which called for an immediate ceasefire and surrender of the Occlusion Zone. In return for these concessions, Starros requested that she be exempted from persecution and named a liaison to the Galactic Senate as worlds once under Nihil rule were reintegrated back into their government. In the war crime trials to follow, the Republic afforded the Nihil suspects due process to show the Republic's regard for the rule of law, and Marchion Ro's trial was held in the Senate Chamber and presided by a magistrate. Chancellor Soh resolved to rebuild faith in the story of the Galactic Republic, telling citizens that their republic was built on shared beliefs and that people had more in common despite disagreements on the details. However, given how close the galaxy had come to its doom, she also realized that the Republic faced a new reality in which it would be irresponsible to depend on having faith in the goodwill and effectiveness of the Jedi.<ref name="Trials" />

===Fading of the golden age===
====Corporate stranglehold====
The [[Republic Ministry of Economic Development]] and the [[Homestead Act]] incentivized migration from the Core Worlds into the galactic frontier and the Outer Rim, aiming to encourage such worlds to join the Republic at various times in its history.<ref name="Kempshall" /> Economic development in outlying systems was encouraged by the Republic's [[Free Trade Zone]]s. Companies that faced threats to shipping and commerce in those peripheral regions founded the Trade Federation<ref name="Insider 200">{{InsiderCite|200|A Certain Point of View}}</ref> in the wake of the Great Hyperspace Disaster.<ref name="Trials" /> Politically, it empowered the [[Neimoidian]] people, who had felt neglected by the Republic until the corporation's founding,<ref name="Brotherhood">[[Brotherhood (novel)|''Brotherhood'']]</ref> For hundreds of years, the Neimoidians colonized a wealthy network of worlds in the Colonies region known as [[Purse world|Purse Worlds]],<ref name="Aliens">''[[Star Wars: Aliens of the Galaxy]]''</ref> with wealth being funneled to the [[Trade monarch|Trade Monarch]] on [[Neimoidia]].<ref name="BMF 52">{{BuildFalconCite|52|Guide to the Galaxy}}</ref> Neimoidians were born as grubs on their homeworld and competed over enforced food scarcity, with successful adults prizing calculation as a strategy for life and traveling off-world while surviving larvae were enslaved as administrative [[Neimoidian drone|drones]].<ref name="CL2016" /> Among the Purse Worlds were [[Narq]], where the native [[Narquois]] people were treated as serfs,<ref name="Aliens" /> and the opulent [[bridge city]] world of [[Cato Neimoidia]].<ref name="BMF 52" />

The [[Corporate Sector]] had been established at the end of the [[Hydian Way]] by the late High Republic Era thanks to the [[Galactic Corporate Policy League]]'s lobbying of key senators.<ref name="Acolyte VG">''[[Star Wars: The Acolyte: The Visual Guide]]''</ref> Two centuries prior, the same groups that founded the Corporate Sector were corporate conglomerates known as the [[Expansionist Oligarchy]], which had secured the rights to develop worlds in the [[Outer Expansion Zone]]. Laborers were subject to cruel practices akin to slavery as the corporations exploited their worlds' resources, which prompted the Republic government to shut down the Oligarchy.<ref name="Rise" /> Among the Galactic Corporate Policy League's members were the Trade Federation of Cato Neimoidia, the [[Tagge Corporation|Tagge Company]] of [[Tepasi]], the [[Consolidated Holdings of Preox-Morlana Corporation]] of the [[Xappyh sector]], [[Kuat]]'s [[Kuat Drive Yards]], the [[Chiewab Amalgamated Pharmaceuticals]] Company with its exploratory fleet, and [[Cybot Galactica]] of [[Affa]]. A treaty negotiated between the Corporate Sector and the Republic stipulated that the sector could not develop on any world with preexisting intelligent life.<ref name="Acolyte VG" /> Subsequent compromises between politicians and corporate interests pulled the Senate away from the common interests of the wider citizenry. Various population and governance criteria existed for a region to join the Republic as a "functional constituency" with its own senators, and corporations such as the Trade Federation gained seats in the Senate.<ref name="Rise" />

[[File:MeditatingInHeaven.jpg|thumb|right|300px|A Padawan's mission in an unfree society uncovered the corporate government's brutality while striving to be consistent to Jedi values and appreciating Aakaash culture and the people's dedication to their communities.]]
In [[200 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> the [[Aakaash system]] was independently ruled by the local [[High Human|human-centric]] [[Land & Sky Corporation]]. It was technologically advanced and heavily industrialized, destroying the planet [[Wind]]'s natural ecosystems and leaving much of the population in economic hardship. The mega-corporation secretly systematically detained members of the native [[Dragon (creature)|dragon]]-like minority population, the [[Loongren]], and harvested their [[Dark yellow blood|blood]] for the apparent effect of granting immortality. Despite the magnate [[Nan Holi]]'s efforts to promote the star system's integration into the Galactic Republic, he was opposed by isolationist forces within the corporation which ruled by draconian laws,<ref name="Vow">''[[The Vow of Silver Dawn]]''</ref> defying the democratic Republic's rule of law.<ref name="Timelines" /> The Jedi [[Mostima]] and [[Sean]] were [[Mission to Aakaash|sent]] to the system to investigate Nan's [[Attack on the Nan family villa|murder]] but were forcibly separated by criminal gangs that worked with the authorities.<ref name="Vow" />

While sheltering with the [[Silver Knights]] in the wastelands of Wind, Sean learned that their leader, [[Li Yu]], had organized among the Knights an elite [[mercenary]] group&mdash;the [[red apricot squad]]&mdash;alongside a community support group for the people in the wastelands, allowing them to live free of the ruling corporation and its thugs at the cost of using cynical and often aggressive tactics against other criminals. Sean later discovered that state-owned companies had been mistreating their own employees, which encouraged corruption, and enslaving the Loongren. To minimize dissent, the mega-corporation brainwashed the rest of the population with [[propaganda]] in the news, politically strengthening the government while insinuating that foreign forces were responsible for internal discord. He vowed to expose the ruling corporation's corruption, which continually deprived citizens of their choices.<ref name="Vow" />

====Institutional decay====
{{Quote|As time went on, Jedi Masters and Knights continued to work hand-in-hand with the Republic Senate on important diplomatic initiatives. The Jedi didn't anticipate it, but their close ties to political power would eventually lead to the fall of their Order.|Luke Skywalker reflects on the late Republic Era|<ref name="SecretsJedi26">''[[Star Wars: The Secrets of the Jedi: The Chronicles of Luke Skywalker]]''</ref>}}
[[File:CrouchingJediHiddenSith-TheAcolyte.png|thumb|left|360px|The hidden Sith strikes at the vulnerabilities of the Jedi Order following a series of impulsive Jedi decisions.]]
In [[148 BBY]],<ref name="Acolyte date">{{TheAcolyteDate}} The [[Mission to Brendok|events]] on [[Brendok]] taking place sixteen years prior therefore occurred in [[148 BBY]].</ref> four Jedi were [[Mission to Brendok|stationed]] on the planet [[Brendok]] to investigate a [[vergence]] in the Force. A series of impulsive decisions made them come into conflict with the [[Brendok coven|witch coven]] there, who sought to protect their secret of creating life, manifest in two bodies: [[Verosha Aniseya|Osha]] and [[Mae-ho Aniseya|Mae]]. Desiring an apprentice of his own, the Jedi [[Sol]] killed [[Mother (title)|Mother]] [[Aniseya]]&mdash;even though she had decided to let Osha go become a Jedi&mdash;and let Mae fall to her apparent death amidst the chaos. The four Jedi took a confused Osha with them and vowed never to tell the truth of the events on Brendok to the Jedi Council, with Sol wishing to protect Osha from knowing the truth too.<ref name="Choice">{{Acolyte|Choice}}</ref>

In [[132 BBY]],<ref name="Acolyte date" /> two of those Jedi were [[assassin]]ated, prompting an investigation by Master [[Vernestra Rwoh]], who convened a meeting with several fellow Jedi. Although they concluded that the assassin was a highly skilled Force user, Rwoh rejected [[Ki-Adi-Mundi]]'s suggestion that they inform the High Council of their findings, stating that the council would be obliged to inform the Senate. She resolved to keep their [[Mission to Khofar|quest]] an internal Jedi affair and sent Master Sol to lead it.<ref name="Day">{{Acolyte|Day (episode)}}</ref> Rwoh also kept in contact with Senator [[Isedwa Chuwant]] to prevent the increasingly popular Senator [[Worus Rayencourt]] and his allies from the Expansion Region to support an external review of the Jedi Order.<ref name="Teach">{{Acolyte|Teach / Corrupt}}</ref> Together with Osha, Master Sol discovered that the assassin was Mae, who had survived the events on Brendok and been trained by a [[The Stranger|Sith]].<ref name="Acolyte">{{Acolyte|The Acolyte (episode)}}</ref>

[[File:VernestraSenateTribunal-TheAcolyte.png|thumb|right|400px|Vernestra Rwoh gave false testimony to Republic senators to cover up for the failings of the Jedi Order.]]
However, Osha was seduced by the dark side as she discovered that Sol had killed her and Mae's mother; Mae wanted Sol to face a trial before the Jedi Council and the Senate, but Osha instead killed Sol and joined the Sith. Believing that the Jedi would kill an individual who used the dark side, Mae asked the Sith to erase her memory and let her be captured by the Jedi to keep them ignorant of the events. Vernestra Rwoh realized that the Sith was a former Jedi pupil of hers and that her fellow Jedi had made a series of grave mistakes; nevertheless, faced with Senator Rayencourt's campaign for increased transparency of the Jedi Order, she lied to a Senate tribunal chaired by<ref name="Acolyte" /> Chancellor [[Veeth Drellik]]<ref name="AcolyteVG">''[[Star Wars: The Acolyte: The Visual Guide]]''</ref> about the events on Brendok. On behalf of the Jedi High Council, Rwoh claimed that the recent Jedi murders had been the sole responsibility of one man who had gone rogue and then taken his own life&mdash;Master Sol&mdash;thereby dismissing Rayencourt's call for a review of the Order.<ref name="Acolyte" />

In the wake of the High Republic Era,<ref name="Timelines" /> the Republic would begin a slow march towards decay as the "old ways" of thinking started to fail<ref name="MoF" /> and corruption took root across the Republic, particularly in the Senate, where trade guilds and corporations were granted representation. The self-interest of many senators also helped to damage the Senate.<ref name="Corruption">{{TCW|Corruption (episode)}}</ref> The Jedi maintained their close ties with the government and its politics,<ref name="SecretsJedi26" /> unknowingly limiting what information and crises they were exposed to.<ref name="Choices">{{TOTJ|Choices}}</ref>

====Dooku confronting corruption====
{{Quote|You serve the Senate.''"<br />"''No. We serve the people of this Republic.|Senator Dagonet and Jedi Master Dooku|<ref name="Justice date" />}}
[[File:DookuStandoff-Justice.png|thumb|left|360px|Jedi Master Dooku was frustrated with the corruption of Senator Dagonet.]]
The Jedi Order had grown to be complacent and comfortable with its elevated stature in the Republic.<ref name="TSWB" /> Jedi Master Dooku, who was born toward the end of the High Republic Era and had been Yoda's apprentice,<ref name="Timelines" /> balked at such corruption.<ref name="TSWB" /> Dooku was a son of the [[Gora|Count of Serenno]], head of the [[Great Houses|noble-led]] [[Great Assembly House|assembly]] of the planet Serenno, named after the [[Serenno (individual)|ancestor]] who led the [[Battle on Serenno|charge]] against the Sith Empire.<ref name="Jedi Lost">''[[Dooku: Jedi Lost]]''</ref> Between [[68 BBY]] and around [[58 BBY]],<ref name="Justice date">{{TOTJ|Justice (episode)}} takes place with [[Qui-Gon Jinn]] as [[Dooku]]'s [[Padawan]]. According to ''[[Star Wars: Timelines]]'', Jinn became Dooku's Padawan in [[68 BBY]] and became a [[Jedi Knight]] around [[58 BBY]], placing "Justice" between those dates.</ref> when Master Dooku and [[Padawan]] [[Qui-Gon Jinn]] [[Mission to rescue Dagonet's son|rescued]] the kidnapped [[Uriel Dagonet|son]] of Senator [[Dagonet]], the Jedi found that the senator had been severely neglecting the people on his [[Wayyl|dilapidated world]]. Dooku's frustration broke out when the senator ordered his [[Dagonet's soldiers|soldiers]] to kill the citizens who had kidnapped his son, and the Jedi Master stopped the soldiers and nearly killed the senator, but he was stopped by Jinn, who had also freed Dagonet's son. The latter promised the people that he would help them before departing with his father.<ref name="Justice">{{TOTJ|Justice (episode)}}</ref>

Years later, Master [[Katri]] of the Jedi Council was killed on [[Raxus Secundus]], and Masters Dooku and [[Mace Windu]] were sent to retrieve her body. However, Dooku deviated from their task to investigate Katri's death and confronted Senator [[Larik]] of Raxus, who revealed that his [[Raxus guard|guards]] were responsible. The guards immediately killed Larik but were defeated by the Jedi. The assailants explained that they had wanted to force Larik to fulfill their wishes in the Senate; they believed that the senator was accumulating personal wealth at the expense of their planet by selling its resources to off-world companies for industrialization, and they also thought that the Jedi were merely enforcers of the Senate. Dooku expressed disagreement with the guards' methods but agreed with their ideology of protecting their world. Windu was subsequently offered a seat on the Jedi Council, and the younger Jedi explained to Dooku that the council had made the decision given that Dooku's actions had led to a senator's death.<ref name="Choices" />

====Corporate colonialism and slavery====
{{Quote|As for my first act, I intend to cancel any and all contracts the government has with Czerka Corporation.''"<br />"''You never did care for them.''"<br />"''Nor did I make any secret of it. Yet the princess never realized we had so much in common. If she would've confided in more of us, she might have realized how much support there was for standing up to the company.|Governor Orth and Qui-Gon Jinn|audio=Pijal15.flac|<ref name="M&A">''[[Master & Apprentice]]''</ref>}}
[[File:PijalCover.jpg|thumb|right|250px|Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi on Pijal]]
Master Dooku had been introduced by his former Padawan [[Rael Averross]] to Senator Sheev Palpatine of [[Naboo]] after Dooku made an address to the Senate<ref name="Jedi Lost" /> in [[42 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> when Dooku left the Jedi Order to reclaim his birth title as [[Count]] of Serenno.<ref name="Jedi Lost" /> Averross served as the [[lord]] [[regent]] of the Inner Rim planet [[Pijal]] until [[Princess]] [[Fanry]]'s coming of age and drafted a [[Governance Treaty|treaty]] alongside the arms manufacturer [[Czerka Arms|Czerka Corporation]] that would transfer power from the [[Pijali monarchy]] to a democratic [[Assembly (Pijal)|Assembly]]. Fanry thought that the treaty's terms would prevent the Assembly from actually representing the population of [[Pijal's moon]] or be able to cancel contracts with Czerka, which would allow the corporation to further exploit the [[Pijali]] people and its enslaved workers. To this end, the crown princess sought to sabotage the treaty proceedings under the passionate belief that her subjects deserved actual freedom from Czerka, enlisting the assistance of the [[Blackguard]]&mdash;a clandestine division of the [[Royal guard (Pijal)|Pijali royal guard]] led by [[Captain]] [[Deren]]&mdash;to stage a [[Blackguard campaign|violent series of attacks]] that Fanry framed for being the work of the [[Opposition]] performance troupe, who had been conducting their own [[Opposition protests|series of peaceful protests]] against the signing of the Governance Treaty initiative. Although she was not involved with the blackguards, [[Minister]] [[Orth]]&mdash;one of Fanry's closest advisors and staunch proponent for Pijal tradition&mdash;expressed contempt for the capabilities of the Galactic Senate, believing that its members were more concerned with "posing for their re-election [[Hologram|holos]]".<ref name="M&A" />

In [[40 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> Qui-Gon Jinn and his Padawan, Obi-Wan Kenobi, were [[Mission to Pijal|sent]] to ratify the Governance Treaty on behalf of the Republic. The Jedi learnt that Czerka was a politically powerful business, secretly using slave labor even in its [[Hosnian Prime factory complex|factory]] on the Republic Core World [[Hosnian Prime]]. At her [[Coronation of Queen Fanry|coronation]], Queen Fanry stabbed Pijal's [[Unidentified Skykeeper|religious leader]] for accepting Czerka bribes and decried democracy before ordering an attack on the [[Meritt Col|Czerka representative's]] [[Leverage|starship]]&mdash;despite the presence of enslaved people aboard. Fanry's attendant and confidante, [[Cady]], was shocked by the new queen's betrayal of her anti-slavery stance and forced the monarch to surrender. A Republic judicial ruling deemed Fanry's crowning illegitimate, and she abdicated her right to Pijal's throne, leaving her cousin [[Lamia]] to reign as [[queen]] to outlaw slavery and establish a democratic Assembly before dissolving the [[monarchy]]. Led by Governor Orth, the new democracy canceled all of Pijal's contracts with Czerka.<ref name="M&A" /> At some point after Dooku's departure from the Jedi Order, Qui-Gon Jinn was sent to rescue the runaway Jedi Padawan [[Lizel Liit]], who was taken by [[Zarah Bliss]] to be auctioned to the highest bidder on [[Desinta]]. Jinn met with [[Prefect]] [[Aminar]], the nominally elected leader of Desinta who was opposed by the [[Dan'Gar]], a political group which also claimed Liit at the auction.<ref name="Resolve">{{StoryCite|story=Resolve (short story)|book=Stories of Jedi and Sith}}</ref>

[[File:JediKnights5-Virgin.jpg|thumb|left|280px|Jedi Knights faced an increasingly corrupt and dishonest galaxy, with even their own members becoming disillusioned and cynical.]]
In 40 BBY, Finis Valorum was elected as the Supreme Chancellor of the Republic.<ref name="JKD">{{JediKnightsDate}}</ref> Valorum tasked the Jedi Order with resolving the conflict between the worlds [[Vekura]] and [[Syrinx Prime]]. The Jedi initially defended Syrinx Prime against Vekura's "invasion" before they could arrange negotiations. Both sides refused to speak with one another and insisted that the politics between them were "complex." The Jedi Council found that both sides were not being truthful and decided to make both parties believe that negotiations would be to each of their best interests. To that end, the Jedi destroyed Vekura's shipyards and Syrinx Prime's weapon forges, and the consequent stalemate forced the two worlds to negotiate a peaceful end to their conflict.<ref name="JK1">[[Jedi Knights 1|''Jedi Knights'' 1]]</ref> At a later point, Jedi Master [[Yaddle]] and Jedi Knight [[Seera Longa]] mediated a dispute between the Trade Federation and the [[Gillanter Corporation]] and realized that neither side were being truthful about their affairs. Their investigation led them to cross paths with the former Jedi Dooku, who had promised to help the Trade Federation and promised to kill Gillanter's [[Lonkus]]. When Dooku's life was threatened, the Jedi abided by their values and saved him, though Dooku himself had abandoned his core values.<ref name="JK5">[[Jedi Knights 5|''Jedi Knights'' 5]]</ref>

Following members of the [[Gran]] species' violent [[Colonization of Malastare|colonization]] of the Mid Rim planet [[Malastare]] at some point in the [[Standard century|centuries]] preceding the Galactic Empire, the Republic's [[Gran Protectorate Act]] notionally partitioned Malastare between Gran colonists on the eastern continent and the native [[Dug]] on the western continent. In effect, the Gran were granted control of the planet and were exclusively represented in the Senate.<ref name="Special Modifications">''[[Special Modifications]]''</ref> Gran senators defended the Republic's status quo as the majority Dug population of Malastare were deprived of their rights by the Gran invaders and practically lived in slavery; [[Ask Aak]], a Gran senator during the Clone Wars, had many Dug servants himself.<ref name="Chanceliers">{{EncyclopediaCite|Chancellors, Senators and States Persons of the Republic}}</ref>

[[File:Malastare_Delagates_TPM.png|thumb|right|350px|Malastare's Gran colonizers were prominent members of the Republic Senate.]]
Ask Aak replaced Senator [[Aks Moe]],<ref name="Chanceliers" /> who represented the [[Congress of Malastare]] in the Galactic Senate's [[Malastare delegation]]. It also included Senator Ainlee Teem of Malastare<ref name="TPM" /> and [[Baskol Yeesrim]] of the [[Gran Protectorate]].<ref name="Geektionary">''[[Star Wars: Geektionary: The Galaxy from A - Z]]''</ref> The injustice on Malastare was a point of embarrassment for the democratic Republic,<ref name="Chanceliers" /> but a [[Republic Navy|Republic fleet]] led by [[Wullf Yularen]] and supported by the Jedi<ref name="XWing94">{{BuildXWingCite|94|Starfighter Aces}}</ref> [[Battle of Malastare Narrows|defeated]] [[Admiral]] [[Trench]]'s Corporate Alliance [[blockade]] of the planet at<ref name="HC 78">{{HelmetCollectionCite|78|Databank A-Z}}</ref> the [[Battle of Malastare Narrows]] in [[34 BBY]].<ref name="Timelines" /> Senator Moe had significant influence among the Republic's politicians, courts, and lobbyists and profited from rampant criminality and disinvestment in Coruscant's lower levels.<ref name="Scum">''[[Star Wars: Scum and Villainy: Case Files on the Galaxy's Most Notorious]]''</ref>

Moe particularly opposed the criminal justice reform proposals of the [[Coruscant Security Force]] [[Inspector]] [[Tanivos Exantor Divo]]. Divo spoke against the complacency produced by a thousand years of peace and changed tact after receiving stiff opposition from men like Moe who were well connected with the Republic bureaucracy, focusing on smaller cases to build up larger ones against Coruscant's crime lords and their associates in politics. The inspector also did not think highly of the Jedi Order that headquartered itself on the crime-ridden capital, but he appreciated the efforts of Master [[Quinlan Vos]] in helping local [[Law enforcement agency|police]].<ref name="Scum" />

====Trade and discontent====
{{Quote|Because something dangerous is brewing in our little corner of the Outer Rim. Discontent is on the rise, as are criminal enterprises and mercenary groups in the employ of self-serving corporations. In the Seswenna sector, several lommite mining concerns are vying for the attention of the Trade Federation, which is determined to forge a monopoly in the free trade zones. Even on my own Naboo, the king finds himself embroiled with the Trade Federation and off world bankers with regard to our plasma exports. Ours are remote worlds, but what transpires in those sectors of the Outer Rim could very well have galactic repercussions.|Senator Palpatine, to Tarkin|<ref name="Tarkin" />}}
[[File:Safe_In_Our_Hands.png|thumb|left|250px|''[[Safe In Our Hands]]'' poster by the Trade Federation]]
While the Republic's expansion bolstered the established Core systems' prosperity via trade, many outlying systems such as Eriadu were forced to take loans from the InterGalactic Banking Clan at high interest rates and thus accumulated debt.<ref name="Tarkin" /> Toward the twilight years of the Republic, many systems that wanted to join it had their applications delayed by senators of the metropole&mdash;particularly those who feared losing political influence in the Senate and wanted their own worlds to profit from the resources of new members without expending resources to the periphery.<ref name="A New Dawn">''[[A New Dawn]]''</ref> [[Coruscanti]]-centric perspectives of the outlying systems typically treated them as a barbaric wilderness. Such beliefs were ingrained by the Outer Rim's high crime rates arising from a lack of Republic protection, driving resentment toward the Republic's elites and institutions among the peoples of the Outer Rim.<ref name="Tarkin" />

While the Neimoidians gradually lost control of the Trade Federation to the [[Kuati]] of Kuat Drive Yards,<ref name="Complete Vehicles">[[Star Wars: Complete Vehicles (2018)|''Star Wars: Complete Vehicles'' (2018)]]</ref> the Federation had consolidated a monopoly over the Free Trade Zones by the final decades of the Republic.<ref name="Tarkin" /> The corporation incentivized colonization, offering to guide [[colonist]]s on their hyperspace routes based on tariffs set by the Federation itself<ref name="Propaganda" /> and its "protection" fees,<ref name="Insider 200" /> In [[65 BBY]], a joint venture between Naboo and [[Damask Holdings]] completed construction of the [[Theed power generator]].<ref name="Nexus" /> Its opening ceremony was attended by the Naboo official Sheev Palpatine, and it extracted [[plasma]] from the depths of Naboo to power the capital city of [[Theed]]. The complex's operations far exceeded Theed's energy needs, and energy was exported off-world in deals arranged via the Trade Federation.<ref name="CL2016" /> Issues of the plasma trade with the Trade Federation and off-world bankers occupied the [[Monarch of Naboo|king of Naboo]].<ref name="Tarkin" /> The Trade Federation also built up its own [[Trade Federation military|armed forces]], with the [[Geonosian]] [[Archduke]] [[Poggle the Lesser]]'s [[Geonosis droid factories|factories]] producing a massive [[Trade Federation Droid Army|droid army]] at the behest of a secretive Lord Sidious.<ref name="USWNE">''[[Ultimate Star Wars, New Edition]]''</ref> Geonosian society was industrious but was prone to warfare when the hives were idle, and the archduke agreed to an offer by [[Baktoid Armor Workshop]] to design those droid factories.<ref name="Catalyst">''[[Catalyst: A Rogue One Novel]]''</ref>

[[File:Palpatine-ROTS.jpg|thumb|right|250px|A seemingly modest Senator Palpatine of Naboo began cultivating influence in the Republic.]]
In lieu of protection from criminal gangs, the Republic member world Eriadu took its own initiative to establish the [[Outland Regions Security Force]], which was funded by off-world loans, bought [[weapon]]ry from arms manufacturers that had been ignoring a Republic ban on selling weapons to member worlds for centuries, and countered [[Greater Seswenna raids|piracy]] in the Seswenna sector under [[Wilhuff Tarkin]]'s leadership.<ref name="Tarkin" /> In [[36 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> Senator Palpatine allied with Tarkin, agreeing with his assessment that the Republic's [[Judicial]]s were being obsoleted in favor of the Jedi Order. Palpatine supported Tarkin's bid to become the [[Governor of Eriadu]], and Tarkin blocked Chancellor Finis Valorum's requests to investigate the [[Eriadu Conference]]; talks between the Republic and the Trade Federation on the Federation's tariffs in the Free Trade Zones had collapsed due to an attack by the radical [[Nebula Front]], which killed the Federation [[Directors of the Trade Federation|directorate]]. With their removal, [[Nute Gunray]] of the [[Neimoidian faction]] was able to take over as the [[Viceroy of the Trade Federation]]<ref name="Tarkin" /> and [[Lott Dod]] became the corporation's senator,<ref name="Insider 200" /> wresting control from the Kuati; the Techno Union subsidiary Kuat Drive Yards firmly aligned itself with the Chancellor of the Republic instead.<ref name="Complete Vehicles" /> Chancellor Valorum later managed to push for a Senate [[Prop 31-814D|proposal]] to tax the Free Trade Zones.<ref name="Insider 200" />
