The Republic relied on volunteer soldiers and Jedi to resolve localized conflicts such as the [[Stark Hyperspace War]],<ref name="Rise" /> of which [[Saesee Tiin]] was a veteran.<ref name="Force Collection">{{ForceCollection|Saesee Tiin|star=4}}</ref> Tiin became a member of the Jedi High Council,<ref name="Living Force" /> and in [[33 BBY]],<ref name="Living Force date">{{LivingForceDate}}</ref> Qui-Gon Jinn challenged the council on their undue focus on large-scale issues while they allowed the closure of [[Jedi outpost]]s such as the [[Kwenn Jedi outpost|one]] scheduled to be shuttered on Kwenn, which had been established through great effort in the High Republic Era. Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi had witnessed the migration of people from Outer Rim worlds affected by the increased presence of criminal groups following the closure of such outposts, and all twelve members of the council agreed to embark on a [[mission to Kwenn]] to help its citizens despite the threat of [[pirate]]s.<ref name="Living Force" />

In Wild Space, the Republic and the Jedi had intervened in the [[Kaleesh-Yam'rii war|war]] between the [[Yam'rii]] and [[Kaleesh]] peoples. The Yam'rii had the benefit of advanced technology, exploiting and enslaving the people of [[Kalee]] until the Kaleesh warlord [[Grievous|Qymaen jai Sheelal]] led his people in a ferocious campaign not only resisting the Yam'rii but also driving them off their homeworld of [[Huk]]. Having been displaced, the Yam'rii successfully sought the help of the Republic,<ref name="The Separatists">{{EncyclopediaCite|The Separatists}}</ref> which censured the Kaleesh and imposed trade embargoes and fines that led to mass starvation.<ref name="English Separatists">{{EncyclopediaCite|The Separatists}} {{C|English version}}</ref> Qymaen jai Sheelal, having taken the name "Grievous," developed a vendetta against the Jedi.<ref name="The Separatists" />

[[File:PalpatineWhispersGOA.png|thumb|left|320px|Palpatine worked with Queen Amidala as Naboo's senatorial representative.]]
Around [[33 BBY]],<ref name="33 BBY">{{JangoFettSeriesDate}}</ref> the [[HoloNet News]] reported on how Chancellor Finis Valorum's administration was beleagured by mounting political crises, including a row with the Trade Federation over taxation and the [[Heist of the Hope of Glee Anselm|collapse]] of a Republic-mediated [[Anselmi–Nautolan treaty|reconciliation effort]] between the [[Anselmi]] and [[Nautolan]] peoples of the planet [[Glee Anselm]] in the Mid Rim. The broadcaster was aware that Republic citizens were tired of "doom and gloom" over threats of war, though such news boosted the company's ratings, and it additionally reported on a [[Unidentified Twi'lek pop icon|pop icon]] dating a [[Unidentified shockball star|shockball star]] as a counterpart to the negativity. Count Dooku took advantage of Glee Anselm's dire situation in further destabilizing the Republic government.<ref name="Jango 4">[[Jango Fett 4|''Jango Fett'' 4]]</ref>

====Two voices from Naboo====
{{Quote|So many people from all over the galaxy, and all they could do was squabble. I told them that our people were suffering, and they would have made us wait until some committee flew out to the Chommell sector and checked. They were more concerned with voting along the lines of their alliance than they were with listening to problems and trying to find solutions. It was a mess, and Chancellor Valorum did nothing. Nothing.|Padmé Amidala, to [[Sabé]]|<ref name="Queen's Peril">''[[Queen's Peril]]''</ref>}}
[[File:Naboo_Royal_Advisory_Council.png|thumb|right|350px|Queen Amidala and her council were frustrated with the Senate's inefficient response to the invasion of Naboo.]]
In [[32 BBY]], Naboo was blockaded and [[Invasion of Naboo|invaded]] by the Trade Federation upon the order of Lord Sidious&mdash;Viceroy Gunray wanted to force the corporation's Senate dispute on trade route taxation to conclude in their favor. However, the sovereign [[Naboo (people)|Naboo]] people had democratically elected the fourteen-year-old [[Padmé Amidala Naberrie|Padmé Amidala]] as their queen<ref name="TPM" />&mdash;her youth reflected Naboo culture's emphasis on education and protecting their monarchical tradition against the impersonal and cynical aspects of modern politics<ref name="Propaganda" />&mdash;who refused to sign a treaty with the Trade Federation. The Chancellor had also sent Jedi [[ambassador]]s Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi to Naboo, and they escorted Queen Amidala away from her world, which was being forcefully invaded by Viceroy Gunray's massive droid army.<ref name="TPM" /> The [[human]] Naboo people had colonized Naboo centuries ago, driving the native [[Gungan]] people [[Water|underwater]] after arriving on the planet as refugees fleeing the [[Grizmallti civil war]] in the Core Worlds,<ref name="Nexus" /> though the Trade Federation also drove the Gungans from their underwater cities.<ref name="TPM" />

Along the way to the Senate on Coruscant, Qui-Gon Jinn recruited the bumbling Gungan [[Jar Jar Binks]] and the human boy [[Anakin Skywalker]] despite Obi-Wan Kenobi's objections. Skywalker had been enslaved on Tatooine since his fatherless birth by his mother, [[Shmi Skywalker Lars|Shmi]], and Jinn found that the boy had great potential in the Force&mdash;the Jedi believed that Skywalker was the prophesized [[Chosen One]] who would restore harmony in the Force, despite the Jedi Council's misgivings, and formed a fatherly bond with the boy. Meanwhile, the Queen was frustrated with the Senate's inaction and complacency as she observed its debates, with Senator Aks Moe agreeing with the Federation's Senator Lott Dod to simply establish a commission to evaluate the events on Naboo.<ref name="TPM" />

Senator Palpatine then convinced Amidala that the Senate was suffering from Chancellor Valorum's weak leadership and alleged that he was being unduly influenced by the Republic's bureaucrats, who were in turn on the payroll of the Trade Federation. Palpatine thus successfully suggested Amidala to move a [[Vote of No Confidence]] in Valorum. Unwilling to sit by in further deliberations while her people were being rounded into prisoner camps, Queen Amidala then returned to Naboo with the Jedi. Despite the traditional mistrust between the human Naboo and the Gungans, Queen Amidala humbly appealed to the Gungan [[Boss]] [[Rugor Nass]] for help. Boss Nass was amused by the humans' casting aside of their typical sense of superiority and gladly accepted to form a coalition. The two peoples embraced their [[Symbiosis|symbiotic bond]] and [[Battle of Naboo|defeated]] the Trade Federation on their homeworld. However, Qui-Gon Jinn was slain by a [[Maul|Sith Lord]]&mdash;the likes of whom the Jedi Order had assumed to be extinct for a thousand years&mdash;who was then defeated by Obi-Wan Kenobi.<ref name="TPM" />

[[File:Globe_of_Peace_handover.png|thumb|left|350px|The humans and Gungans agreed to share their homeworld in peace after uniting to repel the Trade Federation invasion.]]
Meanwhile, with Valorum defeated in government, Palpatine took advantage of other senators' sympathy for Naboo to win the following [[Supreme Chancellor election, 32 BBY|Chancellorship election]], which was contested by [[Bail Antilles]] of [[Alderaan]] and Ainlee Teem of Malastare. Unbeknownst to virtually everyone else, Palpatine was secretly [[Darth]] Sidious&mdash;the [[Sith Master|master]] of Qui-Gon Jinn's killer and mastermind of the Trade Federation invasion.<ref name="TPM" /> As Supreme Chancellor, he retained [[Mas Amedda]] as the Republic's Vice Chancellor and the Senate's Vice Chair and Speaker, with the canny bureaucrat being one of the very few individuals who knew that Palpatine was a [[Sith Lord]].<ref name="Amedda">{{Databank|mas-amedda|Mas Amedda}}</ref> Dooku had allied with Sidious, believing that the Jedi blindly served a corrupt Senate and regretful over the death of Jinn, his former Jedi apprentice. Sidious anointed Dooku as his new [[Sith apprentice]] under the name "Darth Tyranus."<ref name="The Sith Lord">{{TOTJ|The Sith Lord}}</ref>

====A Republic divided====
{{Quote|Loyalty to the Republic is paramount. Loyalty to democracy.''"<br />"''And what if democracy does not return the favor?''"<br />"''Then you must work to restore the democratic process.|Senators Bail Organa and Padmé Amidala|<ref name="Shadow" />}}
[[File:TheAyesHaveIt-HOBS.png|thumb|right|370px|Having forsaken the Jedi, Dooku called on Republic star systems to secede and threaten to take the path of war.]]
The [[Gungan High Council]] subsequently contributed to their planet's democratic process alongside the humans, particularly for off-world relations.<ref name="Nexus" /> [[28 BBY|Four years after]] the Trade Federation invasion and after refusing an offer to amend the [[Constitution of Naboo|Naboo constitution]] to stay as queen, Padmé Amidala took up the post of Republic Senator for Naboo and the wider [[Chommell sector]] at the request of the newly elected Queen [[Réillata]]. Amidala joined the political faction led by Senators [[Bail Prestor Organa|Bail Organa]] of Alderaan, [[Onaconda Farr]] of [[Rodia]], and Mon Mothma of [[Chandrila]]. Although they suspected Senator [[Mina Bonteri]] of [[Onderon]] of covertly working against the Republic's interests and were wary that she was befriending Amidala, Organa spoke with the new senator and realized they shared faith in the Republic and its principles of galactic democracy.<ref name="Shadow" />

In [[24 BBY]], Dooku gave the landmark [[Raxus Address]], in which he lambasted the Republic's ineffectual bureaucracy and corruption and inspired many worlds to leave the Republic.<ref name="TSWB" /> Dooku unsuccessfully attempted to recruit Tarkin as Eriadu's governor to the Separatist cause, proving Tarkin's loyalty to Palpatine<ref name="Tarkin" /> and preventing the count from having a economic stranglehold over the Republic.<ref name="Rise" /> Chancellor Palpatine held on to power past the typical term limits due to the resulting Separatist Crisis, with evermore star systems discontent with the Republic's system of governance threatening to leave the union.<ref name="AOTC" />

Nute Gunray remained as [[viceroy]] of the Trade Federation despite undergoing four trials at the Republic's [[Supreme Court]], and, with an eye on personal profit, he secretly pledged military support for the secessionist movement's new rival state<ref name="AOTC" />&mdash;Dooku's Confederacy of Independent Systems.<ref name="TSWB" /> The Trade Federation's reparations to Naboo paid for the new [[Theed Spaceport]],<ref name="CL2016" /> and the courts additionally ordered the corporation to reduce its army&mdash;however, economic concerns meant the Jedi were not allowed to investigate rumors that this was not carried out.<ref name="AOTC" /> A number of the [[Haor Chall Engineering Corporation]]'s [[IG-227 Hailfire-class droid tank]]s, initially commissioned for the Intergalactic Banking Clan to enforce debt collection, were owned by [[Danry Ledwellow]], a senator of the Republic who took bribes from [[Thalassian]] slavers operating on [[Er'Kit]].<ref name="Commander">''[[Star Wars: Commander]]''</ref>

===War and peace===
====Road to Geonosis====
{{Quote|It's-a clear desa Separatists made a pact wesa desa Federation du Trade. Senators! Dellow felegates! In response to this direct threat to the Republic, mesa propose that the Senate give immediately emergency powers to the Supreme Chancellor!|Representative Jar Jar Binks|<ref name="AOTC" />}}
[[File:Loyalists-Yoda-AOTC.jpg|thumb|left|350px|The targeting of Senator Amidala, a vocal advocate of the Republic's constitutional and peaceful democracy, prompted intervention of Jedi.]]
Several senators, including Bail Organa, Padmé Amidala, Ask Aak, and [[Orn Free Taa]] of Ryloth, joined Chancellor Palpatine's [[Loyalist Committee]] as advisors, wishing to defend the Republic's integrity, democracy, and peace as more member systems joined the Separatists. On behalf of Queen [[Jamillia]], Amidala also tirelessly campaigned against the Republic's [[Military Creation Act|Military Creation Bill]], which she believed would prompt the Separatists to seek armaments from the Commerce Guild or Trade Federation. She knew the corporations had undue influence over Republic bureaucrats, judges, and senators and feared that they would push the galaxy into civil war.<ref name="AOTC" /> The veteran senator for the [[Raioballo sector]], [[Horox Ryyder]], supported Amidala's position against the bill but, depressed and exhausted by politics, bitterly retired from the Senate even though he was one of its most respected members. Ryyder subsequently toured every world that he had represented and continued speaking out against the endemic corruption in the Republic.<ref name="Chanceliers" />

Also among the bill's opposition were the [[Roshu Sune]], [[Gotal]] terrorists who wanted their homeworld of [[Antar 4]] to leave the Republic. A [[Santos Bel-Pak|poet laureate]] of [[Kooriva]] made the poster ''[[Political Doubletalk]]'' for the Roshu Sune's "[[United Committee for Galactic Peace]]" front, alleging that Chancellor Palpatine was engaging in [[Wikipedia:Doublespeak|linguistic distortion]], advocating security and order to actually benefit his politics of war.<ref name="Propaganda" /> The government of [[Carida]] supported the Military Creation Bill given that the [[Carida Academy]] of [[Republic Military]] personnel stood to benefit from it, investing over two million credits to back the re-armament campaign.<ref name="Prologue to the Clone Wars">{{EncyclopediaCite|Prologue to the Clone Wars and the First Battle of Geonosis}}</ref> Ahead of a crucial debate on the legislation, Dooku hired [[bounty hunter]]s to [[Plot to assassinate Senator Amidala|assassinate]] Amidala<ref name="AOTC" /> in [[22 BBY]] to no avail.<ref name="Timelines" />

At a meeting between Palpatine's committee and members of the Jedi Order, the High Council admitted that it was unaware of the identity of the assassins' benefactor but vowed to investigate. Amidala placed her suspicions on Dooku and believed that he intended to destabilize the Republic, though the leading Jedi Council member Ki-Adi-Mundi dismissed Dooku as simply a "political idealist." Later in private, Jedi Master Obi-Wan Kenobi discussed his skepticism of all politicians with his Padawan Anakin Skywalker; though Skywalker felt Amidala and Chancellor Palpatine were different from most politicians, Kenobi spoke from his experience that senators forgone the "niceties of democracy" to appease those who would fund their political campaigns, and he also thought that Palpatine cleverly took advantage of senators' "passions and prejudices."<ref name="AOTC" />

[[File:Right-AOTC.png|thumb|right|300px|Democratic politics were discussed between Anakin Skywalker (left) and Padmé Amidala (right).]]
On the Jedi Council's suggestion and the Chancellor's order, Amidala briefly hid on Naboo with Padawan Skywalker to prevent further attempts on her life. Queen Jamillia welcomed the two and&mdash;after discussing corporate corruption and influence over the Republic's bureaucratic, legislative, and judicial branches and contributions to the heightening tensions between the Republic and the Separatists with [[Governor of Naboo|Governor]] [[Sio Bibble]]&mdash;the queen declared that they must all maintain "faith in the Republic," as "the day we stop believing democracy can work is the day we lose it." As Amidala and Skywalker retreated to the meadows in private and fell in love, the pair debated democracy&mdash;as opposed to simply "popular rule," Amidala believed that constitutional laws such as term limits were integral to democracy so that politics gave people what they "needed" rather than simply what they "wanted," while Skywalker expressed his admiration for Palpatine as a strong leader.<ref name="AOTC" />

Meanwhile, Master Kenobi reported to Jedi Council leaders Yoda and Mace Windu of his discovery of a clone army. Kenobi was told that it had been ordered by the late Master [[Sifo-Dyas]] on [[Kamino]]<ref name="AOTC" />&mdash;an [[extragalactic]] world outside the Republic governed by [[Prime Minister]] [[Lama Su]] and his [[Ruling Council (Kamino)|Ruling Council]]<ref name="CL2016" />&mdash;and the [[clone trooper]]s who composed the army were based on the bounty hunter [[Jango Fett]], who claimed to have been hired by a "Lord Tyranus." Meanwhile, Tyranus himself, in his public persona as Count Dooku on the planet [[Geonosis]], closed a clandestine [[Geonosian accord|deal]] with corporate leaders who pledged support for the Separatist cause.<ref name="AOTC" />

Master Kenobi's discovery of a [[Separatist Droid Army]] on Geonosis prompted Vice Chair Mas Amedda to persuade Naboo's Gungan [[Junior Representative|representative]], Jar Jar Binks, to call on the Senate to [[Emergency Powers Act|grant]] the Chancellor unprecedented emergency powers to form a new Grand Army, whose ranks would be composed of the newly discovered clone troopers. Following Binks' urging<ref name="AOTC" /> and with it seeming as though there was little choice in fighting back,<ref name="Palpatine Image 9">{{Databank|palpatine-biography-gallery|Palpatine Biography Gallery}} {{C|Image 9 caption}}</ref> the fearful Senate<ref name="Palpatine Image 10">{{Databank|palpatine-biography-gallery|Palpatine Biography Gallery}} {{C|Image 10 caption}}</ref> empowered the Supreme Chancellor<ref name="AOTC" /> lest they be attacked by the Separatists<ref name="Palpatine Image 10" /> without a military of their own. Palpatine pledged his "love" for democracy and the Republic and promised to lay down his powers at the end of the conflict.<ref name="AOTC" /> 

[[File:JediSenateSidelines-AOTC.jpg|thumb|left|360px|The Jedi watch from the sidelines of the Senate, which votes to support Palpatine and his war.]]
Yoda later reflected that the Jedi were quick to join the conflict out of arrogance and fear, and even the wisest of the Jedi were clouded by the dark side arising from within themselves<ref name="Shroud">{{Rebels|Shroud of Darkness}}</ref>&mdash;Yoda and Windu were aware that their ability to use the Force had diminished but, believing that only the Sith knew of their weakness, decided against informing the Senate for fear that doing so would provoke more opposition against the Jedi. When a host of Jedi attempted to rescue Kenobi, Skywalker, and Amidala from Separatist detainment on Geonosis, Dooku, intent on starting a war, ordered the execution of all the Jedi there. Clone forces led by Yoda then rushed in and engaged the Separatists, swiftly winning the [[First Battle of Geonosis|battle]] under Jedi leadership while failing to stop the Separatist leadership from fleeing.<ref name="AOTC" />

====Rapid militarization====
{{Quote|You see, the victory of the clone army, for now, is the only thing that can lead us to peace.|Supreme Chancellor Sheev Palpatine|<ref name="Senate Murders">{{TCW|Senate Murders}}</ref>}}
[[File:Ahsoka8-BigTimetextless.png|thumb|right|250px|The Jedi Order found itself the military commanders of the Republic armed forces.]]
The Jedi were irrevocably drawn into the so-called Clone Wars. The [[Jedi Military Integration Act]] formally made Jedi Knights [[Jedi General|generals]] of the Grand Army of the Republic, and even young Padawan learners were folded into the frontiers of war, becoming [[Jedi Commander]]s.<ref name="Brotherhood" /> Darth Sidious had the additional advantage that the Jedi Council continued meeting in the Jedi Temple, where their wisdom was clouded by the historical Sith's dark side energy from the [[Shrine in the Depths]],<ref name="Tarkin" /> as well as by their own dark side, with more Jedi experiencing fear, anger, and hate.<ref name="Shroud" /> Under Jedi leadership, the clone troopers were deployed across the galaxy by the Republic<ref name="Brotherhood" /> and fought in the name of galactic democracy, an ironic idea considering that the clones themselves had few individual rights.<ref name="SWEasy">''[[Star Wars Made Easy]]''</ref> All the same, the clones quickly came to exhibit signs of individuality, which was encouraged by the Jedi.<ref name="Queen's Hope">''[[Queen's Hope]]''</ref> Republic warships, such as the [[Acclamator-class transgalactic military assault ship|''Acclamator''-class transgalactic military assault ship]], were considered military assets mobilized in the name of defending democracy.<ref name="Insider230">{{InsiderCite|230|Launchpad}}</ref>

The Separatist Droid Army answered directly to Dooku's minion, [[Supreme Martial Commander of the Separatist Droid Armies|Supreme Martial Commander]] General Grievous, rather than the Separatist Parliament.<ref name="TSWB" /> The Confederacy's laissez-faire model removed Republic protections against slavery and exploitation of workers as well as for autochthonous sentient beings' rights and environmental protection. While localized resistance arose on Separatist Alliance worlds, local governments ceded political control and resources to Dooku and the Separatist Council. The council oversaw the administration of government and the war effort, being formed of corporate backers<ref name="Rise" /> that dealt in secret, sidelining the Separatist Parliament.<ref name="TSWB" /> Parliament was led by [[Separatist Congress Leader|Speaker]] [[Bec Lawise]] with an ideology of minimal taxation and trade regulation, but the congress was divided between senators who opposed Dooku's war powers or war taxes and [[War Faction|senators]] who wanted to extend the war.<ref name="Rise" />

The Military Creation Act spurred the Republic's founding [[Corellian system|Corellia system]] to close its borders and other star systems to vow to retain their own defense forces or even declare their independence from the Republic&mdash;they wished to avoid paying more taxes to fund the new army.<ref name="Emperor Palpatine">{{EncyclopediaCite|Emperor Palpatine}}</ref> Cato Neimoidia and the Trade Federation declared neutrality at the start of the war,<ref name="Brotherhood" /> although the Federation's neutrality was little more than a mask to cover its eventually clear<ref name="MoF" /> bias towards<ref name="Sphere">{{TCW|Sphere of Influence}}</ref> and sponsorship of the CIS. In what was effectively an open secret by the war's end, other corporations, such as the [[Techno Union]], did much the same by declaring neutrality while helping to maintain the Separatist cause.<ref name="MoF" /> Despite her dislike of the Trade Federation's leadership, the [[Neimoidian Royal Guard|Neimoidian guard]] [[Ruug Quarnom]] believed in the concept of the Federation as a champion of the Neimoidians and thought that the Republic treated her people as no more than shipping statistics, though Quarnom also believed that some Republic citizens were reasonable. When [[Zarra|Cato Neimoidia's capital]] was [[Cadesura disaster|bombed]], both the Republic and the Confederacy sent emissaries to aid in investigating the disaster. Obi-Wan Kenobi represented the Republic while [[Asajj Ventress]] represented the Confederacy, and the latter radicalized [[Ketar Nor|Quarnom's colleague]] against the Republic and goaded him to try to bomb the Jedi.<ref name="Brotherhood" />

[[File:Palpatine_on_balcony.jpg|thumb|left|360px|Padmé Amidala's opposition to militarization in the Senate was overruled by Palpatine's emergency powers and machinations.]]
The [[Reflex Amendment]] to the Republic constitution gave the Chancellor's office near-total power over military matters, making Palpatine the most powerful Chancellor since the [[Ruusan Reformations]].<ref name="Collapse">''[[Collapse of the Republic]]''</ref> The Senate's [[Naval Subcommittee]] approved contracts with Techno Union subsidiary arms manufacturers such as [[Republic Sienar Systems|Sienar Systems]], Kuat Drive Yards, and [[BlasTech Industries]] to supply the [[fleet]]s. However, the Techno Union had also pledged its armies to Count Dooku, manufacturing a million Separatist [[battle droid]]s per Republic warship.<ref name="Rise" /> The Kuat subsidiary [[Rothana Heavy Engineering]] had supplied the Kaminoan government with the [[Acclamator-class transgalactic military assault ship|''Acclamator''-class cruisers]] that Yoda led on Geonosis,<ref name="Ships 1">{{EncyclopediaCite|Republic and Separatist Ships (1)}}</ref> and the commissioning of [[Venator-class Star Destroyer|''Venator-class Star Destroyers]] bolstered the Republic's deep-space warfare capabilities.<ref name="Collapse" />

====Moral quagmires====
=====Christophsis crises=====
{{Expandsect}}<!-- On the Front Lines offers a great deal about the political fallout -->
Republic Venators failed to breach Admiral Trench's blockade of the strategically positioned planet [[Christophsis]], where Senator Bail Organa of Alderaan reported a dire lack of food, water, and munitions at his Republic-run [[Bail Organa's Christophsis refugee camp|refugee camp]]. Among Trench's starships was a [[Invincible|''Providence''-class Dreadnought]], a [[Providence-class Dreadnought|class]] that was built by the Quarren-ran [[Free Dac Volunteers Engineering Corps]] and [[Pammant Docks]] for Viceroy Gunray of the Trade Federation following the Naboo invasion of 32 BBY.<ref name="Ships 1" /> Republic and Jedi ingenuity, coupled with the [[IPV-2C Stealth Corvette|Sienar stealth corvette]] and clone sacrifices, played a key role in eventually winning the [[Battle of Christophsis]], a victory that was hailed by [[Republic High Command]] as an example of Jedi-clone cooperation despite the betrayal of a clone trooper,<ref name="Front Lines">''[[Star Wars: On the Front Lines]]''</ref> Sergeant Slick.<ref name="The Hidden Enemy">{{TCW|The Hidden Enemy}}</ref> Slick's betrayal forced the Jedi to emotionally deal with the factor of free will within the clone ranks.<ref name="Front Lines" />

=====Legitimizing the Hutt Clan=====
{{Quote|We must help Jabba. This is the opportunity we've been looking for. The Jedi must rescue Jabba's son.''"<br />"''I don't like it, dealing with that criminal scum. This is a dark day for the Republic.''"<br />"''I agree, my friend, but what choice do we have? The Hutts control the Outer Rim, and we'll need their space lanes in order to move our troops.|Supreme Chancellor Palpatine defends allying with the Hutt Clan to Jedi General Mace Windu|<ref name="TCW">[[Star Wars: The Clone Wars (film)|''Star Wars: The Clone Wars'' film]]</ref>}}
[[File:Kharrus-TCWf.png|thumb|right|300px|In the name of securing victory, the Republic legitimized the criminal activities of the notorious Hutt Clan.]]
When the [[crime lord]] [[Jabba Desilijic Tiure|Jabba the Hutt's]] [[Rotta|son]] was kidnapped, Chancellor Palpatine asked the Jedi Order to rescue him so that the Republic may negotiate military access to [[Hutt]]-controlled hyperlanes in the Outer Rim. Mace Windu opined it was a "dark day for the Republic" to be working with the Hutt gangsters, but Palpatine defended the action as a necessary evil. In response, Obi-Wan Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker were diverted from Christophsis to negotiate a treaty with Jabba and rescue his son. After the Jedi found that Dooku and the Separatists had secretly kidnapped the child and framed Skywalker for it during the [[Battle of Teth]], Palpatine offered the news to Senator Padmé Amidala during a meeting about security measures the Chancellor had put into place on Naboo. Palpatine had justified those new security measures due to the sudden eruption of the Battle of Teth and other battles in the Outer Rim.<ref name="TCW" />

Concerned for her husband and becoming focused on the crisis, which stopped her from meeting with Palpatine about his security endeavors, Skywalker's predicament prompted Amidala to attempt to re-establish contact with Jabba and negotiate a treaty by herself as a representative of the Galactic Congress. She [[Raid on Ziro's Palace|exposed]] [[Ziro Desilijic Tiure|Ziro the Hutt's]] conspiracy with Dooku to force the Hutts against the Republic, and Skywalker safely returned to Jabba his son with his new Padawan, [[Ahsoka Tano]]. The resultant [[Hutt–Republic treaty|treaty]] allowing the Republic to use Hutt routes to the Outer Rim set back the Separatist war effort<ref name="TCW" /> while legitimizing the Hutts' businesses. Hutt Clan [[lawyer]]s further amended the agreement to allow the Hutts to peek into affairs in Republic space, access routes in the Mid Rim, and make inroads into the Core.<ref name="Scum" />

=====The Ryloth crisis=====
{{Quote|We need to remember why we're here. We came to aid the Twi'leks, not destroy their home.|Jedi General Obi-Wan Kenobi, to his troopers|<ref name="Innocents">{{TCW|Innocents of Ryloth}}</ref>}}
[[File:BinksImprovSculpture-SL.png|thumb|left|350px|Representative Binks deployed his antics to distract Neimodian delegates from Republic supply ships trickling up Toydaria's sky.]]
When the Separatists [[Invasion of Ryloth|blockaded and invaded]] the Republic member planet Ryloth,<ref name="Supply">{{TCW|Supply Lines}}</ref> local residents were forced to surrender after a group led by the radical [[Cham Syndulla]] was massacred near [[Lessu City]].<ref name="Liberty" /> Millions of Ryloth citizens were forced into starvation, and Republic Senator Bail Organa and Representative Jar Jar Binks [[The Matter of Using Toydarian Territory to Aid the Shipment of Relief Supplies to the Citizens of Ryloth|appealed]] to King [[Katuunko]] and his ministers on [[Toydaria]] for assistance. Although the [[Toydarian]]s were keen to remain "neutral" in the war due to pressure from Senator Lott Dod of the Trade Federation and formally refused to allow the Republic to ship relief supplies to Ryloth via Toydarian territory, King Katuunko privately gave assent for Organa and Binks to stage their relief mission in secret from the Trade Federation delegates on Toydaria.<ref name="Supply" /> The king subsequently decided that Toydaria should join the Republic after negotiations with the Separatists broke down given that Separatist [[Supreme Leader (Confederacy of Independent Systems)|leader]] Asajj Ventress had broken her word and [[Battle of Rugosa|ambushed]] the Republic delegation led by Master Yoda and the [[Coruscant Guard]]'s [[diplomatic service]].<ref name="Ambush">{{TCW|Ambush}}</ref>

Separatist [[Emir of Ryloth|Emir]] [[Wat Tambor]] of the Techno Union oversaw the occupation of Ryloth, tasking the [[T-series military strategic analysis and tactics droid|tactical droid]] [[TX-20]] with preventing Republic soldiers from landing using [[Proton cannon|heavy artillery]] in the city of [[Nabat]]. The native [[Twi'lek]]s were deprived of food and forcibly displaced, their homes having been destroyed while the streets were left barren&mdash;TX-20's battle droids took the Nabat's inhabitants hostage and used them as living shields among the cannons, predicting that the Jedi would not be willing to risk the hostages' lives. Indeed, Jedi General Obi-Wan Kenobi was unwilling to endanger the Twi'leks after he and his forces [[Attack on Nabat|breached]] Nabat's high wall, yet clone troopers [[Waxer]] and [[Boil]] found a displaced Twi'lek girl, [[Numa]], who showed them Nabat's underground tunnels. Kenobi's forces distracted TX-20 while the Jedi, Waxer, and Boil freed the Twi'leks and destroyed the cannons. Kenobi and Numa were nearly killed but the Twi'leks, unarmed but angry, overwhelmed TX-20's [[Armored Assault Tank Mk I|tank]] and dismantled the droid, freeing their own home.<ref name="Innocents" />

[[File:Syndulla_charge.png|thumb|right|300px|While he was greatly concerned for what future awaited Ryloth if he aligned with the Republic, Cham Syndulla believed he had no choice but to partner with the clone army due to the damage caused by Confederate occupation.]]
As Jedi General Mace Windu led Republic forces to Lessu, Count Dooku ordered Emir Tambor to evacuate from Ryloth while causing maximum damage, intending to signal the high cost of a Republic victory. Windu asked Cham Syndulla and his [[Twi'lek Resistance]] for support in [[Battle of Lessu|liberating]] the capital. Informed by his mistrust of Senator Orn Free Taa and the possibility of an armed occupation of his homeworld under the Republic, Syndulla was hesitant to commit his group to the Republic's efforts until he heard that Separatists had [[Firebombing campaign on Ryloth|firebombed]] villages around Lessu, which were filled with women and children. Senator Taa was likewise suspicious, concerned that Syndulla would seek power on Ryloth, but Windu convinced the two men to agree on a compromise. The senator promised that the Republic Army would leave Ryloth once the droids on the planet were beaten, and the freedom fighter affirmed that he believed in democracy.<ref name="Liberty" /> In an attack remembered by the Twi'leks as the "Hammer of Ryloth,"<ref name="Homecoming">{{Rebels|Homecoming}}</ref> the joint forces prevented the Separatists from destroying Lessu and accepted their unconditional surrender from Tambor shortly afterward. Additionally, for all of Syndulla's fears about the future of Ryloth under a now-Republic occupation,<ref name="Liberty" /> the battle had earned Windu his respect.<ref name="Homecoming" />

====A grievous commander====
{{Quote|General Grievous is a critical part of my plan for the Clone Wars.|Darth Sidious|<ref name="Shadow Warrior">{{TCW|Shadow Warrior}}</ref>}}
[[File:Keep_Our_Republic_Secure.png|thumb|left|300px|The brutal General Grievous (mask depicted as a shadow) was a useful figure to wartime Republic propaganda.]]
General Grievous was a critical figure in Sidious's plans for the Clone War.<ref name="Shadow Warrior" /> With the cyborg leading brutal campaigns across the galaxy,<ref name="Destruction Asmodee">{{Asmodee|subdomain=shop|url=star-wars-5-shatterpoint-appetite-for-destruction-swp05|text=Star Wars: Shatterpoint - Appetite for Destruction Squad Pack}}</ref> his actions spread fear and destruction across the Republic.<ref name="Rising Malevolence">{{TCW|Rising Malevolence|Rising ''Malevolence''}}</ref> When Grievous [[Battle on Caliban|captured]] Obi-Wan Kenobi and his [[212th Attack Battalion|clone battalion]] on [[Caliban]], he forced them to rebuild a [[Lunar ice bridge|bridge]] despite Kenobi objecting on the basis of the [[Galactic Accord of Systems]], which said prisoners of war should not be forced into manual labor.<ref name="Battle Tales 5">[[Star Wars Adventures: The Clone Wars – Battle Tales 5|''Star Wars Adventures: The Clone Wars – Battle Tales'' 5]]</ref> Jedi Master Ki-Adi-Mundi's encounter with General Grievous in the early weeks of the war brought to light the infamous brutality of the Separatist general,<ref name="Brotherhood" /> who led a [[Battle of Hypori|slaughter]] on [[Hypori]] that demoralized Republic forces.<ref name="Obi-Wan 3">[[Obi-Wan 3|''Obi-Wan'' 3]]</ref>

Indeed, Republic propaganda exploited Grievous as a figure of "evil," such as by depicting the general's mask in works like ''[[Keep Our Republic Secure]]'' and ''[[Evil Has Its Eyes On You]]''. Republic propaganda depicted Grievous as a "mechanical monstrosity," taking advantage of the deep-seated droidophobia that the Clone Wars kicked up and worsened.<ref name="Propaganda" /> Meanwhile, Separatist citizens were shielded from the worst of Grievous's actions.<ref name="Mina SWE">{{SWE|characters|minabonteri|Mina Bonteri}}</ref> Clone troopers listened and watched the [[Grand Army of the Republic broadcast]], hosted by [[Hologram VJ]] as "the voice of the Outer Rim" to keep up morale on the front lines, such as for the [[Mud Jumpers]] "slugging it out" on [[Mimban]].<ref name="Rookies">{{TCW|Rookies (episode)}}</ref> Mimban's rainforests were devastated by the Separatists' indiscriminate mining practices, resulting in significant mud deposits.<ref name="BC 51">{{BustCollectionCite|51|Character}}</ref> The Republic's [[224th Imperial Armored Division|224th Division]] trained and [[Battle of Mimban|fought]] alongside [[Iasento]] and his [[Iasento's tribe|tribe]] of [[Mimbanese]] against Separatist droids who invaded their world<ref name="Solo Guide">''[[Solo: A Star Wars Story The Official Guide]]''</ref> and sought its [[hyperbaride]] minerals.<ref name="Battles">''[[Star Wars: Battles that Changed the Galaxy]]''</ref>

[[File:Lyndon-Willoughby-TheCloneArmy.png|thumb|right|250px|Clone troopers were heralded as the last hope of a democracy that did not recognize their own rights.]]
Grievous also launched a devastating [[Malevolence Campaign|campaign]] on the Republic using the [[Subjugator-class heavy cruiser|dreadnought]] ''[[Malevolence]]'',<ref name="Rising Malevolence">{{TCW|Rising Malevolence|Rising ''Malevolence''}}</ref> which was built by the Free Dac Volunteers<ref name="Ships 1" /> and Pammant Docks<ref name="The Clone Wars Begin">{{EncyclopediaCite|The Clone Wars Begin}}</ref> and armed with [[mega-ion cannon]]s. The campaign spread fear across the Republic as it wiped out dozens of Republic warships<ref name="Rising Malevolence" /> and pushed into Core Worlds territory.<ref name="Timelines" /> The ''Malevolence'' destroyed Venators and even targeted [[Pelta-class frigate|medical transports]] and the [[Kaliida Shoals Medical Center]], which was undefended and filled with over 60,000 injured clone soldiers.<ref name="Shadow M">{{TCW|Shadow of Malevolence|Shadow of ''Malevolence''}}</ref> When Jedi-led forces were poised to overwhelm the ''Malevolence'', Grievous briefly took Senator Padmé Amidala hostage in an effort to call off the Republic assault before abandoning the ''Malevolence'', which Skywalker and Kenobi boarded, joined Amidala and C-3PO aboard, and reprogrammed by sending it into a crash course into the [[Dead Moon of Antar]].<ref name="Destroy M">{{TCW|Destroy Malevolence|Destroy ''Malevolence''}}</ref>

====Shadowfeeds and schemes====
{{Quote|The charter of Chancellor maintains the emergency powers granted by Palpatine ''[&hellip;]'' They are a poison to democracy.|Mon Mothma, reflecting as Chancellor of the New Republic on the office's past|<ref name="Aftermath" />}}
Until Republic forces [[Raid on Skytop Station|destroyed]] [[Skytop Station]], Grievous led another [[Skytop Station campaign|campaign]] in which he ambushed Republic fleets using intelligence collected via the [[listening post]].<ref name="Duel of the Droids">{{TCW|Duel of the Droids}}</ref> The [[Battlesphere|station]] was overseen by the ex&ndash;Republic [[Aqualish]] Senator [[Po Nudo]]'s [[Hyper-Communications Cartel]], which also ran anti-Republic [[Shadowfeed]]s on the [[HoloNet]],<ref name="Rise" /> supervised by the propagandist and Separatist parliamentarian [[Ansibella Dellu]] of Raxus Secundus. The Republic Navy was able to shut down the Shadowfeeds, and the [[Commission for the Protection of the Republic]] (COMPOR) was founded in response. Associated with Republic Admiral Wilhuff Tarkin, COMPOR produced populist propaganda that supported the consolidation of power in the Chancellorship; Palpatine was granted direct control of the HoloNet, which featured damning footage of Separatist rights violations that were timed to attempts for peace talks in the Senate.<ref name="Propaganda" /> Furthermore, official conversations between Republic senators and their Separatist counterparts was illegal under Senatorial decree for fear it would legitimatize the Separatist cause. Palpatine alleged that he was open to negotiation with the Separatists, only to also claim the CIS always ignored his alleged pleas for peace,<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides">{{TCW|Heroes on Both Sides}}</ref> leaving it to seem as though military victory in war was the only path forward.<ref name="Senate Murders" />

[[File:Gree_targets_Argyus.png|thumb|left|350px|Senate Guard Argyus betrayed the Republic in the hope of a lucrative reward.]]
Later in 22 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> Senator Farr of Rodia accepted a deal with Viceroy Nute Gunray to ally with the Separatists and capture Senator Padmé Amidala&mdash;Farr's longtime friend and Gunray's hated political nemesis&mdash;in exchange for vital supplies. Representative Jar Jar Binks and [[3PO-series protocol droid|protocol droid]] [[C-3PO]] [[Mission to Rodia|proved]] to be steadfast companions of Amidala's, and Senator Farr decided to help arrest Viceroy Gunray before Chancellor Palpatine pledged relief efforts to Rodia.<ref name="Bombad">{{TCW|Bombad Jedi}}</ref> Fearing that such turn of events meant Gunray would leak important secrets to the Jedi, Darth Sidious arranged for the viceroy to be either rescued or assassinated while he was in transit&mdash;Asajj Ventress worked with [[Senate Commando]] Captain [[Faro Argyus]] to [[Rescue on the Tranquility|free]] the [[prisoner]]. Having been promised a fortune by Count Dooku,<ref name="Cloak">{{TCW|Cloak of Darkness}}</ref> Argyus' betrayal of the Republic weakened the authority of the [[Senate Guard]]&mdash;elite soldiers clad in distinctive blue armor charged with defending the Republic Senate and its representatives&mdash;and Chancellor Palpatine took the opportunity to expand his favored contingent of [[Emperor's Royal Guard|Red Guards]].<ref name="Rise" />

As the war progressed, the [[Republic Security Council]] changed from a Senate committee that made recommendations to the legislature into an advisory council for the Supreme Chancellor on military matters. It oversaw decision-making on which worlds to defend or attack and, along with the Chancellor, could activate any of the [[Executive Order Codes for the Grand Army of the Republic|150 general orders]] subconsciously memorized by each clone in the Grand Army. While Republic civilians ran the [[Senate Bureau of Intelligence]], it was separate from the [[Republic Intelligence]] service under the military.<ref name="Rise" /> At some point during the war, Republic citizens staged a [[Anti-military protest at the Carida Academy|protest]] at the Carida Academy against militarizationin which a [[Cassian Jeron Andor's father|demonstrator]] was killed.<ref name="ROUVG">''[[Star Wars: Rogue One: The Ultimate Visual Guide]]''</ref>

The successful detonation of an [[electro-proton bomb]] secured the Republic's victory at the [[Battle of Malastare]], but it also awakened the [[Zillo Beast]] with its seemingly indestructible armor. The [[Dug Council]] [[Treaty of Malastare|agreed]] to give the Republic access to [[Malastarian fuel]] in return for the Republic's protection, and Chancellor Palpatine ordered the creature to be cloned for further research.<ref name="Zillo">{{TCW|The Zillo Beast}}</ref> Following the [[Second Battle of Geonosis]] and the capture of Archduke Poggle the Lesser, Palpatine presented [[Death Star plans|plans for a weapon of mass destruction]] to the new [[Strategic Advisory Cell]], a highly exclusive group of a few select senators, military officials, scientists, and industrial representatives that oversaw its [[Republic Special Weapons Group|Special Weapons Group]] in constructing the [[battle station]] in the [[Geonosis system]]&mdash;ostensibly necessary because the Separatists were developing one based on Geonosian schematics. [[Artificial intelligence]] specialist [[Doctor|Dr.]] [[Gubacher]] oversaw augmented droids to expedite progress on the station. Other scientists unknowingly attached to the project and assigned to developing its various aspects were monitored by a dedicated branch of Republic security.<ref name="Catalyst" />

The cell's members met at the [[Imperial Inspectorate HQ|Republic Center for Military Operations]], which had been remodeled and expanded by [[Orson Callan Krennic|Orson Krennic]] of the [[Republic Corps of Engineers|Corps of Engineers]], and were subject to the [[Official Secrets Oath|Official Secrets Act]], which required members to keep their business secret to outsiders, including the Jedi Order. Once appointed as coordinator of the Special Weapons Group, [[Lieutenant Commander]] Krennic took an interest in Dr. [[Galen Walton Erso|Galen Erso's]] renewable energy research for [[Zerpen Industries]], which studied [[kyber crystal]]s at an [[Vallt Experimental Fusion Facility|experimental fusion facility]] on [[Vallt]] in the Outer Rim. When Vallt joined the Separatist Alliance, Vice Chancellor Mas Amedda allowed Krennic to take the initiative leading Republic forces in [[Mission to Merj|abducting]] scientists working on a [[Biological warfare|virus]] that targeted clones from the Separatist world [[Merj]] and [[Vallt Rescue Operation|brokering]] a sham exchange on Vallt, in which the [[Erso family]] were freed only for Republic forces to bombard the [[Phara's faction|Separatist-allied faction]].<ref name="Catalyst" />

====Defenders of diplomacy====
{{Quote|What has happened to democracy&hellip; and why doesn't anyone seem to care?''"<br />"''Well, you do. Forgive me, my lady, umm&hellip; you're not like most politicians.''"<br />"''If only that were enough, Teckla.|Padmé Amidala and Teckla Minnau|<ref name="Pursuit story">{{StoryCite|story=Pursuit of Peace (short story)|book=The Clone Wars: Stories of Light and Dark}}</ref>}}
While they preferred the Separatists over the Republic, many major corporations that supported the CIS maintained the shaky mask of neutrality<ref name="MoF" /> in order to maintain their business with the Republic as a means of war profiting off both sides.<ref name="Umbara Commentary">{{HomeVideoCite|set=Star Wars: The Clone Wars The Complete Season Four|featurette=Darkened World of Umbara}}</ref> For example, the Trade Federation remained officially neutral and continued to be represented in the Republic Senate by Lott Dod.<ref name="Sphere" /> Other Separatist corporate backers, the Techno Union and the Banking Clan, likewise remained represented in the Senate by [[Gume Saam]] and [[Nix Card]] respectively. The trio plotted together despite Card's contempt for Saam.<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" /> Conversely, [[Magistrate]] [[Passel Argente]] of the Corporate Alliance and Kooriva withdrew as a member of the Republic Senate,<ref name="USWNE" /> and [[Voe Atell]] represented the alliance in the Separatist Parliament, which met on Raxus Secundus.<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" /> The Commerce Guild, which competed with the Corporate Alliance, similarly pledged its [[Commerce Guild Punitive Security Forces|forces]] to Count Dooku under [[Presidente]] [[Shu Mai]] of [[Castell]]<ref name="Rise" /> but did not openly join the Separatists,<ref name="CEUE">''[[Star Wars Character Encyclopedia: Updated and Expanded]]''</ref> though [[Amita Fonti]] represented the guild in the Separatist Parliament. The CIS prided itself as a state that was not under the rule<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" /> of corporate monopolies, as its members proclaimed the Supreme Chancellor<ref name="Disciple">''[[Dark Disciple]]''</ref> and Republic were.<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" /> However, with the establishment of the Separatist Council<ref name="AOTC" /> and the great power the corporations held as the Confederacy's sponsors, Separatist fighter [[Hressk]] would reflect that the Confederacy had indeed sold itself out to corporate masters, all in the name of maybe securing the right of self-determination.<ref name="MoF" />

[[File:PhaseIIArmor-SM.png|thumb|right|300px|Halle Burtoni was part of a grouping of senators who defended their pro-war attitude as Republic patriotism.]]
In [[21 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> Count Dooku and Jedi Generals Obi-Wan Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker were captured by [[Hondo Ohnaka]] and his [[Ohnaka Gang|pirate gang]] following a [[Skirmish at Vanqor|battle]]. The Republic sent the Gran Senator [[Kharrus]], Representative Jar Jar Binks, and diplomatic service [[Imperial shock trooper|shock troopers]] to deliver a hefty ransom of [[spice]] to the Ohnaka Gang in exchange for the captives. The [[Mission to Florrum|mission]] fell apart when Kharrus and several troopers were killed and Dooku and the Jedi escaped separately.<ref name="Dooku Captured">{{TCW|Dooku Captured}}</ref> As Senator Gume Saam of the Techno Union pushed for a [[Republic financial reform bill|financial reform bill]] to deregulate the InterGalactic Banking Clan to open more lines of credit to fund the Republic Military, the [[Loyalist]] Senators Padmé Amidala, Bail Organa, Onaconda Farr, and Mon Mothma led the opposition. The bill was also supported by the [[Kaminoan]] Senator [[Halle Burtoni]].<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" />

Meanwhile, Padawan Ahsoka Tano joined Amidala as the senator broke a [[Republic Senate non-communication law|Republic law]] prohibiting diplomatic contact with the Separatists by meeting the pro-peace [[Onderonian]] politician Mina Bonteri, and they spearheaded the [[Confederate–Republic peace initiative]]. When Voe Atell declared that the Corporate Alliance would not allow the Confederacy to make a peace offering in the Separatist Parliament, [[Kerch Kushi]] countered that the Confederacy was a democracy and, "unlike the Republic," was not ruled by corporations. Tano discussed the politics of war with Senator Bonteri's son, [[Lux Bonteri]], and learnt from her time on Raxus that there were heroes on both sides.<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" />

[[File:SeparatistSenate4-HOBS.png|thumb|left|350px|Arguments for and against peace with the Republic were hurled across the Separatist Parliament's benches.]]
However, Dooku bypassed the Separatist legislature by [[Bombing of Coruscant's central power distribution grid|bombing]] Coruscant's power supply, and then ordering a secret [[Attack on Confederate people|assassination]] of Mina Bonteri, blaming the murder on the Republic, which scuppered the peace process.<ref name="Heroes on Both Sides" /> Outraged members of the Senate such as the pro-war [[Tarnab]] [[Mot-Not Rab]] and [[Zinn Paulness]] approved the deregulation bill, debates on financial reform nevertheless continued with Senator Halle Burtoni's [[Republic military enhancement bill|proposal]] for the Republic to purchase five million more [[clone trooper]]s. The Banking Clan, which had been charging the Republic 10% interest on its war loan, claimed that it lended money to the Separatists at an increased interest rate to fund three million new battle droids and increased its Republic interest rate to 25%.<ref name="Pursuit TCW" />

Amidala believed that the Republic would be bankrupt if the Senate passed the bill, leading to public spending cuts to the maintenance of basic social services, education, healthcare, and infrastructure. However, senators who were not in support of the proposals were being intimidated and even severely assaulted by thugs hired by Dooku on Coruscant, which was already experiencing [[Coruscant mandatory blackouts|mandatory power blackouts]]<ref name="Pursuit TCW" /> and other harsh austerity measures of water and [[foodstuff]] under the [[Victory Rationing Program]].<ref name="Collapse" /> Despite Senators Farr, Amidala, and Organa being attacked in succession in the streets of Coruscant, Amidala's aide [[Teckla Minnau]] encouraged the Naboo politician to address the Senate.<ref name="Pursuit TCW" />

[[File:HoloNetSpeech-POP.png|thumb|right|350px|Senator Amidala was defiant in arguing against military spending increases at the expense of basic services to Republic citizens.]]
By citing Minnau as a Republic citizen whose livelihood depended on the basic services provided by the Republic, Amidala argued that the lives for which the Republic defended in the war would be upended if state funding was diverted to the military. Other senators were thus persuaded to defeat the bill, slowing the war to the Chancellor's private annoyance; Palpatine spoke with Mas Amedda about having "all the power in the galaxy" yet a single senator was able to "sway the thought of millions," resolving that they must, "for now," allow the Senate's proceedings to continue and follow "the principles of our democracy."<ref name="Pursuit TCW" />

The Senate eventually passed the proposal to increase military spending and rejected Senators Amidala and Farr's [[Reduced military spending bill|motion]] to reduce it<ref name="Senate Murders" /> despite Amidala having succeeded in persuading the [[Roonan]] Senator [[Aang]], who held the deciding vote on the issue on the [[Military Oversight Committee]].<ref name="Plans">{{TCW|Evil Plans}}</ref> After the vote, Palpatine claimed to Amidala that he did not support the increased troop production, but he also alleged ensuring the clone army's victory was the Republic's only option in pursuing peace and such was a natural thing to do after increasing secruity in the Senate. In any case, Palpatine claimed to Amidala that he was upholding democracy by allowing the policy he supposedly did not agree with to stand.<ref name="Senate Murders" />

====No safe refuge====
{{Quote|I know that there are those among the Separatists who would end this conflict, and I know that there are those individuals in the galaxy who would seek to promote fear and spread chaos. To those who act as agents of chaos, I say this: I stand resolute and unyielding. And if you strike my voice down, know that a chorus of thousands shall rise up in its place, for you have no dominion over the righteous. We are the defenders of truth.|Padmé Amidala|<ref name="Assassin">{{TCW|Assassin (episode)}}</ref>}}
[[File:Strength_and_Unity.jpg|thumb|left|260px|''[[Strength and Unity]]'' poster showcasing peoples of diverse backgrounds uniting for the Republic]]
As a part of the Republic, the [[Pantoran]] people of the [[moon]] [[Pantora]] claimed Pantora's planet, named [[Orto Plutonia]], as their [[Protectorate (government system)|protectorate]] territory as it was believed to be uninhabited. Despite discovering the presence of the native [[Talz]] people<ref name="Trespass">{{TCW|Trespass}}</ref> in 21 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> the Pantoran [[Supreme Chairman]] [[Chi Cho]] insisted on his people's sovereignty over the planet and declared [[One-Day War|war]]; the Jedi were not able to interefere in their capacity as the Republic Senate's peacekeepers as Chairman Cho declared that the Pantorans' dealings with the Talz were an internal affair. Pantoran Senator [[Riyo Chuchi]] then spoke with the democratic [[Pantoran Assembly]], which declared Cho's act of war as out of order and whose [[Speaker of the Assembly|speaker]] authorized the senator to petition the [[Jedi Council]] to interefere. However, Chuchi was able to negotiate directly with the Talz [[Chief]]tain, [[Thi-Sen]], ending the war with the Pantoran people recognizing the sovereignty of the Talz people.<ref name="Trespass" />

Later in 21 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> Onaconda Farr, Zinn Paulness, and fellow senator [[Kin Robb]] of [[Taris]] joined the [[Council of Neutral Systems]], a group of 1,500 star systems that wished to remain neutral in the Clone Wars and were represented in the Republic Senate. The Council was led by [[Duchess]] [[Satine Kryze]] of [[Mandalore]],<ref name="Mandalore Plot">{{TCW|The Mandalore Plot}}</ref> and many of its member systems' economies relied on trade with both Republic and Separatist systems.<ref name="Rise" /> Decrying violence as extremism, the Duchess became occupied with the terrorist activities of [[Death Watch]], a band of militant [[Mandalorian]]s that was funded by Count Dooku<ref name="Mandalore Plot" /> and later appropriated by the renegade Sith Lord and crime lord Maul.<ref name="TSWB" />

[[File:S3E6-AnakinAndAhsokaGreetedOnMandalore.png|thumb|right|350px|Away from the Republic's war, the Jedi Anakin Skywalker and Ahsoka Tano worked with the leaders and people of Mandalore.]]
Duchess Satine Kryze argued for the Mandalorian people's right to self-determination against a [[Mandalore Defense Resolution|Senate bill]] that would commit the Republic Military to "save" Mandalore.<ref name="Duchess">{{TCW|Duchess of Mandalore}}</ref> Although she managed to maintain Mandalore's neutrality from the Clone Wars, supply pressures encouraged corruption within the Mandalorian government and citizens turned to the [[black market]], including for [[tea]] that had been improperly diluted and turned [[Slabin|toxic]]. Prime Minister [[Almec]]'s [[Mandalorian Government Council|government council]] refused to seek Republic help as they believed the Republic was more corrupt, so the Duchess and Senator Amidala privately appealed to the Jedi for help.<ref name="Corruption" /> Padawan Ahsoka Tano was assigned to Mandalore to investigate corruption undercover, ostensibly to teach at the [[Imperial Academy of Mandalore|Royal Academy of Government]]. Tano spoke of citizens' "duty" to scrutinize their leaders to encourage honesty and maintain accountable governance, inspiring a group of [[cadet]]s led by [[Korkie Kryze]] to secretly infiltrate a food supply warehouse. They uncovered the Prime Minister's involvement in the [[Mandalorian black market conspiracy|black market conspiracy]], leading to his arrest.<ref name="Academy" />

Afterward, Senator Bail Organa hosted a [[Alderaan Refugee Conference|conference]] on Alderaan on the swelling numbers of refugees due to displacement by warfare. Padmé Amidala argued that the Senate should enact legislation to protect refugee rights as well as ensure that clone soldiers would have a sense of their own futures beyond the war. Amidala also advocated for opening diplomatic avenues to discuss an end to the war, stating that some in the Confederacy likewise desired peace while agents of chaos were everywhere and declaring that she would be steadfast in defending truth and democracy. Although another assassination attempt was made on Amidala's life, she was saved by Ahsoka Tano.<ref name="Assassin" />

When the Trade Federation [[Blockade of Pantora|blockaded]] Pantora following the election of [[Baron]] [[Notluwiski Papanoida]] as its Chairman, Senator Lott Dod of the Trade Federation refuted Pantoran Senator Riyo Chuchi's accusation that the corporation was aligned with the Confederacy of Independent Systems. Dod insisted in the Senate that his corporation was neutral and the Pantorans had outstanding debts, but Amidala and Tano privately supported Chuchi's efforts to resist the hostile expansion of the Separatist sphere of influence. Count Dooku promised aid for Pantora should it join his Separatist Alliance while bounty hunters [[Kidnapping of the Papanoida daughters|abducted]] Baron Papanoida's daughters, [[Che Amanwe Papanoida|Che Amanwe]] and [[Chi Eekway Papanoida|Chi Eekway]]. The chairman surmised that the "wheels of democracy" were turning too slowly for his people, whose views soured on a seemingly unsympathetic Republic Senate and popular sentiment pivoted toward joining the Separatists. However, instead of accepting Dooku's offer, he took upon himself to investigate his daughters' captors while Chuchi and Tano [[Rescue on the Pantora Droid Control Ship|inspected]] the Trade Federation blockade, uncovering the commerce giant's conspiracy. Although the Trade Federation lifted the blockade, Lott Dod was able to insist that the corporation was not associated with the Separatists, denouncing the supervising officer [[Sib Canay]] and arguing that Canay was influenced by Nute Gunray.<ref name="Sphere" />

[[File:ImInControl-CadeBaneOperativeExpansion.png|thumb|left|250px|At the heart of the Republic, the Senate was sullied by a team of bounty hunters hired by the Hutt Clan.]]
Later in 21 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> the Senate was debating the [[Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill]], which proposed allowing the Chancellor to spy on Republic citizens suspected of being a Separatist,<ref name="VE" /> when a group of senators in opposition were [[Senate hostage crisis|taken hostage]] inside the [[Imperial Executive Building|Senate Office Building]] by [[Cad Bane]]. Leading other bounty hunters, Bane was hired by the Hutt Clan to demand the release of Ziro the Hutt,<ref name="Hostage">{{TCW|Hostage Crisis}}</ref> who, if left in Republic custody, would be able to reveal the [[Grand Hutt Council]]'s many crimes to the Senate via a [[holodiary]] only he knew the location of.<ref name="Hunt for Ziro">{{TCW|Hunt for Ziro}}</ref> While Ziro was set free and the Gran Senator [[Philo]] was killed, the other hostage senators survived, among them Padmé Amidala, Riyo Chuchi, Bail Organa, Onaconda Farr, and Kin Robb.<ref name="Hostage" /> Meanwhile, Bane and his crew escaped into the Coruscant underworld, distracting a pursuing force of Jedi by shooting the engines out of a nearby transport. In the end, Bane and his company were able to escape with Ziro,<ref name="Dangerous Debt">{{TCW|Dangerous Debt}}</ref> who was returned to the Hutt capital of Nal Hutta.<ref name="Hunt for Ziro" />

====Mon Calamari and Quarrens; Gungans and the Naboo====
{{Quote|Rish Loo manipulated mesa head-thoughts with lies and misinformation. He's the real enemy.|Boss Lyonie, on Naboo|<ref name="Shadow Warrior" />}}
The Quarren [[Tikkes]]'s election as Senator of Mon Cala was approved by the [[Calamari Council|Mon Calamari government council]], but he joined the Separatists and gained a seat on the Executive Separatist Council when the war began,<ref name="The Separatists" /> representing the Free Dac Volunteers Engineering Corps and [[Pammant Docks]], which produced Separatist [[capital ship]]s.<ref name="OF84">{{GermanFanCite|84|Database}}</ref> Other Separatist corporate leaders likewise supplied their [[starship]]s and forces to the [[Confederacy military]]. Tikkes was replaced in the Republic Senate by the Quarren [[Tundra Dowmeia]], who shared representation of Mon Cala with the Mon Calamari Senator [[Meena Tills]].<ref name="VE">''[[Star Wars: The Visual Encyclopedia]]''</ref>

[[File:LeeCharCrowning-Prisoners.png|thumb|right|350px|Both the Mon Calamari and Quarren peoples recognized Lee-Char as their king following a civil war stoked by the Separatists.]]
In [[20 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> [[Battle of Mon Cala|civil war]] erupted between the Quarren and Mon Calamari peoples on their shared homeworld after the Separatist-backed [[Quarren Isolation League]] of Chieftain [[Nossor Ri]] rebelled against the [[Mon Calamari monarchy]], which had governed along with [[Calamari Council|representatives]] of both species. The Mon Calamari Senator Meena Tills appointed [[Gial Ackbar]] the personal protector of Prince [[Lee-Char]], heir to the throne, and appealed to the Jedi and the Republic for help, receiving additional Gungan reinforcements from Naboo. Meanwhile, the Quarren were supported and had been pushed into the war by Separatist ambassador [[Riff Tamson]],<ref name="Water War">{{TCW|Water War}}</ref> who had been promised control over Mon Cala by Count Dooku should the Confederacy win the battle.<ref name="Prisoners">{{TCW|Prisoners}}</ref> For all the deception his plans relied on, however, Tamson himself was a legitimate believer in the Confederate cause, which he saw as the best way to uplift his fellow [[Karkarodon]]s.<ref name="RiffTamsonDownload">{{AtomicMassGames|url=transmission/databank-download-riff-tamson|text=Databank Download: Terror from the Deep Squad Pack – Riff Tamson}}</ref> After they discovered that Dooku had promised Tamson control of their world, the Quarrens agreed to [[Coronation of Lee-Char|restore]] the monarchy under the Mon Calamari [[King]] Lee-Char.<ref name="Prisoners">{{TCW|Prisoners}}</ref> All the same, Tikkes remained loyal to the Confederacy and maintained his seat on the Separatist Council.<ref name="ROTS">{{Film|III}}</ref>

Dooku focused on conquering Naboo, which was experiencing civil unrest as the Gungans' [[Otoh Gunga|city]] experienced power shortage. With Dooku promising the Gungan minister [[Rish Loo]] that he would play a key part in the "new order" of a Separatist-occupied Naboo, Rish Loo manipulated the popular Boss [[Lyonie]] using [[Gungan magic|sorcery]]. Lyonie recounted that Rish Loo fed him "lies and misinformation" to stoke discontent against the human Naboo, blaming them for all the troubles of Gungan society. The [[Gungan Grand Army]] embraced Lyonie's rhetoric and was ready to march on the city of Theed, where Queen [[Neeyutnee]] asked Senator Padmé Amidala and Jedi General Anakin Skywalker to find a diplomatic solution. The latter pair spoke with Representative Jar Jar Binks and General [[Roos Tarpals]]&mdash;a veteran of the battle against the Trade Federation and a firm believer in the friendship between the Gungans and the Naboo&mdash;broke Rish Loo's influence on Lyonie, and convinced the Gungan boss to tell the Gungan army to stand down.<ref name="Shadow Warrior" />

However, General Grievous' Separatist Droid Army had been invited to march alongside the Gungans, forcing Representative Binks to masquerade as Boss Lyonie to stall the attack. The Gungans deactivated the droid army and Tarpals sacrificed himself to capture Grievous, yet Darth Sidious, needing the general for his war plans, exploited Senator Amidala's relationship with Skywalker and ordered Dooku to capture the Jedi for a prisoner exchange. Despite believing that Grievous' capture would win the war, Amidala agreed to the exchange with the support of Binks, Boss Lyonie, and Queen Neeyutnee.<ref name="Shadow Warrior" />

====Droid tales====
{{Quote|Congratulations! You are now a democracy!|C-3PO, to the Patitites|<ref name="Nomad">{{TCW|Nomad Droids}}</ref>}}
The [[Aleena]] people on the planet [[Aleen]] maintained a pastoral way of life organized by [[Religion|faith]] and families, which formed [[tahiko]] kinship groups. Tahikos were represented by their eldest member in a planet-wide collective of advisors who elected the largely ceremonial [[King of Aleen]], typically a [[priest]]. The Aleena had little contact with the wider galaxy to maintain peace with the [[Aleen Underworld|Underworld]], home to the [[Kindalo]] people, following a destructive and technologically advanced [[Aleena–Kindalo war|war]] between them millennia before the Clone Wars. The notional Kindalo government was a system of anarchical consensus, never taking action on a societal level unless every Kindalo agreed. The Kindalo therefore had only a handful of absolute laws, one of which was the compact that they shared with the Aleena: the separation of the two peoples, enforced by the [[Great Seal]],<ref name="Nexus" /> which the Republic [[droid]]s C-3PO and [[R2-D2]] helped [[Mission to Aleen|reseal]] when it was disturbed<ref name="Mercy">{{TCW|Mercy Mission (episode)}}</ref> later in 20 BBY.<ref name="Timelines" />

[[File:ThreepioPatititesDemocracy-Nomad.png|thumb|left|350px|C-3PO introduced democracy to the Patitites after shoving R2-D2 onto their dictator.]]
C-3PO and R2-D2, who respectively assisted Senator Amidala and her Jedi [[Wedding of Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala|husband]], Anakin Skywalker,<ref name="Timelines" /> then stumbled upon the planet [[Patitite Pattuna]], where they accidentally and literally stumbled upon the dictator "Big" [[Hay-Zu]]; the potentate of the petite [[Patitite]] people was killed when C-3PO knocked over R2-D2 for making an uncivil remark and the falling R2-D2 crushed the "big" dictator. When the Patitites requested C-3PO to stay as their new leader, the [[Protocol droid|droid]] instead suggested that they find someone among their own. C-3PO asked them to nominate three of the most intelligent, understanding, and compassionate individuals and then asked the remaining people to shout their support for the three nominees in turn. The crowd was unable to decide the winner and began scuffling, at which point C-3PO decided to leave them to build their "happy democracy" on their own.<ref name="Nomad" />

====Mounting frustrations====
{{Quote|One shudders to think where the galaxy would be without the Jedi.|Supreme Chancellor Palpatine, to Jedi Anakin Skywalker and Obi-Wan Kenobi|<ref name="Crisis on Naboo">{{TCW|Crisis on Naboo}}</ref>}}
A [[Confederate–Republic peace conference|peace conference]] between Republic and Separatist politicians was chaired by Duchess Satine Kryze<ref name="Friend">{{TCW|A Friend in Need}}</ref> in 20 BBY.<ref name="Timelines" /> It was attended by Senators Amidala, Organa, and Mothma of the Republic and the Separatist Parliament's speaker, Bec Lawise, and members Voe Atell and [[Amita Fonti]]<ref name="Friend" />&mdash;the latter two were corporate-allied politicians who had little interest in peace,<ref name="Collapse" /> and the talks indeed collapsed: the Separatists arrested Lux Bonteri for accusing Dooku of assassinating his mother, the late Senator Mina Bonteri, when barging into the meeting, and the Jedi Ahsoka Tano and R2-D2 battled the Separatist-allied Death Watch Mandalorians to rescue him.<ref name="Friend" />

[[File:BattleOfOnderon-TP.png|thumb|right|350px|Onderonians fought for their own freedom from the Separatists, though the Republic and the Jedi were slow to provide support.]]
Lux Bonteri later helped persuade Jedi and clone forces to secretly train [[Onderon rebels|insurgents]] led by [[Steela Gerrera|Steela]] and [[Saw Gerrera]] in their "[[Onderonian Civil War|armed struggle]]" against the Separatist-allied [[Royal Court of Onderon|monarchy]] of [[Sanjay Rash]] on Onderon. Rash and his Separatist masters had [[Invasion of Onderon|deposed]] [[Ramsis Dendup]] as [[Monarch of Onderon|king]], whom they tried to publicly execute after he emphasized that dissent was the "will of the people." After Steela and many other rebels had been made martyrs in skirmishes, Onderon militias repelled the Separatists with additional [[RPS-6 rocket launcher|firepower]] supplied by Hondo Ohnaka and his pirates, courtesy of a deal with Anakin Skywalker&mdash;who was frustrated with the Jedi Order's refusal to partake in the civil war&mdash;thus allowing the restored king to assert Onderon's freedom and appoint Bonteri Onderon's representative to the Republic Senate.<ref name="Tipping">{{TCW|Tipping Points}}</ref>

Later in 20 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> Count Dooku organized a bounty hunter [[Plan to kidnap Chancellor Palpatine|plot]] to kidnap Supreme Chancellor Palpatine during a [[Festival of Light (847th)|public address]] on Naboo.<ref name="Crisis on Naboo" /> The Jedi High Council had Obi-Wan Kenobi fake his own death to infiltrate the bounty hunters, and the Jedi kept their counter-operation secret from both the Republic<ref name="Scum" /> and the wider Jedi Order, including Anakin Skywalker.<ref name="Deception">{{TCW|Deception}}</ref> After the Jedi foiled the kidnapping attempt, Skywalker privately confessed his frustration with Kenobi and the council's lack of trust in him to the Chancellor when they were [[Skirmish in Theed Royal Palace|ambushed]] by Dooku. Having successfully distracted the rest of the Jedi security detail, the Sith Lord overwhelmed Skywalker and nearly captured Palpatine when Kenobi intervened.<ref name="Crisis on Naboo" />

Darth Sidious viewed Asajj Ventress and the [[Nightsisters|witches]] of [[Dathomir]] as a threat to his plans. After forcing his apprentice to abandon Ventress as the Separatists [[Battle of Sullust|withdrew]] from [[Sullust]],<ref name="Nightsisters">{{TCW|Nightsisters (episode)}}</ref> which was home to the Commerce Guild's [[SoroSuub Corporation]],<ref name="Rise" /> Sidious tasked General Grievous with the [[Battle of Dathomir|massacre]] on Dathomir, which was outside the Republic and beyond its protection.<ref name="Massacre">{{TCW|Massacre}}</ref> During the war, the Republic also established a [[Population Observation]] program that surveyed public opinions on various aspects of the conflict, which revealed increasing [[anti-Jedi sentiment]].<ref name="Kempshall" /> While the Jedi Order had been thrusted into warfare, some Jedi insisted on remaining true to their traditions whereas others were willing to explore more extreme methods.<ref name="Collapse" /> Jedi General [[Pong Krell]] had become disdainful of clone lives and the Republic, and after sending scores of men to their deaths at the [[Battle of Umbara]]<ref name="Carnage">{{TCW|Carnage of Krell}}</ref> in 20 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> he was relieved of service and killed by clone troopers.<ref name="Carnage" />

[[File:PalpatineArrivesTheed-CoN.png|thumb|left|350px|Palpatine increasingly exploited the Jedi Order's vulnerabilities, particularly Anakin Skywalker's vulnerabilities.]]
Jedi Knight [[Barriss Offee]] was critical of the Order's involvement in the war and [[Bombing of the Jedi Temple hangar|bombed]] the Jedi Temple<ref name="Wrong">{{TCW|The Wrong Jedi}}</ref> in [[19 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> killing fellow Jedi and also clone troopers and civilians to make her point. A number of Coruscanti accused the Jedi of corruption and lambasted their government's "cloning violence" during anti-war protests held in front of the Jedi Temple.<ref name="Wrong" /> The Jedi were deliberately omitted from the Republic's wartime propaganda, and on the Separatist side, the mysterious Jedi were also branded as hypocrites&mdash;self-styled "guardians of peace" who gladly led "slaves bred for war."<ref name="Propaganda" />

During the investigation into the bombing, Offee framed Ahsoka Tano for her own terrorism, and the Jedi Council expelled Tano from the Order so that she may be trialed by the Republic Military. Chancellor Palpatine oversaw the proceedings as [[Adjutant General]] Tarkin pressed for Tano's prosecution while the jury was comprised of senators. Among them were Riyo Chuchi, Mon Mothma, Bail Organa, Mot-Not Rab, and Kin Robb, while Senator Padmé Amidala spoke in Tano's defense. The court case nearly came to a close in favor of Tano's prosecution when Anakin Skywalker presented Offee, whom he had captured following an aggressive confrontation, and she admitted the truth of her actions, vocally condemning the Jedi as being responsible for the Clone Wars while denouncing the Republic, claiming that the democratic union was failing. Although the Jedi Council agreed to invite Tano back into the Order as a Jedi Knight, she decided to refuse and instead find her own path.<ref name="Wrong" />

====Unrestricted power====
{{Quote|In hope of a better tomorrow, we cede control of the banks to the Office of the Chancellor of the Galactic Republic.|Banking Clan representative Nix Card|<ref name="Crisis at the Heart" />}}
[[File:Invest_Wisely.jpg|thumb|right|240px|''[[Invest Wisely]]'' poster, promoted by the IGBC to encourage more business with the bank.]]
Later in 19 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> the Banking Clan continued funding both sides of the war, and its [[Core Five]] leaders were convincted of embezzlement by the [[Muun]] government, which appointed [[Rush Clovis]] at the agreement of both the Republic, whose diplomatic service delegation at the [[Main Vault fortress|Banking Clan headquarters]] on [[Scipio]] was led by Senator Padmé Amidala, and the Separatists, whose delegation was represented by Bec Lawise. When Count Dooku secretly threatened Clovis that the Separatists would default on their Banking Clan loans, Clovis raised interest rates on the Republic. Senators Kin Robb, Meena Tills, and Mot-Not Rab expressed outrage at the bank that was amplified when Chancellor Palpatine confirmed that the Separatists did not experience a similar hike in rates.<ref name="Crisis at the Heart" />

The Republic delegation was then ambushed by Separatist forces, which arrested Senator Amidala upon Dooku's order. When Bec Lawise protested, claiming that the Separatist Parliament would never approve of the arrest, Dooku personally redirected Amidala's [[blaster]] to fire upon Lawise before abandoning Scipio. Given the support of senators alarmed by the news of the Separatist takeover of the banks, Chancellor Palpatine authorized a Republic [[invasion of Scipio]], forcing the bank to cede its assets to the Office of the Chancellor.<ref name="Crisis at the Heart" />

====Stirrings in the Force====
{{Quote|Remember&hellip; you always have a choice to be better. You always have a choice to&hellip; to pick the right path. Even if that choice comes a little late.|Asajj Ventress, to Quinlan Vos|<ref name="Disciple" />}}
In the same year,<ref name="Timelines" /> [[Monarch of Bardotta|Queen]] [[Julia]] appealed to the Senate&mdash;specifically Jar Jar Binks&mdash;for help when members of her ruling [[Bahk-tov Council]] began to disappear.<ref name="Disappeared I">{{TCW|The Disappeared, Part I}}</ref> Although the council was led by the monarch whom it elected, policy decisions could only be made via an unanimous vote of the council, which included members of the pacifist [[Dagoyan Order]].<ref name="Nexus" /> The Dagoyan Masters were abducted by the militant [[Frangawl Cult]], but they were defeated by Jedi Master Mace Windu, who had been dispatched as Representative Binks' partner. Queen Julia thus proposed a new era of more friendly relations between the Dagoyan and Jedi Orders.<ref name="Disappeared II">{{TCW|The Disappeared, Part II}}</ref>

[[File:EpGuide611.png|thumb|left|350px|Already troubled by eroded public faith, the Jedi Order struggled to comprehend the Sith plot behind the clone army, protracted conflict, and centralization of the Republic.]]
Already bogged by three years of warfare, the Jedi Council was shocked by Dooku's public [[Atrocity on Mahranee|genocide]] on [[Mahranee]] and sent Master Quinlan Vos to secretly [[Mission to Raxus Secundus|assassinate]] Dooku. Vos teamed up with Dooku's [[Dark Acolyte|ex-pupil]] Asajj Ventress, fell in love with her, and, despite Ventress' warnings, succumbed to the singular mission of targeting Dooku&mdash;he fell to the dark side and became a deadly Separatist admiral. The Jedi Council realized that the plan was a grave strategic and moral mistake, particularly as Ventress continued making personal sacrifices to save Vos and persuaded him to choose the light. Vos did discover that Dooku himself was the Sith apprentice of Darth Sidious, the name of the Sith Lord whom Dooku claimed was controlling the Republic at the start of the war.<ref name="Disciple" /> Late in the third year of the war,<ref name="Collapse" /> Obi-Wan Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker further [[Mission to Oba Diah (Clone Wars)|discovered]] that Dooku was the very same "Lord Tyranus"<ref name="Lost">{{TCW|The Lost One}}</ref> who had hired Jango Fett to be the clone troopers' template and worked with Sifo-Dyas&mdash;the Jedi Master who had placed the order for a clone army on behalf of the Republic with the Kaminoans without the Senate's authorization.<ref name="AOTC" />

Embarking on a spiritual quest where he learned about the Living Force's relationship with the Cosmic Force, Grand Master Yoda accepted that he had a dark side, as with every Jedi. Yoda believed that the Jedi's collective ignorance of their own darkness had already cost them victory in the war, yet he resolved to commit to the light&mdash;and nearly uncovered Darth Sidious' identity.<ref name="Sacrifice">{{TCW|Sacrifice (episode)}}</ref> The Sith Lord therefore rushed to implement his Grand Plan against the Jedi by instigating the [[Battle of Coruscant]].<ref name="Collapse" /> Separatist forces led by General Grievous kidnapped the Supreme Chancellor from Coruscant, prompting the Jedi Council to recall Kenobi and Skywalker from the [[Outer Rim Sieges]].
