The act foiled an attempt by Sidious' ex-apprentice, Maul, to lure Skywalker to the [[Siege of Mandalore|war-torn]] world of Mandalore and caused Maul&mdash;who knew of the Grand Plan&mdash;to send his [[Shadow Collective]] criminal empire into hiding.<ref name="Apprentice">{{TCW|The Phantom Apprentice}}</ref> Against Jedi ethics, Anakin Skywalker executed Dooku at Palpatine's command at the battle, furthering his fall to the dark side.<ref name="ROTS" />

[[File:Do_Not_Bind_His_Hands.png|thumb|right|240px|''[[Do Not Bind His Hands]]'' poster praising Palpatine's procuring of power.]]
Although previous peace initiatives had failed, Padmé Amidala was a part of the [[Delegation of 2,000]],<ref name="Dawn">''[[Star Wars: Dawn of Rebellion: The Visual Guide]]''</ref> Senatorial representatives including Meena Tills, Tundra Dowmeia, Mon Mothma,<ref name="VE" /> [[Fang Zar]] of [[Sern sector]] worlds including [[Ghorman]],<ref name="EoCW">{{EncyclopediaCite|The End of the Clone Wars}}</ref> Chi Eekway Papanoida in Riyo Chuchi's stead,<ref name="Dawn" /> and [[Terr Taneel]] of the [[Senex sector]]&mdash;who [[Cantham House meetings|gathered]] at Bail Organa's [[Cantham House]] office.<ref name="TRF">''[[Star Wars: The Rebel Files]]''</ref> The [[Sector Governance Decree]]&mdash;an amendment to the Galactic Constitution of sending [[governor]]s to coordinate the Republic war effort by procuring any production facilities in their assigned sectors<ref name="OF84" />&mdash;was passed late into the third year of the conflict and served as the catalyst for the delegation, who planned to present their [[Petition of 2,000|petition]] to Chancellor Palpatine to let go of his emergency powers once they had reached 2000 signatures.<ref name="Collapse" /> As the Outer Rim Sieges cornered the Separatists, the delegation wanted Palpatine to revoke his Sector Governance Decree and start ceasefire talks.<ref name="TRF" />

===Dictatorship and tyranny===
====Fall of the Jedi====
{{Quote|This war represents a failure to listen. Now you're closer to the chancellor than anyone. Please, ask him to stop the fighting and let diplomacy resume!|Senator Padmé Amidala, to Anakin Skywalker|<ref name="ROTS" />}}
[[File:Jedi_Council_RotS.png|thumb|left|350px|The Jedi Council was concerned that the dark side clouded their foresight and questioned Palpatine's powers in the final days of the war.]]
Upon Dooku's demise, the Jedi Masters believed that the war would end once General Grievous was captured. The Jedi also wanted Palpatine to step down, given that his term limit had expired long ago and the Senate was due to vote on whether to grant the Chancellor even more emergency powers. Nevertheless, the council focused on the [[Outer Rim Sieges]] pursuing the Separatists&mdash;at the suggestion of Master Ki-Adi-Mundi, Yoda was diverted to [[Battle of Kashyyyk|defend]] the [[Wookiee]]s on [[Kashyyyk]].<ref name="ROTS" /> Yoda was burdened by his premonition that an era was at an end throughout the war,<ref name="Shroud" /> but before he left Coruscant, the Grand Master gave counsel to Anakin Skywalker. The Jedi war hero had been [[Force vision|dreaming]] about his secret lover, Padmé Amidala, dying at [[Reproduction|childbirth]], and Yoda asserted that the young man must let go of everything he feared losing.<ref name="ROTS" />

[[File:Anakin_Padme_Hug.png|thumb|right|350px|Skywalker was not in the mood for political discussion with Senator Amidala, instead focusing on his fear that she would die at childbirth.]]
Skywalker discussed his worry that the Jedi Order was breaking the principles of the Republic due to the war with Amidala. The senator replied with her concern that the Republic was becoming the "evil" they had been "fighting to destroy." She was questioning if the democracy that thought they were serving no longer existed and thought that the war represented a "failure to listen," pleading Skywalker to use his closeness with Palpatine to call for a return to diplomacy. Skywalker compared her concerns to the Separatists but also admitted that he was irritated&mdash;not least by his nightmares, which reminded him of his visions of his [[Shmi Skywalker Lars|mother]] dying.<ref name="ROTS" />

Skywalker promised Amidala that he would not let her die and also spoke with Palpatine, who appointed the troubled Jedi as his representative to the Jedi High Council, which in turn troubled the Jedi Masters given that they had elected their own members and were concerned about Skywalker's friendship with Palpatine. The council did allow Skywalker to join&mdash;but without the rank of Jedi Master, and, off-record, the masters further tasked Skywalker to spy on the Chancellor. Although Skywalker believed that it was [[treason]] against the Jedi Code, the Republic, and Palpatine as his longtime [[Teacher|mentor]] and friend, Obi-Wan Kenobi emphasised that they were at war and that the Jedi's allegiance was to the Senate, rather than to its leader.<ref name="ROTS" />

[[File:Palpatine_and_Anakin.jpg|thumb|left|350px|Skywalker's friendship with the Chancellor and their strained relations with the Jedi Council reinforced the young man's belief that both Palpatine and himself deserved more powers to finish the war.]]
Darth Sidious contacted General Grievous on Utapau and ordered him to send the Separatist Council to the hellish world of [[Mustafar]]. The Sith Lord then met Skywalker again, telling him that Grievous was on Utapau and expressing his wish for the young Jedi to lead the [[Battle of Utapau|battle]] against the general. Skywalker was glad to hear that he had the Chancellor's confidence and discussed the Jedi Council's spying assignment with Palpatine, who suggested that the masters did not trust the young man and were plotting to take over the Republic. Skywalker concurred that he felt the Jedi Council was disrespecting the Republic's democracy, but he argued that the Jedi were selfless and therefore very different from the Sith who only thought about themselves. The canny politician thus moved on to indicating his knowledge about Sith arcana, catching Skywalker's intrigue with a [[Tragedy of Darth Plagueis the Wise|story]] about [[Darth Plagueis]]' study of the power to create life and [[Immortality|prevent death]].<ref name="ROTS" />

[[File:AnakinPalpatineSithReveal-ROTS.png|thumb|right|350px|Skywalker wanted to kill the Chancellor upon his revelation that he was the Sith Lord, but he decided to relay the news to the Jedi Council instead.]]
When Skywalker relayed Grievous' whereabouts to the Jedi Council, the masters voted in agreement with Ki-Adi-Mundi's proposal to send Kenobi to Utapau instead. Upon the news that Kenobi was fighting Grievous, Mace Windu sent Skywalker to determine whether Palpatine would lay down his powers upon the general's defeat. Ki-Adi-Mundi stated that the Chancellor must lay down his powers, and Windu said that he sensed the shroud of the dark side surrounding the Chancellor himself, suggesting that the Jedi Council might be forced to "take control of the Senate to ensure a peaceful transition" of power. However, Grand Master Yoda warned that such a line of thought could take them down a "dark place." Plagued by the thought that other Jedi were withholding him from gaining a power he deemed necessary to "save" Padmé Amidala, Skywalker was seduced by the Chancellor, who revealed that he indeed was the remaining Dark Lord of the Sith, Darth Sidious, who had orchestrated the Clone Wars.<ref name="ROTS" />

====Revenge of the Sith====
{{Quote|Emperor ''Palpatine'' rose to power because he made people afraid. He made people fear the ''Jedi''. He called them ''traitors''. He said they used their mystical powers to subvert democracy and peace, to cause war. It worked, possibly because as much as people ''loved'' the Jedi, they also ''hated'' them, on some level. It is hard to look at people who have become their ''best selves''. It reminds you that ''you have not''. The Jedi Order was an enemy of the Empire and needed to be purged&mdash;not just from reality, but from ''thought''. From ''memory''.|Madelin Sun, on the fall of the Jedi|<ref name="CR3">[[Crimson Reign 3|''Crimson Reign'' 3]]</ref>}}
Angered by the revelation, Skywalker initially wanted to kill the Sith Lord but refrained from doing so. The Chancellor concurred that Skywalker must do his duty as a Jedi to report to the Jedi Council. As Mace Windu learned from Skywalker that Palpatine was Darth Sidious, the Jedi Master sensed Skywalker's emotional confusion and ordered him to remain in the Jedi Temple. Windu and several other council members then went to the Chancellor's office with the expectation of arresting the statesman. Invoking the name of the Republic Senate, they demanded that the Chancellor relinquish his executive powers over to the Senate and let the legislative body decide his fate. However, Darth Sidious retorted that he personally was "the Senate" and [[Duel in Palpatine's office|attacked]] the Jedi within the confines of his office. The Sith Lord proved a formidable opponent until Windu deprived him of his [[Darth Sidious' lightsabers|lightsaber]].<ref name="ROTS" />

[[File:Anakin-Makes-A-Choice.png|thumb|left|350px|In his fear and greed, Anakin Skywalker chose darkness&mdash;power to the Sith.]]
The Jedi Master was ready to kill Sidious when Skywalker, frustrated by the Jedi's distrust and anxious about the fate of his wife, barged into the office. Skywalker's request that the Chancellor stand trial was dismissed by Windu, who reasoned that the Sith Lord had control of the Senate and the courts and must be eliminated to prevent the return of the "oppression of the Sith." Skywalker was ultimately lured by Sidious' promise of power and intervened in favor of the Sith, who killed Windu instead. Skywalker surrendered to the Dark Lord, who appointed him as his new Sith apprentice under the name Darth Vader.<ref name="ROTS" />

Vader's first assignment was to [[Operation: Knightfall|massacre]] the Jedi, including [[youngling]]s, in Coruscant's Jedi Temple. The Chancellor then [[Order 66|ordered]] the Republic army to [[Great Jedi Purge|destroy]] the Jedi Order and summoned the Republic's representatives for a [[special session of Congress]]. Addressing the Senate Rotunda, Darth Sidious was applauded as he announced the quelling of "the Jedi rebellion"&mdash;the man was visibly scarred by the Jedi attack and donned a [[Color|red]]<ref name="ROTS" /> Sith robe that showed a portion of his disfigured face<ref name="VE" /> as he dropped the voice and mannerism of a kindly senator and declared all Jedi as traitors of the Republic. He then [[Proclamation of the New Order|proclaimed]] the reorganization of the Republic<ref name="ROTS" /> into the [[Fascism|fascist]] Galactic Empire.<ref name="Galactic Empire">{{Databank|galactic-empire|Galactic Empire}}</ref>

====A New Order====
{{Quote|So this is how liberty dies&mdash;with thunderous applause.|Senator Padmé Amidala|<ref name="ROTS" />}}
[[File:KillingDemocracyIsTotallyMetal.png|thumb|right|360px|After turning Skywalker against the Jedi, Palpatine declared himself Emperor, casting away democracy and freedom and appealing to senators' desires for peace and security.]]
The vast majority of clone troopers heeded Palpatine's command and enacted the genocide of the Jedi Order, compelled by the [[Behavioral modification biochip|programmed chips]] in their heads, with some justifying their murders through the words "good soldiers follow orders."<ref name="Replacements">{{TBB|Replacements}}</ref> With the peoples of the galaxy weary of war<ref name="Propaganda" /> and many hoping for peace no matter the cost<ref name="TSWB" /> after untold billions had died,<ref name="HC 10 A-Z">{{HelmetCollectionCite|10|Databank A-Z}}</ref> Palpatine's proclamation was gladly welcomed by many Senate representatives<ref name="ROTS" /> and the wider galaxy.<ref name="Dawn" /> As Amidala sadly noted in the Senate Chamber, democracy died with a thunderous round of applause. Her husband later went to Mustafar, where he [[Mission to Mustafar|executed]] the Separatist Council and ensured the immediate shutdown of the Separatist Droid Army at the new Emperor's behest.<ref name="ROTS" /> In the Empire's telling of events, the Jedi had been puppeteering the Separatist Alliance<ref name="DV 2017 1">[[Darth Vader (2017) 1|''Darth Vader'' (2017) 1]]</ref> and created the war as part of a plan to supplant<ref name="CR3" /> and overthrow the Republic.<ref name="ROTS" /> Other Imperial narratives, however, would paint the CIS and Jedi as separate.<ref name="Leia">''[[Leia, Princess of Alderaan]]''</ref><ref name="MoF" /> Nonetheless, the public knowledge that Dooku had once been a Jedi helped to sell the idea that the Order in its entirety could have been composed of traitors.<ref name="Propaganda" />

The war was over, and democratic principles were effectively rolled back on the galactic level with Palpatine's personal dictatorship.<ref name="Galactic Empire" /> The Jedi Purge suppressed the possibility of a Jedi rebellion against the [[New Order]]&mdash;though among the handful of survivors were Yoda and Obi-Wan Kenobi, who received help from Senator Organa. Kenobi joined Padmé Amidala as she confronted her husband on Mustafar. The Jedi Master affirmed that he was loyal to the Republic and to democracy, but Vader had been corrupted by his new master's temptation of power and believed the Jedi were in the wrong&mdash;he claimed that he had created his own "new empire" of "peace, freedom, justice, and security." Vader even boasted to his wife that he could overthrow Palpatine so that they may rule the galaxy together, but she rejected the offer, realizing that her husband had indeed led the attack on the Jedi Temple and subverted democracy. Vader was defeated in a [[Duel on Mustafar|duel]] with Kenobi, but he survived to become the [[Emperor]]'s primary enforcer, clad in fearsome armor; Amidala lost her will to live, but not before she gave birth to the twins [[Luke Skywalker|Luke]] and Leia.<ref name="ROTS" />

[[File:AmeddaJediEradicationCeremony-2017DarthVader1.jpg|thumb|left|300px|Marred by warfare, the galaxy of the Empire celebrated the end to the conflict and to the Jedi.]]
The self-anointed Emperor ushered in an [[Imperial Era|era]] of rule by draconian laws,<ref name="TSWB" /> terror, and oppression,<ref name="Rix Road" /> installing an authoritarian military state that was largely controlled by the [[Imperial Navy|Imperial Starfleet]] and armies. He appointed [[Governor-General]]s and [[Moff]]s&mdash;sector governors who commanded planetary governors and were accountable only to the Imperial executive of high-ranking bureaucrats and the Emperor himself, rather than the war-weary citizens.<ref name="TSWB" /> [[Grand Vizier]] Mas Amedda led a [[Celebration marking the eradication of the Jedi Order|celebration]] of "freedom" from the Jedi "Separatist plots" on assassinating Palpatine, and the Emperor converted the Jedi Temple into his own Imperial Palace.<ref name="DV 2017 1" /> People who remembered the Jedi and were unwilling to die for their beliefs remained silent, becoming afraid and apathetic.<ref name="CR3" />

====Liberty's lament====
{{Quote|It is my duty to act in your best interest and it is why I can no longer condone this unjust occupation. They wish to intimidate us, to scare us, but we shall not cower at their acts of oppression.|Avi Singh, condemning the Imperial occupation of Raxus Secundus|<ref name="Common Ground">{{TBB|Common Ground}}</ref>}}
While the Emperor allowed the existence of an [[Imperial Senate]],<ref name="Darth Vader 1">[[Darth Vader (2015) 1|''Darth Vader'' (2015) 1]]</ref> sixty-three senators who had been members of the Delegation of 2,000 were [[Senatorial arrests|arrested]] and charged with treason on the day after the Empire's founding. Senators Mon Mothma and Bail Organa were released, but they found the new Senate disempowered.<ref name="TRF" />

[[File:Imperial-AT-TEs.png|thumb|right|380px|The Empire betrayed its promises and quelled opposition with brutal force, imposing a dictatorship over the ashes of democracy.]]
The Empire began [[Occupation of Raxus Secundus|occupying]] the former Separatist capital world, Raxus Secundus, early in its reign. Ex-Senator [[Avi Singh]] addressed a disgruntled crowd in [[Raxulon plaza square]] against the Imperial presence imposing itself without the people's consent, but Imperial [[All Terrain Tactical Enforcer|tanks]] forcibly cleared the square.<ref name="Common Ground" /> Moff Tarkin was remembered on the Colonies planet Ghorman for forcefully landing his cruiser in [[Palmo Plaza]], [[Tarkin Massacre|massacring]] hundreds of unarmed demonstrators,<ref name="Ever Been" /> and he also oversaw indiscriminate mass arrests and massacre on the former Separatist world Antar 4. Although the Empire censored news reports, the media dubbed it the [[Antar Atrocity]], and Emperor Palpatine distanced himself from the controversy by removing Tarkin from the Gotal homeworld. Tarkin was then assigned to the [[Pacification operations in the Western Reaches|military campaign]] in the [[Western Reaches]] to eliminate remaining [[Separatist holdouts]]. However, the Antar Atrocity also prompted the former Republic Intelligence captain [[Berch Teller]] to wage an [[Berch Teller campaign|insurgency]] against the New Order.<ref name="Tarkin" />

The Empire rescinded generations-old statutes protecting [[Legacy world]]s from resource-exploitation to strip-mine [[doonium]] and [[dolovite]] ores for the battle station over Geonosis. Aside from withholding media reports and stationing the relevant scientists across various other worlds&mdash;including Dr. Erso at the newly evicted [[B'ankor Refuge]] on Coruscant, who was convinced by Krennic that they were working for renewable energy solutions for worlds in need&mdash;authorities ensured the project's secrecy by framing companies with limited mining rights to Legacy worlds in the Western Reaches for illegal arms-trading. When Krennic placed Moff Tarkin in the framing of Zerpen Industries in the autonomous star system [[Salient system|Salient]], near other autonomous systems of the Corporate Sector, the confrontation escalated into [[Salient Campaign|open warfare]] that roused the Imperial Senate. The conflict drew in Krennic's former smuggler [[Has Obitt]], who had been moved by his crew's reactions to the Empire's ecocide of appropriated worlds, and the insurgent Saw Gerrera, whose homeworld of Onderon had been lost to the Empire's control, as well as supporters from other autonomous systems. Tarkin was nevertheless determined to make a harsh example of the Salient system to set the precedent, particularly for governments in the Corporate Sector, that the Empire could revoke autonomy at its pleasure.<ref name="Catalyst" />

[[File:ImperialDooniumRefinery-DD.png|thumb|left|360px|The Empire took resources off its worlds without restraint.]]
Where the Empire applied oppressive force, [[Early rebellion against the Galactic Empire|acts of resistance]] increased too.<ref name="Timelines" /> Even planets that had been allied with the Republic during the war were militarily occupied by the Empire against the will of their populations, setting the cause for localized armed resistance movements.<ref name="JFO">''[[Star Wars Jedi: Fallen Order]]''</ref> After helping Has Obitt extricate the Erso family from Coruscant,<ref name="Catalyst" /> Saw Gerrera eventually led his [[Partisans]] in battling the Imperial [[occupation of Kashyyyk]], where the Wookiees were enslaved as laborers.<ref name="JFO" /> In [[18 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> the farmers who welcomed the former Jedi Ahsoka Tano on the moon [[Raada]] mounted a spontaneous but ill-fated [[Raada Uprising|uprising]] in which Tano revealed her Force powers, attracting the attention of the [[Inquisitorius]]<ref name="Ahsoka">[[Ahsoka (novel)|''Ahsoka'']]</ref>&mdash;the Emperor's secretive dark side servants who hunted and abducted Jedi and Force-sensitives by operating outside and above the Imperial military hierarchy.<ref name="Red Blade">''[[Inquisitor: Rise of the Red Blade]]''</ref> After Organa's resistance movement rescued the survivors of Raada, Tano decided to join the movement as an informant under the codename "[[Fulcrum]]."<ref name="Ahsoka" /> Inquisitors also pursued the shadow lord Maul on the secluded world of [[Janix]], a self-governing democracy with its own law enforcement and criminal underworld.<ref name="Janix">{{Databank|janix|Janix}}</ref>

Cham Syndulla formed the [[Free Ryloth Movement]] as Imperial forces reneged on the Republic's promise to leave their homeworld and exploited its natural resources instead. Senator Orn Free Taa was booed when he told his people to support the Empire in a speech in Lessu, and Cham Syndulla followed by asking his followers to lay down their arms, which received some cheers. However, freedom fighters [[Gobi Glie]] and [[Serin]], along with Cham's daughter, [[Hera Syndulla|Hera]], procured weapons from the defected "[[Clone Force 99|Bad Batch]]" clone [[squad]], wary of a new [[Imperial doonium refinery]]. The Twi'leks were arrested and deemed guilty of treason without trial by the Empire, forcing Cham and [[Eleni Syndulla]] to [[Rescue of Hera Syndulla (Ryloth)|rescue]] their daughter.<ref name="Deal">{{TBB|Devil's Deal}}</ref> Although Ryloth was designated a "free and independent" protectorate by the Empire, the [[Syndulla clan|Syndullas]] waged an [[Ryloth Insurgency|insurgency]] against the occupation, which also exploited the planet for its wealth in spices.<ref name="Lords">''[[Lords of the Sith]]''</ref>

====Replacing the Clone Army====
[[File:HoloDestructionTipocaCity.png|thumb|right|350px|The Senate was shown the military attack on Kamino before the Emperor persuaded it to approve the transition from a clone army to a stormtrooper army.]]
Around the same time, upon Admiral Tarkin's instruction, [[Vice Admiral]] [[Edmon Rampart]]'s Imperial forces [[Attack on Kamino|destroyed]] the Kamino [[cloning]] facilities. When the Senate debated the [[Imperial Defense Recruitment Bill]] thereafter, Rampart explained that the bill would allow the Imperial Military to replace the aging clone trooper veterans inherited from the Republic with soldiers enlisted from Imperial populations. Senator [[Gani Riduli]] of the Banking Clan and the [[Unidentified Commerce Guild senator|Commerce Guild representative]] backed the bill. Speaking in opposition were Senators Riyo Chuchi, Bail Organa, and [[Tynnra Pamlo]]&mdash;the latter represented Taris and criticized the bill as a plan to unnecessarily build a new military, and they successfully forced the bill to be postponed for multiple debate sessions.<ref name="Conspiracy">{{TBB|The Clone Conspiracy}}</ref> The Kaminoan senator Halle Burtoni had been removed from office after learning that Rampart was embezzling funds on the [[Defense Finance Committee]] for his [[Project War-Mantle|stormtrooper program]], and she attempted to live a quiet life on Coruscant after criticizing Kaminoan Prime Minister Lama Su for arrogantly believing that the Empire needed their people's cloning services.<ref name="Truth and Consequences">{{TBB|Truth and Consequences}}</ref>

Senator Chuchi persuaded Burtoni to testify before the Senate about Rampart's misuse of government funding and&mdash;with the help of the Bad Batch and the [[Clone Underground]] movement, whose fellow clones were refused representation in the Senate and were fugitives from Imperial service themselves&mdash;publicly uncovered evidence of [[Edmon Rampart's fleet|Rampart's fleet]] attacking Kamino. However, Emperor Palpatine, who had been absent from most Senate sessions and let Grand Vizier Amedda preside alone, personally appeared in the Senate Chamber. He claimed that Rampart had acted without authorization and convinced the Imperial Senate to pass the bill into law, heralding the age of the Imperial [[stormtrooper]].<ref name="Truth and Consequences" /> Military service recruits trained in the new [[Imperial Academy|Imperial Academies]], which appealed to young people who wanted to travel off-world or otherwise felt a lack of control over their own lives, especially in the Outer Rim.<ref name="Propaganda" />

[[File:Blood_collection_room_weyland.png|thumb|left|380px|The science of medicine was exploited by Imperial experiments on cloned soldiers, unrestrained by the democratic ideals that ended with the war.]]
Shortly afterward, Dr. [[Royce Hemlock]], whose invasive procedures had violated the democratic ideals of the Republic, headed the Imperial [[Advanced Science Division]] and worked on unwilling clone subjects for the Emperor's secret biological experiments<ref name="Hemlock">{{Databank|dr-royce-hemlock|Dr. Royce Hemlock}}</ref> as part of the [[Imperial cloning program]], which also included the Zillo Beast, and [[Project Necromancer]]. After the Bad Batch [[Skirmish on Wayland|scuppered]] Hemlock's [[Mount Tantiss|facility]], Governor Wilhuff Tarkin redirected the relevant funds to [[Project Stardust]].<ref name="Cavalry">{{TBB|The Cavalry Has Arrived}}</ref> Emperor Palpatine promoted Tarkin to [[Grand Moff]] after he defeated the Berch Teller insurgency, which had been disrupting supply stations to Geonosis.<ref name="Tarkin" /> A separate [[Nightswan's campaign|insurgency]] led by "[[Nevil Cygni|Nightswan]]" monitored and sabotaged shipments of doonium, [[iridium]], and other minerals across the Empire, and in the course of countering the insurgency, [[Mitth'raw'nuruodo]]&mdash;an exile from the Unknown Regions' [[Chiss Ascendancy]] who joined the Galactic Empire under the name "Thrawn"&mdash;ascended its ranks as a talented naval officer. During his investigation, [[Commodore]] Thrawn discovered that the Empire was transporting enslaved Wookiees and discussed Nightswan's intentions, including the rebel's belief that the Empire was gathering material for a secret project.<ref name="Thrawn">[[Thrawn (novel)|''Thrawn'']]</ref>

====A populist peace====
{{Quote|Palpatine is a populist. The reason he appeals to worlds like Troithe is because people there see him making real changes in their lives. He cracked down on the corporations. He ended the war. He's promising that the era of out-of-touch politicians and twelfth-generation nobility shaping the galaxy for their own benefit is over.''"<br />"''He's&mdash;''"<br />"''What you're doing is only proving him right.|Lud Marroi and Mon Mothma, who cut herself off from arguing the point|<ref name="MoF" />}}
[[File:StrengthAndObedience-SWPropaganda.jpg|thumb|right|240px|''[[Strength & Obedience]]'' poster glorifying the Empire's military prowess.]]
While day-to-day life proved difficult for the average citizen, government propaganda created a narrative that anyone could become successful: Senator [[Daho Sejan]] proclaimed that the Empire was built on prosperity and security, blaming ongoing issues on alleged dissidents and terrorists hiding among the population, and he called on "loyal" citizens to report the alleged foes among them. Sejan promised that such loyalists would have new opportunities in life as the regime expanded across the stars.<ref name="Jedi: Survivor">''[[Star Wars Jedi: Survivor]]''</ref> The Emperor was popular on the ecumenopolis Troithe, which was suffering a slow industrial decline by the time of the Clone Wars.<ref name="Shadow Fall" /> Senator [[Lud Marroi]] of Troithe told Mon Mothma that Palpatine was an appealing populist who made tangible changes felt by citizens; he was seen to have cracked down on corporations, ended the war, and promised to end the influence of out-of-touch politicians and nepotistic nobility. However, Mothma recognized that power was simply shifting to Palpatine and his cronies instead of to the people. Mothma also identified the Empire as a [[kleptocracy]].<ref name="MoF" />

Indeed, the Empire was kind to the galaxy's wealthy, who were afforded comfortable lifestyles<ref name="SW 2020 46">[[Star Wars (2020) 46|''Star Wars'' (2020) 46]]</ref> at the expense of average working populations, particularly those in the Outer Rim.<ref name="Kempshall" /> On the planet [[Bracca]], the Empire and its allies in the [[Scrapper Guild]] exploited the guild's base of workers, who were given lesser pay and put under more exploitative conditions.<ref name="Jedi Fallen Order">''[[Star Wars Jedi: Fallen Order]]''</ref> All the while, the wealthy established ties with the Empire and its military.<ref name="Kempshall" /> The wealthy would mingle with Imperial officers at parties<ref name="One Year Later">{{Andor|One Year Later}}</ref> and sponsor Imperial efforts, such as the careers of promising officers or the creation of new warships. In return for such sponsorship, the wealthy individual or company could expect beneficial treatment from the government.<ref name="Kempshall" />

[[Arsin Crassus]] and other financiers channeled Separatist accounts to the Empire.<ref name="Aftermath" /> The regime either absorbed the corporate groups that had backed the Separatist Alliance<ref name="CIS DB" /> or permitted them to continue, so long as they pledged themselves to the new government.<ref name="MoF" /> The heavily industrialized former Separatist worlds were a boon to the Empire, which nationalized the means of production as part of [[Imperialization]] and brought substantial weapons equipment into the fold of the Imperial Military.<ref name="Kempshall" /> The InterGalactic Banking Clan feigned loyalty to the Empire, which was blockading their headquarters of Scipio; the corporation secretly funded armed resistance against Imperial rule<ref name="Endless Vigil">''[[Endless Vigil]]''</ref> and was embroiled in violent [[trade wars]] against the Hutt Clan for access to [[Smuggler|smuggling]] routes.<ref name="GE 2">[[Galaxy's Edge 2|''Galaxy's Edge'' 2]]</ref> The new regime took advantage of the Outer Rim hyperlanes that the Republic had secured with the Hutt Cartel during the war, expanding without due regard to the sovereignty of neutral and independent star systems.<ref name="Kempshall" />

====Brutalist base====
{{Quote|Need I remind the honorable members that Palpatine maintained the illusion of the Imperial Senate for nearly twenty years after the Old Republic's fall? A tyrant can make anything seem to be 'the will of the people.'|Senator Leia Organa, reflecting on the Empire's history in the New Republic Senate|<ref name="Bloodline" />}}
[[File:Coronet_City.png|thumb|left|350px|The Empire put to use Corellia's industrial capacity for new Star Destroyers at the expense of the world's workers and environment.]]
The Imperial Senate continued to operate on Coruscant with increased culpability for the regime's schemes.<ref name="Kempshall" /> During the investiture of senators, the [[Oathkeeper]] led the Senate Chamber in a collective oath-taking that pledged "full allegiance" to the Empire.<ref name="Festive">{{Andor|What a Festive Evening}}</ref> Oppositional figures such as Bail Organa and Mon Mothma were allowed to remain through the existing local electoral processes, but senators were monitored by government authorities, and support for benevolent campaigns were warped by advisors and governors to the effect of furthering Imperial oppression and staining senators' reputations: the Senate's provision of "accomodation and food" for [[Wobani]] resulted in the Mid Rim planet becoming a prisoner and labor camp, and an employment bill for the Core World Corellia was twisted into turning the<ref name="Kempshall" /> civilian<ref name="ROUVG" /> Corellian [[shipyard]]s into an offshoot of Kuat Drive Yards that made [[Imperial-class Star Destroyer|Star Destroyers]].<ref name="Kempshall" />

Consequently, Corellia's upper atmosphere was heavily polluted<ref name="Kempshall" /> and the [[bay squid]]s of [[Ugyir River]] became extinct,<ref name="HBA">''[[Hunters: Battle for the Arena]]''</ref> with the [[White Worms]] criminal gang roaming the streets between factories co-owned by Sienar Fleet Systems and the [[Santhe Corporation]]. By [[10 BBY]], Imperial forces were locked in [[trench warfare]] with the [[Mimbanese Liberation Army]] who resisted the Empire's reneging on the Republic's promise of freedom<ref name="Solo Guide" /> and instead exploiting Mimban for hyperbaride.<ref name="Battles" /> At the same time, the [[Cloud-Riders]] were rebellious marauders led by [[Enfys Nest]] who waged war on both the Empire and the [[Five Crime Syndicates]], holding them responsible for atrocities.<ref name="Solo Guide" />

[[File:EmperorsAdvisers-RotJAVA.png|thumb|right|320px|The Emperor used opaque systems of bureaucracy to manage the Empire's many regions.]]
The Imperial Palace, the Imperial Inspectorate HQ that replaced the Republic Center for Military Operations, and the [[COMPNOR arcology|arcology complex]]&mdash;the latter housing the newly founded [[Commission for the Preservation of the New Order]] (COMPNOR), including the [[ISB Central Office]], and the [[Ubiqtorate]] of [[Imperial Intelligence]]&mdash;formed the "supreme triangle" of the Federal District.<ref name="Tarkin" /> Coruscant's skyline was increasingly dominated by Palpatine's favored [[Brutalist (architecture)|brutalist]] aesthetic over the sweeping lines of [[Republic architecture]] around the Senate,<ref name="ROUVG" /> which received additional pressure from the executive. [[Imperial High Command]] requested the Senate to revoke the [[Unknown Alien protocols]], which required all contact with undiscovered sentient species to be investigated on behalf of the legislative body, and rumors that the Emperor was working to revoke the protocols circulated within the military.<ref name="Thrawn" />

The Imperial Starfleet earned a fearsome reputation following its [[Base Gamma One|orbital bombardment]] of the ex-Separatist world [[Haruun Kal]], which put down the [[Bombardment of Haruun Kal|local insurrection]].<ref name="Final Order">{{StoryCite|story=The Final Order|book=From a Certain Point of View: The Empire Strikes Back}}</ref> While the military and loyal administrators' powers increased, senators' decisiveness and willingness to vote and debate decreased, reflected in declining Senate attendance and reinforcing the Senate's reputation as useless whereas governors could seemingly act without the constraint of politics. However, the Imperial leadership recognized the Senate's residual power: Emperor Palpatine allowed some legislation that hindered his plans to pass despite maintaining a veto, and [[Imperial Naval Regulation 132.CAT.ch(22)]] restricted ships from firing on escape pods in response to Senate investigations of possible war crimes.<ref name="Kempshall" />

====Imperialist ignorance====
{{Quote|We made the story. We shaped it. We blew it up. We decided when it was over. With the right ideas planted in the right markets, in the right sequence, we can now weaponize this galactic opinion.|[[Dee Shambo]] of the Empire's Ministry of Enlightenment|<ref name="OYL">{{Andor|One Year Later}}</ref>}}
[[File:ImperiusUnitadaoberTotallex.png|thumb|left|250px|''Imperius Unitada ober Totallex'']]
The scholar [[The Archivist|Madelin Sun]] observed that the Emperor rose to power by making people afraid of the Jedi&mdash;citizens accepted their elected leader's claim that the Jedi, who had powers beyond most people's understanding, were subverters of democracy and peace&mdash;and the Empire went on to eliminate memories of the Jedi.<ref name="CR3" /> History was rewritten. Alternative Republic and Separatist views of the Clone Wars were suppressed and relevant records, including any mention of the Jed, were censored.<ref name="Kempshall" /> While older Imperials remembered the Jedi's alleged coup on Palpatine, Imperial teachings dismissed the Jedi as a marginal cult and emphasised the Emperor's promise of uprooting the corruption and inefficiencies endemic in the Republic, restraining local governments and corporate powers alike and reducing petty crime rates.<ref name="Alphabet" />

COMPNOR was formed from the [[Commission for the Protection of the Republic]] populist propaganda movement and tasked with glorifying the ideals of the New Order, enshrined in the [[Imperial Charter]]. Shortly after its drafting, the Empire's ''[[Imperius Unitada ober Totallex]]'' poster promoted the ideology of "Empire united over all" using brutalist and fascist imagery including a human fist, as the New Order promoted [[xenophobia]] against non-human "[[alien]]s" as well. Post-Imperial [[historian]]s criticized the twilight years of the Republic for being an era of distractions, with citizens embracing template escapist tales of heroes versus villains when attention should have been focused on corruption in governing institutions such as the Senate. COMPNOR's [[Sub-Adult Group]] served as a non-military alternative for young people who did not enlist in the Imperial Academies, training them as loyal administrators for the Imperial bureaucracy.<ref name="Propaganda" />

===Rebellions are built on hope===
====Nothing can come of nothing====
{{Quote|When do we start fighting back?''"<br />"''We have.''"<br />"''By walking away?''"<br />"''We fight to win. That means we lose. And lose and lose and lose&hellip;until we're ready.|Kleya and Luthen|<ref name="MIS">{{Andor|Make It Stop}}</ref>}}
When the Empire was newly founded, Padmé Amidala's [[Naboo Royal Handmaidens|handmaiden]] [[Saché]] had joined [[Naboo planetary legislative assembly|Naboo's legislative assembly]] due to her heroism during the Trade Federation invasion of Naboo and been a favorite to be elected to succeed Sio Bibble as the world's governor, yet democracy was subverted as [[Quarsh Panaka]] was installed as the Moff of Emperor Palpatine's homeworld.<ref name="Shadow" /> Under Imperial rule, Gungan people were systematically evicted and detained and fled to their underwater cities.<ref name="Nexus" /> During an [[Imperial Army]] deployment elsewhere, [[Imperial swamp trooper|mudtroopers]] were ordered to round up and execute any people they found. Among the soldiers was [[Sergeant]] [[Luthen Rael|Lear]], who deserted the army after rescuing a [[Kleya Marki|girl]] from the scene whom he named "Kleya" while he took on the name "Luthen." Whereas the Empire's victims were arbitrary and actions indiscriminate, the man taught Kleya that their hatred and acts of resistance ought to be used effectively. At times, he treated the girl as his daughter.<ref name="MIS" />

On Naboo, Luthen told Kleya to enjoy her desert to keep in mind what they stood to lose and asked if she was sure she wanted to commit to the fight; he told her she had to accept what she was leaving behind and refrained from letting her set off the explosives that he had planted by an Imperial convoy on a bridge.<ref name="MIS" /> Wullf Yularen, who heroically served Anakin Skywalker during the Clone Wars,<ref name="Timelines" /> became a leading member of the [[Imperial Security Bureau]] (ISB), which took on law enforcement powers from local agencies such as Tanivos Divo's Coruscant Security Force; Divo retired during the Imperial Era, and his daughter, [[Andressa Divo]], became a Security Bureau [[agent]] who supported the government's increasingly extreme law enforcement tactics.<ref name="Scum" />

[[File:Tarkin_Doctrine.png|thumb|right|500px|Governor Tarkin oversaw notorious brutalities that were meant to democratize fear.]]
While the Empire quelled localized insurgencies, the regime's brutalities encouraged greater rebellion across star systems.<ref name="TSWB" /> Grand Moff Tarkin oversaw the [[Invasion of Mon Cala]] alongside Darth Vader and the Inquisitors. The Jedi [[Ferren Barr]] thought that announcing to the wider galaxy that Palpatine's enforcers were former Jedi could have undermined the new regime in its infancy, but he believed it was too late given the Emperor's successful lies. Instead, Barr wanted to dramatize the more readily understood concepts of oppression and freedom by provoking the Empire's aggression on Mon Cala into war, believing that it would stir the people of the galaxy into resistance. King Lee-Char heeded Barr's prophecy that the Mon Calamari would be at the center of the struggle for liberty and mobilized [[Raddus]] and Gial Ackbar's forces, but the Imperial war machine quickly conquered Mon Cala.<ref name="DV 2017 14">[[Darth Vader (2017) 14|''Darth Vader'' (2017) 14]]</ref> Consequently, Raddus led an exodus of [[city-ship]]s into space, and the [[Mon Calamari Exodus Fleet|fleet]] eventually became a backbone of an interstellar rebel movement.<ref name="ROUVG" />

When [[Lasan]] resisted the Empire, it [[Siege of Lasan|invaded]] the world in [[7 BBY]]<ref name="Kempshall" /> and nearly wiped out the [[Lasat]] people using [[T-7 ion disruptor rifle]]s. The Imperial Senate banned the use of T-7s,<ref name="Distress">{{Rebels|Droids in Distress}}</ref> which were recalled and destroyed&mdash;Senator Tynnra Pamlo personally saw evidence of the atrocity,<ref name="ROUVG" /> though [[Alexsandr Kallus]], who had ordered the [[Disruptor rifle|disruptors]] to be used against the Lasat, continued as an ISB agent with the authority to override civilian and military orders and commandeer military forces, including Star Destroyers. He was later deployed to the Outer Rim [[agriworld]] [[Lothal]], where a family of rebels named the [[Spectres]] had stationed themselves<ref name="AEYNTK">''[[Star Wars: Absolutely Everything You Need to Know]]''</ref> after [[Raiding missions on Imperial supply ships|raiding Imperial supply ships]] on Ryloth, Christophsis, Kashyyyk, and Toydaria.<ref name="Ghost">{{SW|url=games-apps/star-wars-rebels-ghost-raid|text=''Star Wars Rebels: Ghost Raid''|int=Star Wars Rebels: Ghost Raid|nolive=1}}</ref>

====From the brave, the bold, the battered====
{{Quote|The Senate increasingly resembles a rigged casino. Given that Palpatine always wins the political games, it seems pointless to play unless you can also secure your own prizes at the same time.|Senator [[Daho Sejan]], who was later executed for treason|<ref name="Kempshall" />}}
[[File:Rebels_at_Lothal_Senate_Building.png|thumb|left|350px|The Spectres (foreground) opposed the Imperial takeover of Lothal, which shuttered the world's Old Republic Senate (background).]]
Lothal had been governed from the [[Lothal City Capitol Building|Senate Building]] of the planet's [[Capital City|capital]] until the Imperial [[Takeover of Lothal|occupation]]. A growing government bureaucracy at the [[Imperial Complex (Lothal)|Imperial Complex]] under Governor [[Arihnda Pryce]] supplanted the [[Lothal Senate]] and Governor [[Ryder Azadi]].<ref name="Nexus" /> Azadi supported [[Ephraim Bridger|Ephraim]] and [[Mira Bridger]]'s underground broadcasts to the people of Lothal<ref name="Steal">''[[Adventures in Wild Space: The Steal]]''</ref> until they were arrested by the Empire for "treason,"<ref name="Gathering Forces">{{Rebels|Gathering Forces}}</ref> and Azadi himself was likewise jailed alongside the Bridgers.<ref name="Legacy">{{Rebels|Legacy (episode)}}</ref> Having been appointed by Grand Moff Tarkin to replace Azadi,<ref name="Thrawn" /> Pryce funneled her world's mineral resources to the Empire's new industrial factories that manufactured military equipment.<ref name="Nexus" /> Although the Empire initially compensated farmers who were displaced by the mining with new homes and jobs for positive stories, the authorities eventually took land by force and threat of imprisonment, forcing Lothalites to shantytowns such as [[Lothal re-settlement camp 43]], a slum nicknamed Tarkintown after the Grand Moff.<ref name="Fact File 82">{{FactFile2014|82|LOT 5-6|Tarkintown}}</ref>

The planet Devaron in the Colonies region continued as a representative democracy, with the sparsely populated world's economy supported by remittances from off-world [[Devaronian]]s&mdash;mostly [[Gender|men]]. Women dominated the Devaronian government and other leadership roles, including in businesses and the Senate, though the rule of law continued to enshrine gender equality. The people of Devaron were unhappy with the appointment of a human Imperial governor and tacitly approved a significant number of Devaronians to join the Rebellion, prompting the Empire to interfere in Devaronian remittances.<ref name="Nexus" /> [[Tholoth]] was also a democratic planet in the Colonies, being led by a head executive chair and a legislative senate in a governmental structure similar to that of the Republic's, but it was methodically Imperialized and its cosmopolitan culture of many species, architecture, and languages was stripped of elements that the Empire deemed foreign to the [[Tholothian]] species, becoming a mere resort for [[Imperial officer]]s.<ref name="Unlimited" />

[[File:Tyrant-ESB.png|thumb|right|350px|The Empire had significant pools of resources to blockade and occupy entire worlds.]]
The Expansion Region planet [[Iktotch]] was also a representative democracy. Instead of installing an Imperial governor, the Empire simply imposed a blockade as the [[Iktotchi]] people were largely isolationist, informed by their cautious [[Precognition|foresight]] of Imperial tyranny.<ref name="Nexus" /> Elsewhere in the region, the Empire sought to recruit warriors from Thisspias, where Imperial propaganda insisted on the Republic's decadence. The [[Thisspiasian]]s resisted the Empire, whose governor deposed the Blood Monarch only to provoke more insurrection, with royalists and monarchical abolitionists working together against the Imperial occupation.<ref name="Unlimited" /> The Imperial [[occupation of Castell]] led by the "Burning Moff" [[Ssaria]] was brutal and provoked [[Toren|Papa Toren]], a crime lord who lost a [[Toren's object|trinket]] to Ssaria's plundering.<ref name="Lando 1">[[Lando 1|''Lando'' 1]]</ref>

The semi-nomadic [[Vurk]] people of [[Sembla]] in the Outer Rim had a complex system of personal governance and political participation that was often dismissed and underestimated as primitive. Vurks addressed interpersonal disagreements by trying potential compromises to solve the issue, escalating to the community level and becoming casual rules of behavior. Vurks who disagreed migrated to other Vurk communities and were judged by their reputations, which in turn prompted new discussions on mores and consent, always questioning ethical and moral issues to better understand them. Imperials exploited the Vurks as a cheap labor force, and Vurks on other parts of Sembla were sometimes slow to unite against the Empire due to their culture of witnessing and discussing issues on a case-by-case basis.<ref name="Unlimited" />

====Upping the ante====
{{Quote|Remember that the frontier of the Rebellion is everywhere. And even the smallest act of insurrection pushes our lines forward.|Karis Nemik's manifesto|<ref name="Rix Road" />}}
[[File:Imperial_Senator_Mothma-Andor.png|thumb|left|380px|Democracy was not merely a memory but a hope for Mon Mothma, who continued speaking out for democratic principles in the Imperial Senate until she founded the Rebel Alliance.]]
In [[5 BBY]], Senator Mon Mothma worked with Luthen Rael and Kleya Marki's [[Axis network]] and redirected funding for the nascent rebellion. Unable to continue drawing money from her family accounts, Mothma asked her [[Chandrilan]] [[Bank of Kolma|banker]] friend [[Tay Kolma]] to chair a new "charity" programme. When the oligarch [[Davo Sculdun]] found that Mothma had disappeared 400,000 credits, he pressured the senator to arrange for her thirteen-year-old daughter, [[Leida Mothma]], to [[Wedding of Stekan Sculdun and Leida Mothma|marry]] his [[Stekan Sculdun|son]], in exchange for keeping her funds secure.<ref name="One">{{Andor|One Way Out}}</ref> Axis' massive [[Aldhani heist|heist]] on [[Aldhani]] brought the rebellion 80 million credits, and the Empire introduced the [[Imperial Emergency Act]] and the [[Public Order Resentencing Directive]] (PORD), which would boost Imperial Military and Security Bureau powers to facilitate harsh crackdowns on any act deemed criminal. Senator Mothma decried the directive as a "march towards complete unchallenged authority," which the Senate debated before passing the legislation.<ref name="Nobody">{{Andor|Nobody's Listening!}}</ref>

Among the rebels who took part in the Aldhani heist were [[Cassian Jeron Andor|Cassian Andor]] and [[Karis Nemik]]. The latter appreciated that Andor joined the crew as a mercenary, and he wrote a manifesto, ''[[The Trail of Political Consciousness]]'', which advocated freedom as a "pure" idea that emerged spontaneously against the Empire's fragile tyranny, denouncing it as "the mask of fear."<ref name="Rix Road" /> Although Andor enjoyed a holiday on [[Niamos]] to prevent detection by Imperial authorities, the Empire's reach caught up to the man.<ref name="Announcement" /> A [[Unidentified coastal defender stormtrooper (Niamos)|shoretrooper]] who wanted to exploit the power he held over civilians<ref name="HighlightsAnnouncement">{{SW|url=news/andor-episode-7-announcement-highlights|text=''Andor'' Analyzed: 5 Highlights from Episode 7, "Announcement"}}</ref> arrested Andor on a popular beach because he kept "looking around" and "sweating" as though he had been running. The [[Niamos Courthouse|local courthouse]] sentenced Andor to six years of [[prison]] labor for various spurious charges including "civil disruption," "anti-Imperial speech," "fleeing the scene of anti-Imperial activity," and "attempted damage to Imperial property."<ref name="Announcement" />

====Do you hear the people sing?====
{{Quote|The Empire is a disease that thrives in darkness. It is never more alive than when we sleep. It's easy for the dead to tell you to fight, and maybe it's true, maybe fighting is useless. Perhaps it's too late. But I'll tell you this&hellip; If I could do it again, I'd wake up early and be fighting these bastards&hellip; from the start. Fight the Empire!|Maarva Andor|<ref name="Rix Road" />|audio=MaarvaSpeech.flac}}
Andor was incarcerated at the [[Narkina 5 Imperial Prison Complex]], where prisoners were increasingly brutalized by guards given the passing of PORD while being forced to construct modular parts<ref name="Announcement" />&mdash;they contributed to the highly secretive Project Stardust.<ref name="Rix Road" /> Andor and his fellow inmates realized that prisoners who had completed the sentence were not released but simply moved around to another level of the facility. They organized a [[Narkina 5 prison escape|revolt]] in which [[floor manager]] [[Kino Loy]] encouraged the people to help each other while they escaped.<ref name="One" /> Cassian Andor fled the Empire's unjust criminal justice system only to learn that his mother [[Maarva Carassi Andor]], a prominent figure of civil society on his homeworld [[Ferrix]], had passed away.<ref name="Announcement" />

[[File:Andor_riot.png|thumb|right|380px|Citizens (right) fight for freedom against Imperial oppression.]]
Andor returned home for the [[Maarva's funeral procession|funeral]], which the Imperial authorities allowed to go ahead, having recently installed a [[Ferrix occupation|direct military occupation]] after replacing the [[Consolidated Holdings of Preox-Morlana Corporation|Preox-Morlana corporation's]] civil control of Ferrix's [[Morlani system|star system]]. At the procession, Ferrixians listened to Maarva's recorded speech in which she encouraged her people to fight the "disease" perpetuated by the Imperial "bastards." The strength of feeling for Maarva and the community's freedom was such that the citizens were inspired to spontaneously [[Ferrix Riot|attack]] the surrounding Imperial armed forces. Cassian himself decided to join the Axis network, undermining the Empire.<ref name="Rix Road" />

Luthen Rael also worked with Saw Gerrera even though he considered the Partisan leader an anarchist borne of desperation, while Gerrera dismissed various rebel groupings as "separatist" or "neo-republican"<ref name="Narkina 5">{{Andor|Narkina 5 (episode)}}</ref> and criticised Mothma for being passive.<ref name="Name">{{Rebels|In the Name of the Rebellion}}</ref> In [[4 BBY]], Ferrixians were settling as "toolies" living in a [[mobil-haus]] and working the farms of [[Mina-Rau]] when the agriworld was subject to an sudden Imperial inspection. The Imperials checked for visas and arrested undocumented migrants, and [[Lieutenant]] [[Krole]] abused his position to intimidate and sexually assault residents, spurring a [[Skirmish on Mina-Rau|deadly skirmish]] in which [[Bix Caleen]] and [[Wilmon Paak]] fled with Cassian Andor to join the rebellion.<ref name="Harvest">{{Andor|Harvest}}</ref>

====A new generation====
{{Quote|Some individuals have decided that&mdash;that the worlds of this galaxy need a defense other than the Empire&mdash;a defense against the Empire&mdash;''"<br />"''An ''uprising''. About time!''"<br />"''Don't be so happy about it. Do you realize what that would mean? For us, for the whole galaxy?''"<br />"''Yes, I do. It's still time.|Leia Organa and Amilyn Holdo|<ref name="Leia" />}}
[[File:Leia_and_the_Ghost_crew.png|thumb|left|350px|Future senator Leia Organa used her privilege as a princess to run mercy missions that also shored up the rebel movement.]]
By the time the [[Princess]] of Alderaan, Leia Organa, joined the Empire's [[Apprentice Legislature]] in 4 BBY, only forty worlds bothered sending junior representatives to the program given the declining influence of the Senate, but Princess Leia found allies such as [[Harp Allor]] of Chandrila and [[Amilyn Holdo]] of [[Gatalenta]]. The princess had been working as her father's aide in the Senate and learnt from her mother, [[Alderaanian monarchy|Queen]] [[Breha Organa]],<ref name="Leia" /> who not only raised her but taught other young people such as [[Evaan Verlaine]] as Alderaan's [[Minister of Education]].<ref name="Breha DB">{{Databank|queen-breha-organa|Queen Breha Organa}}</ref> The young legislator discovered her parents' underground democracy movement when she found Bail Organa's [[Crait outpost|rebel base]] on [[Crait]]. She committed to humanitarian diplomacy, including a visit to Naboo's moon of [[Onoam]] to deliver safety equipment to striking [[Onoam mine]]rs. The miners told Leia Organa that they could not accept her safety equipment as their Imperial overseers would simply take them to sell for personal profit. Organa met with Moff Panaka and Queen [[Dalné]] to discuss the issue, and Panaka was assassinated by Saw Gerrera's Partisans immediately afterward. Organa was surprised but hoped that Dalné could serve as Naboo's true queen for a few days or weeks while the Empire attempted to re-establish authority.<ref name="Leia" />

The assassination of Panaka was a source of division between Gerrera and various power players of the burgeoning rebel movement, including Bail Organa, who hoped that the Moff might have been open to working alongside them in the future and believed that the Partisans needed to be corralled. After finding that Empire had forcibly displacement of [[Arreyel]]'s entire population to build factories and collectively punished [[Chasmeene]] by poisoning the planet's farmland, Princess Leia entrusted the information with Senator Mon Mothma, her future mentor. Leia later personally [[Evacuation of Paucris Major|evacuated]] a rebel base alongside Amilyn Holdo and eventually decided to join her parents' anti-Imperial alliance.<ref name="Leia" /> She personally delivered three [[Sphyrna-class Hammerhead corvette|Hammerhead corvettes]] to Imperial-occupied Lothal under the guise of transporting relief aid. The corvettes were instead "stolen" by the local rebels to bolster [[Phoenix Cell]].<ref name="APOL">{{Rebels|A Princess on Lothal}}</ref>

====Lighting the spark====
{{Quote|All I sense is evil. Why would they burn Tarkintown?"''<br />''"To spread fear, make us afraid. The people that lived here just got caught in the middle."''<br />''"This happened because of us?"''<br />''"There's a cost for any action we take now, Ezra.|Ezra Bridger and Kanan Jarrus|<ref name="SOL">''[[Star Wars Rebels: The Siege of Lothal]]''</ref>}}
[[File:Rebel_fleet_Mustafar.png|thumb|right|350px|Rebel action over Mustafar killed the Grand Inquisitor and humiliated Grand Moff Tarkin but also attracted Darth Vader's personal attention.]]
The Spectres' harrying of Imperial forces and supplies to Lothalites in the capital and Tarkintown caught the attention of Agent Kallus.<ref name="Spark">''[[Star Wars Rebels: Spark of Rebellion]]''</ref> When the Spectres planned to broadcast a call to rebellion on Lothal, their Jedi leader, [[Kanan Jarrus]], was [[Raid on the communications tower|captured]] by Grand Moff Tarkin and [[the Grand Inquisitor]]. Nevertheless, Jarrus' pupil, [[Ezra Bridger]], made his own [[Ezra's broadcast|broadcast]] that inspired his fellow [[Lothalite]]s and people in the surrounding systems to resist the Empire.<ref name="Call">{{Rebels|Call to Action}}</ref> Ezra's call reached his parents, Ephraim and Mira, who had been jailed by the Empire, and they [[Revolt in an Imperial prison|rallied others]] to break out of their prison&mdash;Ephraim and Mira were killed by the Empire, but rebels and their sympathizers, including Lothal's former governor Azadi, were free thanks to them, and Ezra continued to carry their spirit.<ref name="Legacy">{{Rebels|Legacy (episode)}}</ref>

To rescue Kanan Jarrus, Ezra and the Spectres [[Action at Mustafar|infiltrated]] [[Sovereign|Tarkin's Star Destroyer]] over Mustafar, where Jarrus had been interrogated for information about the rebellion and the informant known as "Fulcrum."<ref name="Mustafar DB">{{Databank|mustafar|Mustafar}}</ref> They destroyed the Grand Moff's flagship and successfully extracted Jarrus with the help of Phoenix Cell. The Grand Inquisitor let himself die rather than suffer for his failures,<ref name="Fire Across the Galaxy">{{Rebels|Fire Across the Galaxy}}</ref> and the defeat embarassed Tarkin.<ref name="Epic Battles">''[[Star Wars Rebels: Visual Guide: Epic Battles]]''</ref> Although the action at Mustafar marked one of the earliest rebel victories in the [[Galactic Civil War]] against the Empire,<ref name="Mustafar DB" /> it prompted Emperor Palpatine to send Darth Vader to personally handle the Lothal crisis.<ref name="Fire Across the Galaxy" />

With Darth Vader in the lead, the Empire tightened control over Lothal beginning with the torching of Tarkintown, destroying the refuge and either imprisoning or killing the people who lived there in order to force the Jedi to show themselves.<ref name="SOL" /> At the same time, Mon Mothma was cautious that her and Bail Organa's rebel network had revealed their sector fleet's naval capacity to Imperial authorities.<ref name="TRF" /> A [[Imperial blockade of Lothal|military lockdown]] of Lothal was subsequently enforced by [[Grand Admiral]] Thrawn and his [[Seventh Fleet (Galactic Empire)|Seventh Fleet]] at Governor Pryce's request, the two Imperials having previously worked together to defeat Nightswan.<ref name="Thrawn" /> Ezra Bridger vowed to protect his loved ones from the Empire, and he received advice from Yoda not to fixate on fighting to win the war but to think about choosing how to win.<ref name="Shroud" />

====Today Ghorman, tomorrow&mdash;====
{{Quote|The death of truth is the ultimate victory of evil ''[&hellip;]'' what happened yesterday on Ghorman was unprovoked genocide! Yes! Genocide! And that truth has been exiled from this chamber! And the monster screaming the loudest? The monster we've helped create? The monster who will come for us all soon enough is Emperor Palpatine!|Senator Mon Mothma, addressing the Imperial Senate|<ref name="Welcome">{{Andor|Welcome to the Rebellion}}</ref>}}
[[File:GhormanFrontHeadquarters-BeenToGorman.jpg|thumb|right|350px|The Ghorman Front grew increasingly impatient to resist the Empire as Imperial oppression grew increasingly brazen.]]
As the [[Ghorman Front]] resisted the Imperial regime in 5 BBY,<ref name="The Eye">{{Andor|The Eye}}</ref> [[Imperial Ruling Council]] officials [[Sly Moore]] and [[Ars Dangor]] cut off the shipping lanes to Ghorman, which Senator Mothma believed face starvation.<ref name="Aldhani">{{Andor|Aldhani (episode)}}</ref> She backed fellow Senator [[Dhow]] in proposing to send a fact-finding mission to "prove" that the [[Ghorman (people)|Ghorman]] people were simply "requesting their basic rights" but received scant attention from her colleagues, who had called for Imperial decree to handle the situation.<ref name="The Eye" /> In 4 BBY, [[Director of Advanced Weapons Research|Director]] Krennic of an "energy project"<ref name="OYL" />&mdash;Project Stardust<ref name="ROUVG" />&mdash;enlisted select officers to [[Ghorman project|secure]] Ghorman for the mineral [[kalkite]], necessary for coating reaction lenses, at the cost of destabilizing the planet core. The [[Ministry of Enlightenment]]'s propaganda "weaponized" galactic opinion against the Ghor while the Empire built an [[Palmo armory|armory]] in the city of [[Palmo]],<ref name="OYL" /> prompting protests against the over-shadowing of the [[Monument to the Fallen]], which commemorated the Tarkin Massacre of fifteen years ago.<ref name="Ever Been" />

[[ISB Supervisor]] [[Dedra Meero]] oversaw Imperial efforts to [[Suppression of Ghorman|secretly incite]] increasingly violent Ghorman resistance as pretext for a crackdown that would allow the Empire to mine kalkite. To that end, Meero's partner, the bureaucrat [[Syril Karn]], was posted on Ghorman to work with the Ghorman Front. The front received help from the Palmo city councillor [[Carro Rylanz]], despite his customary calls for peaceful resistance, and the Axis network.<ref name="Festive" /> As curfews were imposed on Ghorman, Imperial journalists reported headily on "insurgents" and "terrorists" against "Imperial norms" while Ghorman rebels suffered from disunity over whether to adopt more violent forms of resistance&mdash;during an argument, [[Lezine]] stressed that they must not let their collective identity as Ghor to be corrupted by the Empire.<ref name="Messenger">{{Andor|Messenger}}</ref> When Mon Mothma tried to convince fellow senators to back her legislative efforts against the PORD in [[3 BBY]], even Senator [[Dasi Oran]] of Ghorman refused to support her, citing his world's vulnerability as a single export economy&mdash;requiring the import of everything beside the twill that made Ghorman prosperous&mdash;to further restrictions that could be imposed in retaliation by Imperial authorities.<ref name="Ever Been" />

In [[2 BBY]],<ref name="Messenger" /> General [[Jan Dodonna]]'s [[Massassi Group]] had set up base in a [[Great Temple|hidden fortress]] on the Outer Rim moon [[Yavin 4]],<ref name="ROUVG" /> with a growing community of rebels<ref name="Force healer">{{Databank|force-healer|Force healer}}</ref> including General [[Davits Draven]], Captain Cassian Andor, Bix Caleen, Wilmon Paak, [[Vel Sartha]],<ref name="Messenger" /> and [[Ruescott Melshi]].<ref name="Welcome" /> Andor's clarity of feeling inspired a [[Unidentified Force healer|healer]]<ref name="Messenger" /> at the base who had faith in the Force and comforted [[Rebel trooper|rebel troops]] and [[Rebel Alliance Starfighter Corps|pilots]].<ref name="Force healer" /> Based on intelligence from Hondo Ohnaka, the Spectres [[Mission to Reklam Station|stole]] five [[BTL-A4 Y-wing assault starfighter/bomber|Y-wings]] from the Empire's [[Reklam Station]] [[salvage yard]] and gave them to Dodonna's [[Gold Squadron (Rebel Alliance)|Gold Squadron]].<ref name="SIS">''[[Star Wars Rebels: Steps Into Shadow]]''</ref>

[[File:GhormanMassacre-AndorRebelionDigitalZine.png|thumb|left|350px|Protesters gathered in Palmo Plaza, unaware of the carnage that was to come.]]
The Empire committed the [[Ghorman Massacre]] in that year.<ref name="Timelines" /> Rumors of Imperial mining rigs landing in the Ghorman countryside spread to Palmo and barricades were set up in the city's plaza, inviting Ghorman civilians&mdash;a few armed with blasters, with [[Dilan|one]] distributing [[flame jar]]s and another breaking up the ground for chunks of throwable debris, but mostly unarmed&mdash;to rally there after calling for a general strike. Inexperienced [[Imperial security trooper]]s from off-world were then sent from Palmo Plaza's [[Imperial office complex]] in riot gear to push through the crowd to reach the central monument. When Supervisor Meero was confronted by Syril Karn regarding the Empire's true intentions with Ghorman, she rebutted that he had taken his promotions gladly and attempted to justify her involvement by claiming that it had been developed for years without them. She was nevertheless responsible for relaying the order from Coruscant to proceed: before the riot troops could reach the monument, a hidden [[Unidentified Imperial sniper|Imperial sniper]] shot one of them, and the panicked troops fired their blasters at the protesters. As the Ghor retaliated, stormtroopers encircling the plaza began firing upon them as well, followed by [[KX-series security droid]]s that cleared the plaza by chasing down and tossing demonstrators, killing many. The massacre then spilled into the surrounding streets. Surviving rebels desperately broadcasted raw accounts of the atrocity on open channels.<ref name="WAY">{{Andor|Who Are You?}}</ref> Thousands were slaughtered.<ref name="Kempshall" />

[[File:SenateBldgReflection-WttR.png|thumb|right|360px|A reflection of the Imperial Senate Building on Coruscant on the night of the Ghorman Massacre]]
Imperial state media portrayed the event as a failed uprising and hailed the fallen Imperial personnel as heroes who died for peace and security. Senator Oran was soon [[Arrest of Dasi Oran|arrested]] without charge by stormtroopers before other senators in the Senate Building.<ref name="Welcome" /> The massacre was the last straw for Mon Mothma.<ref name="ROUVG" /> She agreed with Bail Organa that while he would brave staying in the Imperial Senate, she would publicly denounce the massacre as genocide and then lead the rebels on Yavin 4. During the next day's session of the Imperial Senate, which the [[Oathkeeper]] convened early and only called senators loyal to the regime to speak, several representatives commemorated Imperial "martyrs" who died on Ghorman. When [[Senior Senator]] [[Erveen]] was called, she yielded her time to Senior Senator Organa to make a Point of Order, invoking [[Article 17-252]] from the Emperor's Council to yield in turn to Senior Senator Mothma. She made a speech denouncing the Ghorman Massacre and the distortion of truth within the Senate&mdash;Emperor Palpatine was the "monster" who would destroy them all. Mothma was then [[Escape from the Senate|extracted]] from the Senate by the Axis network, including Cassian Andor. Although Andor wanted to leave the Rebellion to live with his lover Bix Caleen, she let him go, leaving him on Yavin 4 so that he may dedicate himself to the rebel cause.<ref name="Welcome" />

====Fire across the galaxy====
{{Quote|I've been called a traitor for speaking out against a corrupt Galactic Senate, a Senate manipulated by the sinister tactics of the Emperor. For too long, I've watched the heavy hand of the Empire strangle our liberties, stifling our freedoms in the name of ensuring our safety. No longer. Despite Imperial threats, despite the Emperor himself, I have no fear as I take new action. For I am not alone. Beginning today, we stand together as allies. I hereby resign from the Senate to fight for you. Not from the distant halls of politics, but from the front lines. We will not rest until we bring an end to the Empire, until we restore our Republic. Are you with me?|Mon Mothma, assembling the Rebel Alliance|<ref name="Secret Cargo">{{Rebels|Secret Cargo}}</ref>}}
[[File:Secret_Cargo.jpg|thumb|left|330px|Mon Mothma formally founded the Rebellion in a broadcast from the ''Ghost'' after fleeing from Coruscant in the aftermath of the Ghorman Massacre.]]
Branded a traitor and [[Operation Handoff|pursued]] by Thrawn's Seventh Fleet and [[TIE/d "Defender" Multi-Role Starfighter|TIE Defender]] prototype, Mon Mothma fled Coruscant with her aide [[Erskin Semaj]]. They were escorted by Gold Squadron and the Spectres with their home ship, the ''[[Ghost]]'', to the planet [[Dantooine]]. Mothma then announced her resignation from the Senate in a [[Declaration of the Rebel Alliance|speech broadcast on the HoloNet]], expressing her belief that she had exhausted all avenues of defending people's rights and freedoms from within the Senate. She accused Emperor Palpatine for ordering the attack on the Ghormans and called on the people of the galaxy to join her in battle against the "tyranny" of the Imperial regime and "restore our Republic." Disparate rebel groups heeded her call and formally formed the [[Alliance to Restore the Republic]],<ref name="Secret Cargo" /> aiming to restore galactic democracy.<ref name="Rebel Alliance">{{Databank|rebel-alliance|Rebel Alliance}}</ref> Mothma encouraged debate and disagreement in the [[Alliance Cabinet|cabinet]] of the [[Alliance Civil Government]], which partook in [[Alliance High Command]] meetings alongside military leaders on Yavin 4.<ref name="ROUVG" />

The movement was beset with rifts, particularly with Saw Gerrera and his Partisans.<ref name="TRF" /> The Empire's Tanivos Divo classed Gerrera as a "terrorist" who violated the [[Alderaan Convention]] against [[Torture|torturing]] prisoners.<ref name="Scum" /> When Saw Gerrera and the Spectres [[Mission to investigate Geonosis|confirmed]] that the Empire had committed [[Sterilization of Geonosis|genocide]] on the Geonosians, the rebels recovered images of the poison canisters used. Bail Organa thought that the Senate would not be convinced but the evidence could rally more star systems to the rebel cause,<ref name="Ghosts">{{Rebels|Ghosts of Geonosis}}</ref> and Agent Kallus had begun to sympathize with the rebel cause following a [[Ambush on an Imperial construction module|run-in]] over Geonosis with the Spectres' Lasat member, [[Garazeb Orrelios]],<ref name="THO">{{Rebels|The Honorable Ones}}</ref> a year prior.<ref name="Timelines" /> The Alliance later [[Resolution of Censure Condemning Saw Gerrera of the Partisans|severed ties]] with Gerrera's group for their brutal tactics and targeting of civilians, which Mothma deemed to violate the principles of the [[Ruusan Armistice]] and were unjustified even under a state of war.<ref name="TRF" /> Nevertheless, Gerrera later pressed members of the Spectres to help him [[Mission to Faos Station|investigate]] an Imperial shipment of a giant kyber crystal, with the rebels freeing technicians who suggested that the cargo came from the [[Jedha system]].<ref name="Name" />

[[File:Thrawns_Office_AIM.png|thumb|right|350px|Thrawn studied artwork associated with the Spectres to guage their motives and vulnerabilities.]]
Before Phoenix Cell's [[Chopper Base|base]] on [[Atollon]] was [[Battle of Atollon|destroyed]] by Thrawn's fleet<ref name="Zero Hour">{{Rebels|Zero Hour}}</ref> in 2 BBY,<ref name="Timelines" /> which necessitated the survivors' retreat to Yavin 4,<ref name="Zero Hour" /> the Spectres had helped the Rebellion procure a [[Prototype B6|prototype]] "blockade buster" starfighter [[Project Shantipole|designed]] by the master engineer [[Quarrie]]<ref name="TRF" /> in 4 BBY.<ref name="Timelines" /> Quarrie later joined the crew of Admiral Ackbar's rebel [[flagship]], the [[MC80A Home One Type Heavy Star Cruiser|star cruiser]] ''Home One'', alongside the [[Freemakers]] family of mechanics.<ref name="Droidography">''[[Star Wars: Droidography]]''</ref> Based on the prototype, Senator Bail Organa persuaded [[Slayn & Korpil]] to manufacture the powerful [[A/SF-01 B-wing starfighter|B-wing starfighter]] for the rebel cause.<ref name="TRF" /> Princess Leia was elected as Alderaan's senator after her father stepped down, becoming the legislature's youngest member while continuing her humanitarian efforts.<ref name="ANHPSF">''[[A New Hope: The Princess, the Scoundrel, and the Farm Boy]]''</ref>

In [[1 BBY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> the Spectres joined their member [[Sabine Wren]]'s efforts to defend her people on [[Occupation of Mandalore|Imperial-occupied]] Mandalore.<ref name="Heroes of Mandalore">''[[Star Wars Rebels: Heroes of Mandalore]]''</ref> Wren noted that the popularity of [[Mandalorian cubism]] and its [[Mandalorian mural|murals]] of ancient Mandalorian crusaders killing Jedi resurfaced during the Clone Wars, and she disapproved of how the imagery had been used to promote violence despite being initially intended to condemn war.<ref name="Sketchbook">''[[Sabine My Rebel Sketchbook]]''</ref> As both a rebel and a Mandalorian of [[Clan Wren]], Wren [[Skirmish on Krownest|defended]] her family, helped depose Imperial Viceroy [[Gar Saxon]] and his brother [[Tiber Saxon]], and, alongside [[Lady]] [[Bo-Katan Kryze]], [[Mission to Mandalore|destroyed]] the [[Arc Pulse Generator|anti-Mandalorian weapon]] that Wren herself had developed during her time at the [[Imperial Academy of Mandalore|Imperial Academy]].<ref name="Heroes of Mandalore" />

The Rebel Alliance then sanctioned General Hera Syndulla of the Spectres to lead an [[Attack on Lothal]] to destroy Thrawn's TIE Defender program, but the rebels were defeated. With Director Krennic lobbying for more funding to be diverted to Project Stardust, Grand Admiral Thrawn left Lothal to persuade the Emperor on Coruscant to maintain funding for his TIE Defender program. Meanwhile, the Spectres persisted in battling the occupation forces led by Governor Pryce on their homeworld, which [[Rescue of Hera Syndulla (Lothal)|compromised]] the TIE program.<ref name="Jedi Night">{{Rebels|Jedi Night}}</ref> The Emperor personally tried to lure Ezra Bridger to help him breach into the mystical [[World Between Worlds]] and Thrawn returned to take charge of Lothal, but the rebels successfully [[Liberation of Lothal|liberated]] the planet in a final battle in which Thrawn was exiled to [[Peridea|parts unknown]].<ref name="FRF">{{Rebels|Family Reunion – and Farewell}}</ref>

===Revolutions are built on love===
====Looking up to Stardust====
{{Quote|You can stand to see the Imperial flag reign across the galaxy?''"<br />"''It's not a problem if you don't look up.|Saw Gerrera and Jyn Erso|<ref name="Rogue">[[Rogue One: A Star Wars Story (novelization)|''Rogue One: A Star Wars Story'' novelization]]</ref>}}
[[File:Jedha_insurgency.png|thumb|left|350px|Saw Gerrera's Partisans battled Imperial forces in Jedha City.]]
Around the same year,<ref name="Timelines" /> the Empire took direct control of HoloNet News, but its outgoing editor-in-chief asked the reporter [[Calliope Drouth]] to secretly help the growing rebel movement via the recording droid [[Zox]]. At the [[Imperial Ball]], Drouth learnt from Krennic's subordinates of the Empire's interest in crystals on [[Jedha]], which the reporter relayed via hand signals.<ref name="SWI170">{{InsiderCite|170|The Voice of the Empire}}</ref> Indeed, the Mid Rim moon Jedha was a pilgrims' holy site from which the Empire extracted kyber crystals amidst an [[Jedha insurgency|insurgency]] by Saw Gerrera. The Partisans attacked shipments of kyber, which were transported to the Outer Rim planet [[Eadu]] for refinement at Dr. Galen Erso's [[Eadu Energy Conversion Laboratory|laboratory]].<ref name="ROUVG" /> Against the urging of Alliance High Command to stop escalating,<ref name="JKE">{{Andor|Jedha, Kyber, Erso}}</ref> Gerrera's rebel cell turned the [[Jedha City|Holy City]] into a warzone, which orphaned increasingly more children. Realizing that the Empire was willing to bear hundreds of stormtrooper casualties and lost kyber shipments, two [[Guardians of the Whills]] who had supported the insurgency&mdash;[[Chirrut Îmwe]] and [[Baze Malbus]]&mdash;led a march of orphans to the shuttle that the Partisans had planned to use as a bomb for the [[Dauntless (Imperial I-class)|Star Destroyer]] that had newly parked over the city, forcing the rebels to abandon the plot.<ref name="Guardians">[[Guardians of the Whills (novel)|''Guardians of the Whills'']]</ref>

In her obsession with hunting Axis, Dedra Meero had been collating information about Imperial activities, including Ghorman kalkite, Jedha kyber, and Dr. Galen Erso in relation to a top-secret project. [[Lonni Jung]], Luthen Rael's spy in the ISB who had been wanting to keep his [[Lonni Jung's wife|wife]] and [[Lonni Jung's daughter|daughter]] safe, discovered the project in Meero's files and revealed the information to Rael in the Imperial capital in 1 BBY. Although the ISB tried to stop the leak and finally foiled Rael's front, Kleya Marki ensured she honored her mentor and their rebellious cause. She prevented the Empire from learning more about their network,<ref name="MIS" /> passed on the information to Cassian Andor, and reached Yavin 4 to join the wider Rebellion alive herself. Assembling short of their required quorum for an agreement, Alliance High Command was initially reluctant to believe Captain Andor's claims when he relayed the information, but he was allowed to meet with [[Tivik]], his contact with Saw Gerrera's group.<ref name="JKE" /> Tivik [[Mission to the Ring of Kafrene|confirmed]] that the Empire was building a "planet killer" based on a message sent by Dr. Erso.<ref name="Rogue" />

[[File:DestructionofJedhaCity-RO.png|thumb|right|350px|The Empire's weapon of terror was unleashed on Jedha City; among the last reports to the Senate was a "mining accident" on Jedha.]]
Nineteen years after the Empire's founding, the regime completed its crowning weapon of terror&mdash;the [[DS-1 Orbital Battle Station|Death Star]]. After undermining Director Krennic's position for Project Stardust's delays and its spate of information leaks, Grand Moff Tarkin tasked the [[superweapon]] with [[Destruction of Jedha City|test-firing]] upon Jedha City, killing the Holy City's residents and pilgrims and shattering the moon. The blast forced the Partisans stationed near the [[city]] to evacuate with their [[Cavern Angels]] squadron, and Saw Gerrera perished in the blast, but not before he received Dr. Erso's message, addressed to his "stardust"&mdash;his daughter, [[Jyn Erso]]. Living in the hope of helping her and the galaxy, Galen had planted a secret [[Thermal exhaust port|weakpoint]] in the Death Star. Working with Cassian Andor, Jyn decided to help the Rebellion. Although the Alliance had confirmation of the Empire's "ultimate weapon," the Imperial Senate was told that Jedha City was destroyed in a mining accident to prevent defections. Alliance High Command agreed to send Captain Andor and Jyn to [[Operation Fracture|capture]] Galen for a Senate hearing to expose the Empire's secret, but Galen was killed in action.<ref name="Rogue" />

The rebel leadership subsequently failed to agree on whether the Alliance should [[Battle of Scarif|infiltrate]] the [[Imperial Center of Military Research|Imperial research installation]] on [[Scarif]] to secure the Death Star plans directly. Jyn Erso reframed the debate on "what chance" the Rebellion had to "what choice" they had,<ref name="Rogue" /> but senators such as Tynnra Pamlo of Taris refused to let the rebels escalate the war against the Empire out of fear for their people.
