After arguing with Mon Mothma, Senator Pamlo traveled to Coruscant to publicly decry the Death Star, resign her office, and urge the Rebellion to disband.<ref name="ROUVG" /> Nevertheless, Erso's intervention had inspired a group of volunteer rebels to infiltrate Scarif in defiance of High Command. Led by Erso and Cassian Andor, they left Yavin 4 under the code name [[Rogue One]]. They were later supported by Admiral [[Raddus]]' [[Rebel Alliance Navy|Alliance Fleet]], beginning open warfare against the Empire. While Director Krennic led a frantic defense of Scarif's base, Grand Moff Tarkin took charge of the Death Star to destroy the base and its entire vicinity of rebels and Imperials. All of Rogue One died in action, as did much of Raddus' fleet, but the plans were successfully transferred to the [[Tantive IV|diplomatic cruiser]] of Senator Leia Organa as it fled the scene of battle.<ref name="Rogue" /> Later, a rebel propaganda [[holovid]] featuring Jyn Erso prompted [[Chass na Chadic]] to fly with the Cavern Angels and eventually join the Rebel Alliance, and the rebel hero's sacrifice remained a lasting inspiration for the pilot.<ref name="Alphabet" />

====Crucible====
{{Quote|The more you tighten your grip, Tarkin, the more star systems will slip through your fingers.|Princess Leia Organa, aboard the Death Star|audio=LeiaSlipThroughYourFingers.flac|<ref name="ANH" />}}
[[File:Motti_and_Tagge.jpg|thumb|left|380px|The Emperor dispensed entirely with the guise of democracy and formally instituted direct rule by regional governors.]]
Despite Senator Organa's legal protection as a diplomat, Darth Vader traced and [[Secret mission to Tatooine|attacked]] her cruiser and captured her for interrogation after realizing that the battle station plans had been sent to Tatooine. When Imperial officer [[Daine Jir]] warned Vader that news of Leia Organa's detainment could "generate sympathy for the Rebellion in the Senate," the Sith Lord ordered that her ship be filed as accidentally destroyed. Meanwhile, the astromech R2-D2, accompanied by C-3PO, endured the Tatooine heat and enslavement by local [[Jawa]] scavengers<ref name="ANH">{{Film|IV}}</ref> to continue Rogue One's mission, carrying the galaxy's hope in the form of the Death Star plans.<ref name="Rogue" /> R2-D2 convinced [[R5-D4]] to help the Rebellion by helping him be sold to [[Lars family|local farmers]],<ref name="The Red One">{{StoryCite|story=The Red One|book=From a Certain Point of View}}</ref> and the rebel astromech, joined by the farmboy Luke Skywalker, delivered Leia's plea from her father Bail to Obi-Wan Kenobi for the Jedi to help the Rebellion.<ref name="ANH" />

Soon afterward,<ref name="ANH" /> Emperor Palpatine formally [[Dissolution of the Imperial Senate|dissolved]] the Imperial Senate and transferred direct control of Imperial space to his regional governors.<ref name="Galactic Empire" /> Grand Moff Tarkin declared the Senate's dissolution as the removal of the last remnant of the Republic when the [[Joint Chiefs of the Imperial Military]] met aboard the Death Star, which included leaders such as [[Chief of the Imperial Army|Chief]] [[Cassio Tagge]] of the Imperial Army, [[Colonel]] Yularen of the ISB, and Darth Vader.<ref name="ANH" /> The Empire had become a [[stratocracy]]<ref name="ROUVG" /> under the [[Tarkin Doctrine]] of military governorship<ref name="Tarkin" /> ruling by fear.<ref name="Dawn" /> Tagge was shocked by the news of the Senate's dissolution, believing that the legislature, though troublesome, involved bureaucratic hurdles that were necessary to deter dissent.<ref name="Operational">{{StoryCite|story=Fully Operational (short story)|book=From a Certain Point of View}}</ref>

Bail and Leia Organa had been monitored by Andressa Divo and the ISB, which found evidence of the [[Alderaanian]]s' links to the Rebellion.<ref name="Scum" /> With Princess Leia detained aboard the new battle station as a rebel, Tarkin demonstrated its full [[Mk I Superlaser|firepower]] on Alderaan,<ref name="ANH" /> where billions of beings&mdash;including Bail and Breha Organa<ref name="Contingency">{{StoryCite|story=Contingency Plan|book=From a Certain Point of View}}</ref> and Tanivos Divo<ref name="Scum" />&mdash;were killed as the world [[Destruction of Alderaan|exploded]]. Tarkin believed that a galaxy-wide fear of the Death Star would silence any dissent.<ref name="ANH" />

[[File:Star_Wars_Marvel_2015_Alex_Ross_Textless.jpg|thumb|right|290px|Guided by figures of the past and leaders of their own making, a younger generation arose to stop Imperial tyranny and restore the Republic.]]
When Princess Leia [[Rescue of Princess Leia|escaped]] with the help of Obi-Wan Kenobi and his crew to Yavin 4, the station pursued them, and rebel pilots prepared a daring assault on the Death Star based on their stolen schematics.<ref name="ANH" /> Mon Mothma decided to travel to Coruscant to surrender to the Empire in the event of Yavin 4's annihilation, willing to publicly disavow the Alliance and her life's work in the hope of sparing other worlds from incuring the Empire's wrath.<ref name="Contingency" /> Instead, at the [[Battle of Yavin]], the Death Star was destroyed by rebel pilot Luke Skywalker with the Force as his ally.<ref name="TSWB" /> Mothma contemplated that as the news of hope spread throughout the galaxy, more young rebels who did not know democracy, regret, or a lover's kiss would rally to the revolution and die by her command.<ref name="Contingency" />

====Remembering Alderaan====
{{Quote|You can never undo the wrong, but you can always do right.|Markona|<ref name="SW 2015 60">[[Star Wars (2015) 60|''Star Wars'' (2015) 60]]</ref>}}
Princess Leia later worked with fellow Alderaanian Evaan Verlaine to unite [[Mission to rescue the survivors of Alderaan|survivors]] of Alderaan's destruction. The gathered [[Alderaan Flotilla]] was attacked by an [[Dreed's Star Destroyer|Imperial Star Destroyer]] over the Inner Rim world [[Espirion]], which had a multispecies population&mdash;including [[Alder-Espirion]]s led by [[Beon Beonel]] who were descended from Alderaanian humans and the [[Espirion (species)|Espirion]] species. The people were inspired by the princess' speech affirming that "Alderaan survives" so long as the values and voices of wisdom, creativity, and hope survived, and they destroyed the Star Destroyer before joining the Alderaan Flotilla. Expecting the united Alderaanians to find a new world while she herself returned to the Rebel Alliance, Organa then endorsed Verlaine to stand for a fair election as Alderaan's princess.<ref name="Leia 5">[[Princess Leia 5|''Princess Leia'' 5]]</ref>

Tarkin and many of the Joint Chiefs perished aboard the Death Star, and the ISB's role in the war against the Rebellion diminished after the debacle. The historian [[Beaumont Kin]] thought that the bureau became known for their failures over the Death Star and the Empire's strategy increasingly favored more power to the Imperial Military.<ref name="Kempshall" /> Nevertheless, in the quest to find rebel outposts, the ISB and Imperial Intelligence analyzed data on suspicious activities with reference to population data and used AI predictors to list targets for [[probe droid]]s.<ref name="Scum" /> [[Task Force 99]] "Scar Squadron" [[Mission to Horox III|found and destroyed]] a [[Horox III rebel outpost|rebel outpost]] in the Outer Rim following a report by Imperial Intelligence, and the elite stormtrooper squad hunted the [[Heroes of Yavin]]&mdash;Luke Skywalker, Leia Organa, [[Han Solo]], [[Chewbacca]], R2-D2, and C-3PO.<ref name="SW 2015 36">[[Star Wars (2015) 36|''Star Wars'' (2015) 36]]</ref>

The heroes were briefly stranded on [[Hubin]], where they met [[Clan Markona]]. The clan lived largely in isolation and paid tithes to the Empire, which left the community alone as it did not seek Hubin's limited little resources. The clan leader [[Markona]] had lived through the collapse of democracy and retired after lending assistance to Imperial forces, believing that there was no clear line of distinction between the end of the Republic and the Empire's beginning.<ref name="SW 2015 57">[[Star Wars (2015) 57|''Star Wars'' (2015) 57]]</ref> Although the Markona were initially satisfied with their isolation, they were inspired by the rebel heroes' defiance and joined the fight against the Empire.<ref name="SW 2015 60" /> As Scar Squadron continued fighting rebels on other worlds, they realized that the Empire had already lost the war of hearts and minds; stormtroopers such as themselves were no longer being hailed as heroes and children cosplayed as the Heroes of Yavin instead. Fearing that they would go down in history as villains rather than heroic "defenders of law and order," Scar Squadron began to massacre civilians.<ref name="Scar">{{StoryCite|story=For the Love of the Empire|book=A New Legacy 1}}</ref>

[[File:Declaration_of_Rebellion-SW_Propaganda.jpg|thumb|left|250px|The Declaration of Rebellion established the legal, democratic conditions for the Alliance to Restore the Republic's war against the Empire.]]
Conversely, the Rebel Alliance's "[[61st Mobile Infantry|Twilight Company]]" actively recruited ordinary civilians who shared the rebel movement's ideals of freedom and democracy on worlds where they battled the Empire, even though Alliance policy officially forbade the practice. Despite the realities of war, young cadets enlisting in the [[company]] had the expectation of saving people as heroes of democracy.<ref name="Twilight">''[[Battlefront: Twilight Company]]''</ref> To counter the Empire's claims that her rebels were anarchists and terrorists who sought to destroy the galaxy, Mothma wrote the [[Declaration of Rebellion]] based on the language of law. The declaration recognized the need for a "Galactic Government" with jurisdiction over all beings who must give up "certain rights and freedoms" to ensure harmony between everyone. Such "power and right to rule" derived from "the consent of the governed" and that "free beings" had the "unalienable right" and "duty" to change a government with a sustained history of "willfully and malignantly" usurping their rights.<ref name="Propaganda" />

The vessels of the Alliance Navy, such as the ''[[Home One]]'', were characterized as defenders of democracy, much like the starships of the Republic Navy before it.<ref name="Insider230" /> The Alliance decried the Empire's abuses, namely the disbandment of the Senate; a policy of human-centric speciesism and genocide; the replacement of member worlds' chosen leaders with moffs and governors; the raising of taxes "without the consent of those taxed;" imposing legal punishments such as imprisonment and the [[death penalty]] without trial; "unlawfully" taking "land and property;" and excess military expansion for the "sole purpose" of oppression. Refusing "any Imperial law contrary to the rights of free beings," the beings of the Rebel Alliance therefore pledged their property, honor, and lives to destroy the Emperor and his Empire and "make forever free all beings in the galaxy."<ref name="Propaganda" />

====What is stronger than fear?====
{{Quote|I believe in the Rebel Alliance. I believe in its mission. I believe we will succeed. The Empire will be defeated, and freedom will return to this galaxy. I will not stop until we win, and then I will fight to make sure nothing like the Empire ever returns.|Leia Organa|<ref name="SW 2020 49">[[Star Wars (2020) 49|''Star Wars'' (2020) 49]]</ref>}}
Despite massive losses to the Empire at the [[Battle of Mako-Ta|Battles of Mako-Ta]] and [[Battle of Hoth|Hoth]], the Rebellion continued to grow as Imperial military abuses against citizens intensified. Facing organized [[Mandalorian resistance|local resistance]], Moff [[Gideon]] oversaw the near-total [[Great Purge of Mandalore|genocide]] of Mandalore.<ref name="Timelines" /> When [[Chase on Milvayne|riots]] broke out on [[Milvayne]]&mdash;which [[Coalition for Progress|Imperial propaganda]] had touted as an ideal world, heavily policed by the [[Milvayne Authority|local authority]] without a visible Imperial presence&mdash;minister [[Pitina Mar-Mas Voor]] said "I daresay Milvayne will be back on track" if the Empire imposed "three years of martial law and a few well-placed bombardments."<ref name="Aphra 31">[[Doctor Aphra (2016) 31|''Doctor Aphra'' (2016) 31]]</ref> So concerned was Voor for the Empire's public image that the minister wanted to overthrow the "unlovable" Emperor; ironically, her plot was leaked to the HoloNet before the Empire censored it from the public and executed her.<ref name="Kempshall" />

[[File:Darth_Vader_25_textless_cover.jpg|thumb|right|260px|The Emperor was losing control of the galaxy as the war raged on.]]
Both the Empire and the Rebellion grappled with the chaos of the criminal underworld, including the [[Syndicate War]] instigated by [[Crimson Dawn]]<ref name="Timelines" />&mdash;criminal groups competed for the Emperor's favor based on the rumor that he was ending the Empire's informal arrangement with the Hutt Clan under Jabba.<ref name="CR5">[[Crimson Reign 5|''Crimson Reign'' 5]]</ref> Relying on hatred for the Empire instead<ref name="Hidden Empire 4">[[Hidden Empire 4|''Hidden Empire'' 4]]</ref> of also embracing the hope that helped to fuel the Alliance,<ref name="Rogue" /> Lady [[Qi'ra]] failed to topple the Imperial regime in her syndicate's [[war against the Sith]],<ref name="Hidden Empire 5">[[Hidden Empire 5|''Hidden Empire'' 5]]</ref> but the Rebellion continued to grow<ref name="Timelines" /> and learned that the Empire was building a [[DS-2 Death Star II Mobile Battle Station|second Death Star]] thanks to Crimson Dawn.<ref name="2020SW28">[[Star Wars (2020) 28|''Star Wars'' (2020) 28]]</ref> The scoundrel [[Kay Vess]] sought a free life between taking on jobs for competing syndicates, in the process helping the rebels foil the Imperial Security Bureau's infiltration of the criminal underworld and gain a codex of ISB files.<ref name="Outlaws">''[[Star Wars Outlaws]]''</ref> The [[Rokana Raiders]] pirate crew was active around the same time and operated under the democratic [[Rokana Code]], in which the pirates put important matters to a vote.<ref name="Pirates Fortune">''[[Star Wars Outlaws]]'' {{C|''A Pirate's Fortune'' DLC}}</ref>

Citizens watching the HoloNet witnessed a rebel [[Unity Day attack|attack]] on an [[Empire Day]] parade where Alliance forces took care not to inflict civilian casualties, followed by rebel starships [[Assault on Coruscant|damaging]] a giant statue of the Emperor near Coruscant's [[Monument Plaza]].<ref name="SW 2020 36">[[Star Wars (2020) 36|''Star Wars'' (2020) 36]]</ref> The Alderaan Flotilla was later [[Attack on the Survivor Fleet|attacked]] by the late Wilhuff Tarkin's protégée, [[Ellian Zahra]]. With support from Luke Skywalker, Leia Organa reconciled her duties as a princess, senator, and general after letting Zahra escape and&mdash;when she insisted on her obsession to exact vengeance against Organa&mdash;ordering the rebel fleet to kill Zahra instead. Thus relieved of the imminent threat and her sense of guilt over the Death Star's destruction of Alderaan, Organa spoke with Beon Beonel of the Alderaan Flotilla. She learnt that the Alderaanians had voted to disband the fleet: they wanted to find new places to call home and live in the wider galaxy, rather than narrow themselves to a singular identity as surviving refugees. While she dedicated herself to the war against the Empire and wished to fight on to prevent the return of tyranny, Organa also wanted a future in which she lived fully "with purpose in peace" and chose to focus on [[Rescue of Han Solo|rescuing]] her lover, Han Solo, from Jabba the Hutt before comitting to battle against Death Star II.<ref name="SW 2020 49" />

[[Bothan]] spies had confirmed that the battle station was being assembled in the [[Endor system]], which prompted Organa to lead [[Operation Yellow Moon]] to distract Imperial forces in the faraway [[Corva sector]] as the Alliance Fleet gathered in the [[Sullust system]]. Organa led [[Nien Nunb]], [[Antrot]], [[Kidi Aleri]], and [[Lokmarcha]] under the ruse of a recruitment run, but with potential recruits' lives at risk, she had herself and her team risk their own lives to ensure others' safety; she believed that the rebels fought not only for the anti-Imperial cause and duty but for each other, with love as an even more powerful force.<ref name="Moving Target">''[[Moving Target: A Princess Leia Adventure]]''</ref> During the rebel rendezvous over [[Sullust]], [[Tomasso]] of the [[Spice Runners of Kijimi]] told Mon Mothma that the Emperor and his ruling coterie were aboard the new Death Star. Mothma and Tomasso were nearly killed by an [[Lan-Drus|assassin]] of the Empire when they met on Desinta.<ref name="Rebellion 1">[[Return of the Jedi – The Rebellion 1|''Return of the Jedi – The Rebellion'' 1]]</ref> Unbeknownst to the rebels, the Emperor was luring them toward his best and well-prepared forces, with a focus on turning Luke Skywalker into his new apprentice.<ref name="ROTJ">{{Film|VI}}</ref>

===The New Republic===
====Heart of darkness====
{{Quote|This is democracy. It is strange. And it is messy. It's not about getting it right. It's about trying to get it right. Yes, it's a bit chaotic. Certainly we will get some things wrong. The Empire? They cared nothing for democracy. ''[&hellip;]'' But we will listen. To the countless voices crying out across the galaxy, we have opened our ears, and we will always listen. That is how democracy survives. That is how it thrives.|Olia Choko|<ref name="Aftermath" />}}
[[File:Celebration_on_Endor.png|thumb|left|350px|Heroes and friends toppled the Imperial regime after years of intense struggle.]]
At the [[Battle of Endor]] in [[4 ABY]], the rebels befriended the [[Bright Tree tribe|local Ewoks]] and they overcame the Imperial forces together. At the same time, Luke Skywalker affirmed that he was a Jedi and inspired his father's conscience. In an act of self-sacrifice for his son, Anakin turned against his Sith master and killed the Emperor.<ref name="ROTJ" /> News of the Emperor's death and the Rebellion's discovery and destruction of the second Death Star inspired spontaneous [[Uprisings against the Galactic Empire|uprisings]] across Imperial worlds, with [[Coruscant civil war|civil war]] erupting on Coruscant<ref name="Aftermath" /> as Imperials deserted the military in droves. Loyal Imperial forces enacted the Emperor's [[Contingency]], heeding his posthumous instruction to initiate [[Operation: Cinder]]&mdash;select worlds, regardless of whether or not they were affiliated with the Empire or the value of their resources were devestated. The genocide of billions prompted a second wave of deserting Imperials.<ref name="Alphabet" /> Meanwhile, the late Emperor's protégé [[Gallius Rax]] quickly gathered the bulk of the Empire's military might under Grand Admiral [[Rae Sloane]]'s [[Shadow Council]], secretly aiming for a final showdown on the Inner Rim world [[Jakku]]. In accordance with the Emperor's will, Rax intended to both bring down rebel forces and cull an empire that had failed its Emperor.<ref name="Empire's End">''[[Aftermath: Empire's End]]''</ref>

[[File:BF2_Cinder_Devastation.png|thumb|right|350px|While the Empire succumbed to chaotic retreats, mass defection, and infighting, loyalists heeded the Emperor's last command to commit genocide across both rebellious and faithful Imperial worlds.]]
Among the worlds selected for Operation: Cinder was the Emperor's homeworld of Naboo. Although Naboo's monarchy had been rendered ceremonial, Queen [[Sosha Soruna]] and Governor [[Donta Gesset]] joined Leia Organa and other rebels in [[Battle of Theed|repelling]] Imperial forces over Naboo twenty days following the Battle of Endor.<ref name="Shattered">[[Shattered Empire 3|''Shattered Empire'' 3]]</ref> As the Rebel Alliance reorganized itself as the New Republic, Naboo became a member of the new democratic state.<ref name="Battles" /> Mon Mothma served as the Chancellor of the New Republic, reviving democracy with the [[New Republic Senate|restoration]] of the Galactic Senate on Chandrila. Mothma's public relations representative, [[Olia Choko]], gave an interview in which she was assaulted by a [[fruit]] thrown by [[Geeska Dotalo]]. The disgruntled citizen spoke of his homeworld, [[Gan Moradir]], being embroiled in chaos and famine due to the war, and Choko encouraged Dotalo to serve as his world's representative in the Senate. Choko then chastised a [[Camerand Argell|New Republic officer]] nearby for parading captured Imperial soldiers, stating that the New Republic should not replicate the demeaning acts of the Empire; she believed that mistakes were inevitable in a democracy, which relied on listening to and learning from everyone to survive and thrive.<ref name="Aftermath" />

At the time of his "[[Iron Blockade]]" over the [[Anoat sector]], the rogue Moff [[Ubrik Adelhard]] reached out to the criminal underworld and allied with [[Zeva Bliss]]' spice runners on the Outer Rim world [[Kijimi]], who were willing to fuel further chaos so as to better their own advantage. Adelhard enlisted hunters to assassinate prospective senators,<ref name="Republic Under Siege 2">[[Battle of Jakku — Republic Under Siege 2|''Battle of Jakku — Republic Under Siege'' 2]]</ref> while the Sith-worshipping [[Acolytes of the Beyond]] emerged on Taris and recruited disaffected youths. The Acolytes led [[Coronet City revolution|civil unrest]] on Corellia in which they destroyed [[speeder]]s, attacked [[Peace and Security]] officers, looted stores, and mobbed pedestrians. They then attacked New Republic soldiers setting up aid outposts on worlds such as Devaron<ref name="Empire's End" /> and targeted leaders such as Leia Organa. Both she and her brother reflected that the fanatics had no understanding of the Force, murdering people to steal [[Sith artifact|dark side relics]] simply out of their obsessive fascination for the Sith and later as terror agents of Moff Adelhard.<ref name="Last Stand 2">[[Battle of Jakku — Last Stand 2|''Battle of Jakku — Last Stand'' 2]]</ref><ref name="SOTS">''[[Shadow of the Sith]]''</ref>

[[New Republic Intelligence]] officer [[Caern Adan]] was concerned with the nascent government's national security vulnerabilities from terrorist bombings and precision strikes by starfighter forces such as the [[204th Imperial Fighter Wing]], dubbed "Shadow Wing." Adan formed a dedicated working group targeting Shadow Wing with General Hera Syndulla's support: [[Alphabet Squadron]], led by [[Yrica Quell]], a former Shadow Wing pilot who partook in the [[Destruction of Nacronis|genocide]] of [[Nacronis]] during Operation: Cinder.<ref name="Alphabet" /> During the [[Cerberon system campaign|war]] in the [[Cerberon system]], the local [[Children of the Empty Sun]] religious sect endured both Imperial and Republic bombs, and its [[Let'ij|leader]] taught that both sides were "oppressors," with the New Republic differing "in its words, not its deeds." The group believed there would be no "peace through bureaucracy" and only its particular vision of the Force promised "true peace." When Alphabet Squadron discovered the truth of Quell's actions on Nacronis, the pilot Chass na Chadic cynically thought that the New Republic was corrupt enough to knowingly recuit war criminals.<ref name="Shadow Fall" />

====Victory and death====
{{Quote|For those who survive, wiping the slate clean is the only way to bring peace. If a record of the Empire's sins exists, it will breed violence for decades.|Soran Keize|<ref name="VP">''[[Victory's Price: An Alphabet Squadron Novel]]''</ref>}}
[[File:Liberty_for_all.jpg|thumb|right|260px|''[[Liberty for All]]'', a poster calling on the freedom-loving people of the galaxy to break the chains of Imperial oppression]]
Wishing to accelerate the Empire's defeat, the New Republic Senate voted for the creation of three new [[Starhawk-class battleship|''Starhawk''-class battleships]] in addition to the prototype ''[[Starhawk]]'' to rival the firepower of Imperial capital ships,<ref name="Starhawk">{{Databank|the-starhawk|the Starhawk}}</ref> which [[Defense of Kuat|lost control]] of Kuat Drive Yards in [[5 ABY]].<ref name="Timelines" /> Chancellor Mothma ordered New Republic forces not to engage occupying Imperial forces on Kashyyyk for subsequent [[Imperial–Republic peace talks|peace talks]] with the Empire. Before they collapsed due to Imperial sabotage, Leia Organa led rebel veterans in the unauthorized [[liberation of Kashyyyk]] and the enslaved Wookiees on their homeworld, which briefly made her a political pariah.<ref name="Empire's End" /> As Imperial forces were repelled from sectors across the galaxy, surviving forces gathered in the sparsely populated [[Jakku system]], in a remote part of the Western Reaches. Beyond either Imperial or Republic control were the [[New Separatist Union]] and the [[pirate territories]] of the [[Sovereign Latitudes of Maracavanya]].<ref name="VP" />

Colonel [[Soran Keize]] of the 204th Imperial Fighter Wing thought that his forces would lose the war, but he believed that the Republic would be merciless even if Imperials surrendered or deserted to live in peace&mdash;he realized that the Emperor kept [[Hall of Imperial Register|meticulous records]] of his people's complicity on Coruscant, binding even the lowest ranking functionaries of the Empire to the regime's atrocities by guilt, and he was resolved to [[Attack on Coruscant's Verity District|destroy]] the Emperor's data banks in a bid to keep his people from retribution. Meanwhile, Commander [[Wyl Lark]] of General Syndulla's starfighter squadrons decided to leave the war given the Imperial retreat, believing that it would be impossible to stop all Imperials, and attempts to do so would simply antagonize more ex-Imperials and "breed martyrs."<ref name="VP" /> Having completed his preparations, Gallius Rax leaked his forces' location to the New Republic, and the Senate narrowly voted to approve sending the military to Jakku.<ref name="Empire's End" />

Chancellor Mothma pushed for the [[Military Disarmament Act]]<ref name="TFA VD" /> in the lead-up to the [[Battle of Jakku]], claiming "democracy is not in need of defense" but "people are," proposing to cut down the [[New Republic Defense Force]] to 10% and divert resources to train member worlds' own local militaries once the war was won. Military control was also redirected from the Chancellor's office to [[New Republic High Command]].<ref name="Aftermath" /> With the benefit of hindsight, the historian Beaumont Kin suggested that the Republic was not ready for demilitarization, which was intended to allow star systems and sectors to maintain their own defense forces and form ad-hoc coalitions if necessary but ultimately undermined the Republic.<ref name="Kempshall" />

[[File:GalacticConcordance-BattleOfJakkuLastStand4.png|thumb|left|360px|The New Republic secured the surrender of Imperial remnants after the Battle of Jakku.]]
At the Battle of Jakku, Adelhard intended for his [[Adelhard coalition|coalition]] to destroy both Imperial and Republic forces, but the Spice Runners of Kijimi had become wary of Adelhard's power and helped the Republic through their underworld contacts; [[Eleodie Maracavanya]]'s band of pirates aboard the Super Star Destroyer ''[[Liberty's Misrule]]'' attacked the Imperials<ref name="Last Stand 3">[[Battle of Jakku — Last Stand 3|''Battle of Jakku — Last Stand'' 3]]</ref> while the Kijimi Spice Runners themselves attacked Adelhard's forces.<ref name="Last Stand 4">[[Battle of Jakku — Last Stand 4|''Battle of Jakku — Last Stand'' 4]]</ref> Commodore [[Kyrsta Agate]]'s Starhawk brought down the Imperial Super Star Destroyer ''[[Ravager]]'', and at the battle's end, the New Republic emerged victorious.<ref name="Empire's End" />

====Concord in civility?====
=====Spreading ideals=====
{{Quote|We didn't fight these battles&mdash;these ''wars''&mdash;to merely sit back and savor the victory. There's work to be done. And the onus is on ''us'' to spread the ideals that we hope to build this New Republic on. We've got to welcome the galaxy into our fold. And it won't always be easy&mdash;it never is.|Princess Leia Organa, to Chancellor Mon Mothma|<ref name="SW 2025 1">[[Star Wars (2025) 1|''Star Wars'' (2025) 1]]</ref>}}
[[File:A_New_Dawn-SWP.jpg|thumb|right|260px|''[[A New Dawn]]'', celebrating the restoration of democracy on Chandrila, the first capital of the New Republic.]]
Within a year, the signing of the [[Galactic Concordance]] ended the Galactic Civil War, with large portions of the remaining Imperial territories being incorporated into the New Republic. A few traditionally conservative worlds of the [[inner systems' Imperial remnant]] also surrendered but remained outside the New Republic on the conditions set out in the Galactic Concordance, but the remnant soon collapsed due to its members' disunity.<ref name="Battles" /> Leia Organa pushed for the Republic to spread its ideals across the galaxy by persuading more systems to join it.<ref name="SW 2025 1" /> Likewise, a report for the [[Committee of Democratic Implementation]] stressed that the New Republic should focus not just on new laws but to give citizens an understanding of justice and a system in which laws were consistently and fairly applied.<ref name="Kempshall" />

Some worlds chose not to join the New Republic and remained neutral, such as Ryloth, whose freedom fighters had their hard-won independence.<ref name="Resistance Reborn">''[[Resistance Reborn]]''</ref> The Narquois people sued their Neimoidian neighbors for imposing hundreds of years of serfdom on them, with the New Republic courts handling unprecedented levels of compensation claims.<ref name="Aliens" /> Through elections, the government periodically relocated its capital across member worlds, placating the outlying systems that had seceded from the Republic in the lead-up to the rise of the Empire.<ref name="TFA VD">''[[Star Wars: The Force Awakens: The Visual Dictionary]]''</ref> The New Republic maintained a [[New Republic Defense Fleet|defense fleet]] but not a formal military. Leia Organa likened the new democracy to being a teenager, where every new crisis felt like the first one.<ref name="Last Shot">''[[Last Shot]]''</ref>

=====The Pirros problem=====
{{Quote|Is this how our grand revolution ends? The second our mettle is tested, we fall back to old habits? We answer threats and violence with ''bigger'' threats and violence? So much for the ''New Republic'' then. If we have to fight, we ''will'' fight&mdash;but we did dream of a softer way. I didn't imagine it. A way less reliant on ''anger'' and ''violence''.|Leia Organa, to Mon Mothma|<ref name="SW 2025 6">[[Star Wars (2025) 6|''Star Wars (2025) 6]]</ref>}}
Around [[6 ABY]],<ref name="SW2025Date">{{StarWars2025Date|1}}</ref> the New Republic attempted to woo the world [[Pirros]] ahead of their [[Unidentified trade summit|trade summit]]. Although it was once part of the previous Galactic Republic, however, the world's new leadership was hesitant to accept that membership in the New Republic would improve trade and defense, especially since Pirros was a leader of the [[Fenril sector]]. On top of that, the supplies sent by the Republic in an act of goodwill had to endure pirate attacks, which made the Republic seem weak. After accusing Republic [[Councilor]] [[Kith Alaytia]] of having been Moff Adelhard's lieutenant, Pirrosi Prime Minister [[Kezrin Almovar]] expelled the Republic from the summit and suspended trade, with the [[Fenril Consortium]] shooting down a Republic trading ship,<ref name="SW 2025 1" /> the ''[[Nechrim's Wake]]'',<ref name="SW 2025 3">[[Star Wars (2025) 3|''Star Wars'' (2025) 3]]</ref> for "trespass."<ref name="SW 2025 1" /> The ''Nechrim's Wake'' crashed on the planet [[Loradil]], where its crew was attacked by<ref name="SW 2025 3">[[Star Wars (2025) 3|''Star Wars'' (2025) 3]]</ref> remnants of the Separatist Droid Army that had been trapped on world since the Clone Wars.<ref name="SW 2025 5">[[Star Wars (2025) 5|''Star Wars'' (2025) 5]]</ref>

As Han Solo, Luke Skywalker, and Beilert Valance donned the garb of the fallen old Republic in the form of clone trooper gear so as to defeat the Separatist remnant, the Fenril Consortium-New Republic struggle continued and escalated, much to Organa's upset as it represented a fallback into threats and conflict.<ref name="SW 2025 6" />

=====Operating an amnesty program=====
{{Main|New Republic Amnesty Program}}
The New Republic started a de-Imperialization process with ex-Imperial civil servants who were deemed to be low-value and low-risk, fast-tracking their return to important administrative and bureaucratic positions under the new state. Ex-Imperials who committed lesser criminal offenses were gradually released with restrictions on weapon ownership and voting rights, which were lifted when they were judged to pose no potential threat. The [[New Republic Amnesty Program]] rehabilitated ex-Imperials on Coruscant with [[Parole droid|droid counselors]] while the [[Reconciliation Project]] attempted to provide healing and closure by pairing ex-Imperials with citizens who had suffered under the regime. The education systems of some worlds also incorporated the [[Honor Lost on Lasan: Serving the Empire, Fighting for the Alliance|memoir]] of former ISB agent and [[Rebel Alliance Intelligence Service|Alliance intelligence officer]] Alexsandr Kallus, which reflected on the war from both perspectives.<ref name="Kempshall" />

Under [[the Duchess]] and ex-Imperial Captain [[Bombardier (individual)|Bombardier]], the [[royal family of Plazir-15]] transformed itself into an elective monarchy leading a [[direct democracy]] following the Empire's collapse; [[Plazir-15]]'s charter affirmed the world's pacifism and pluralism, with citizens directly involved in decision-making and choosing to be independent of the New Republic.<ref name="Guns">{{TheMandalorian|Chapter 22: Guns for Hire}}</ref> In [[9 ABY]],<ref name="MandoDate">{{MandoDate|22}}</ref> a tannoy announcement at Plazir-15's [[Unidentified domed city|domed city]] declared that it was the only remaining direct democracy in the Outer Rim. Its society was welcoming of droids, who made up most of the city's workforce while organic beings were largely free to live comfortable lives. The human security chief [[Helgait]], a long-time supporter of Count Dooku and his vision of "democracy," infected some of the droids' [[Nepenthé]] [[lubricant]]s to cause mayhem in the city, but he was stopped by Mandalorians Bo-Katan Kryze and [[Din Djarin]].<ref name="Guns" /> In previous decades, the advance of the Separatist Droid Army and the Empire's encouragement of prejudice had exacerbated anti-droid sentiment, though the age of Rebellion inspired droid uprisings demanding liberty and equality. Even prominent human rebels treated droids as fellow family members<ref name="Droidography" /> despite a brief galaxy-wide [[Scourge crisis|droid plague]] occurring between Crimson Dawn's war against the Empire and the Battle of Endor,<ref name="Dark Droids 1">[[Dark Droids 1|''Dark Droids'' 1]]</ref> and the New Republic encouraged the trend of acceptance between organic and mechanical beings.<ref name="Droidography" />

====Imperial Remnant threats====
{{Quote|You cannot defeat evil once and consider yourself victorious. It is our duty as the New Republic to challenge evil when we see it, no matter how scarred or hurt we are from past conflicts. We either stand for what we believe in forever without limit or qualification, with strength and bravery, or we shall fall to the same elements that crushed the Republic decades ago.|Senator Leia Organa, cautioning against complacency in the New Republic Senate|<ref name="Free Fall">''[[Poe Dameron: Free Fall]]''</ref>}}
To [[Hunt for Grogu|protect]] the child [[Din Grogu|Grogu]] from Imperial warlord Moff [[Gideon]] and his [[Gideon's Imperial remnant|forces]], Din Djarin also worked with [[Marshal]] [[Carasynthia Dune]] of the New Republic. At her Mandalorian friend's request, Dune circumvented protocol to discharge convict [[Migs Mayfeld]]. The ex-Imperial [[Mission to Morak|infiltrated]] the secret [[Morak Imperial refinery|Imperial refinery]] on [[Morak]] alongside Djarin by ferrying explosive [[rhydonium]] fuel to the site, where Mayfeld met [[Valin Hess]], his [[commanding officer]] during Operation: Cinder. Detesting the man's sadistic indifference about their last [[Destruction of Burnin Konn|operation]] on [[Burnin Konn]], where thousands of civilians and even Mayfeld's fellow soldiers were slaughtered, Mayfeld killed Hess and destroyed the refinery while Djarin obtained information about [[Gideon's light cruiser|Gideon's cruiser]].<ref name="Believer">{{TheMandalorian|Chapter 15: The Believer}}</ref>

[[File:Pershing-Kane_in_Monument_Plaza.png|thumb|left|380px|The New Republic welcomed captured Imperials such as [[Elia Kane]] and Dr. Pershing into its Amnesty Program.]]
Following the [[Second rescue of Grogu|capture]] of Gideon and his cruiser, several of its ex-Imperials were placed in the New Republic's Amnesty Program. Cloning specialist Dr. [[Penn Pershing]] was among the scientists working for the New Republic on Coruscant, though his [[Unidentified parole droid|parole droid]] advised him that the [[Coruscant Accords]] had since banned cloning.<ref name="Convert">{{TheMandalorian|Chapter 19: The Convert}}</ref> Escaping from New Republic custody, Moff Gideon was able to organize with his forces within the Republic and speak with the [[Shadow Council (Imperial Remnants)|Shadow Council]] of other [[Imperial Remnants]].<ref name="Spies">{{TheMandalorian|Chapter 23: The Spies}}</ref> Gideon gathered his forces on Mandalore, wishing to engineer an army using research into cloning, the Force, and Mandalorian technology, but a diaspora of warriors who rallied around Bo-Katan Kryze [[Conflict on Mandalore|ousted]] the warlord's forces from their homeworld.<ref name="Return">{{TheMandalorian|Chapter 24: The Return}}</ref>

Around the same time,<ref name="Timelines" /> the Santhe Corporation's Corellian shipyards had been repurposed by the New Republic to dismantle Imperial Star Destroyers and repurpose key parts for the New Republic Defense Fleet. According to [[Regional Supervisor]] [[Myn Weaver]], other parts were sold to the shipyard's investors to generate profits for Republic reconstruction projects. However, he and the shipyard workers were secretly Imperial loyalists who shipped the hyperdrive cores to [[Morgan Elsbeth]], an ally of Grand Admiral Thrawn.<ref name="Ahsoka II">{{Ahsoka|Part Two: Toil and Trouble}}</ref> Elsbeth used the cores to build a [[Eye of Sion|hyperspace ring]] that delivered Thrawn from his exile to the witches' world of Dathomir.<ref name="Ahsoka VIII">{{Ahsoka|Part Eight: The Jedi, the Witch, and the Warlord}}</ref> The resulting military campaign was classified by the New Republic.<ref name="Kempshall" />

[[File:New_republic_defense_council.png|thumb|right|380px|The New Republic debated issues of defense in a joint committee of senators and armed forces leaders.]]
New Republic General Hera Syndulla had briefed Chancellor Mothma and the [[Senate Oversight Committee]] about her [[Mission to Corellia (New Republic)|intervention]] at Corellia's shipyard and raised warnings about the threat of Thrawn's return, but Senator [[Hamato Xiono]] led opposition to Syndulla's request for aid.<ref name="Ahsoka III">{{Ahsoka|Part Three: Time to Fly}}</ref> Defying the committee's denial of permission to investigate further, Syndulla and Captain [[Carson Teva]] led a squadron of ships to the [[Hyperspace transport ring|hyperspace ring's]] [[Denab system|place of assembly]], where two of Teva's pilots died in a collision.<ref name="Ahsoka IV">{{Ahsoka|Part Four: Fallen Jedi}}</ref> A detachment of New Republic vessels retrieved Syndulla and Teva's squadron, and the general was summoned before the [[Senate Defense Council]]. Senator Xiono derided General Syndulla at the hearing, chaired by the Chancellor, and commented that Syndulla's rebellious actions were not appropriate for an established government like the New Republic. When Captain Teva raised Moff Gideon as an example of the threat posed by Imperial remnant leaders, Senator Xiono asserted that Gideon acted alone. Although Xiono wanted to refer Syndulla to a [[court-martial]], C-3PO interrupted the meeting, relaying Senator Leia Organa's cover-up story that she had authorized Syndulla's mission unaware of the Oversight Committee's prior denial.<ref name="Ahsoka VII">{{Ahsoka|Part Seven: Dreams and Madness}}</ref>

====Light in the underworld====
{{Quote|Living with the Tuskens has made you soft.''"<br />"''No, its made me strong. You can only get so far without a tribe.|Fennec Shand and Boba Fett|<ref name="FettChap4">{{BOBF|Chapter 4: The Gathering Storm}}</ref>}}
Concurrent to the developing Imperial Remnant crisis<ref name="Chapter 16">{{TheMandalorian|Chapter 16: The Rescue}}</ref> in 9 ABY,<ref name="Timelines" /> the famed bounty hunter<ref name="Boba Databank">{{Databank|boba-fett|Boba Fett}}</ref> [[Boba Fett]] returned to Tatooine with the [[assassin]] [[Fennec Shand]] at his side<ref name="Chapter 16" /> and claimed control over the criminal empire that had once belonged to the late Jabba the Hutt,<ref name="FettChap1">{{BOBF|Chapter 1: Stranger in a Strange Land}}</ref> having decided that "idiotic" crime lords had led one too many bounty hunters to their deaths. Instead, Fett intended to forge his own [[Fett gotra|gotra]]<ref name="FettChap4" /> built off of respect, instead of relying on fear.<ref name="FettChap1" /> Further, inspired by his time living amongst the [[Tusken Raider]]s, he hoped his new gotra would be a true community that would strengthen its members<ref name="FettChap4" /> and protect the community of [[Mos Espa]]. Fett's rule was challenged by the [[Pyke Syndicate]]'s arrival on Tatooine, but he was able to affirm his place as [[Daimyo]] of Mos Espa by triumphing in the battle, winning the love of the community in the process.<ref name="FettChap7">{{BOBF|Chapter 7: In the Name of Honor}}</ref>

In [[18 ABY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> [[Poe Dameron]] left his home on Yavin 4 and fell in with the Spice Runners of Kijimi, falling in love with [[Zorii Bliss]] in the process. Many months later, they helped Zorii's mother, Zeva, arrange the [[Kijimi summit]] of key players in the galactic criminal underworld to elevate the [[Spicerunner|spice runners']] position. However, Dameron's idealism foiled the Bliss' brutal tactics, and he fled [[Kijimi City]] to join the New Republic, having found resonance with Senator Leia Organa's principles of needing to continually challenge evil, which she expressed in broadcast Senate debates.<ref name="Free Fall" />

====Fascism festers====
{{Quote|In the post-war years people became so determined to look forward that they did not spend nearly enough time looking back at what they had left behind. They tied themselves so tightly to the belief that this should never happen again that they neglected to take the steps to ensure that it could not.|Beaumont Kin, ''The Rise and Fall of the Galactic Empire''|<ref name="Kempshall" />}}
[[File:Episode_VIII_Stormtroopers.png|thumb|left|380px|Behind machinery and masks, the First Order's fascism took advantage of individuals' sense of insecurity in its ambition to restore the Empire's might.]]
Not all inhabitants of the galaxy were swayed back to democracy. Aiming to revive the Empire's false glories after a period of hiding in the Unknown Regions, the [[First Order]] was a fascist junta<ref name="VII Junior">''[[Star Wars: The Force Awakens: A Junior Novel]]''</ref> formed from a [[Unknown Regions Imperial remnant|select few Imperial loyalists]] and [[Gallius Rax's child soldiers]] who survived the Battle of Jakku as part of the late Emperor's Contingency.<ref name="Empire's End" /> The Order came under the control of [[Snoke]], a secret creation of Darth Sidious' [[Sith Eternal]] [[Sith cult|cult]] on [[Exegol]],<ref name="TROS EE">''[[Star Wars: The Rise of Skywalker: Expanded Edition]]''</ref> and took advantage of the New Republic's demilitarization under Chancellor Mothma to strengthen a [[First Order military|military]] more technologically advanced than that of the Old Empire's.<ref name="TLJ ICS">''[[Star Wars: The Last Jedi: Incredible Cross-Sections]]''</ref>

Despite the New Republic's arms manufacturing restrictions, major arms corporations<ref name="TSWB" /> such as Sienar Fleet Systems and Kuat Drive Yards created subsidiaries such as [[Sienar-Jaemus Fleet Systems]] and [[Kuat-Entralla Drive Yards]] operating outside the New Republic's boundaries to supply the First Order.<ref name="TLJ ICS" /> The First Order kidnapped children to be indoctrinated and trained for its [[First Order stormtrooper|stormtrooper]] program as part of [[Project Resurrection]], which eventually came under General [[Armitage Hux]] and Captain [[Phasma]]'s oversight.<ref name="USWNE" /> The Order specifically targeted rebel veterans such as [[Landonis Balthazar Calrissian|Lando Calrissian]],<ref name="TROS EE" /> whose [[Kadara Calrissian|daughter]] was kidnapped in [[15 ABY]]. By [[21 ABY]],<ref name="Timelines" /> the Corporate Sector Authority was covertly funding the First Order and had transformed part of its own [[Corporate Sector Authority Security Police|Security Police]] into an army.<ref name="SOTS" /> [[Jinata Security]] collaborated with the Order on Project Resurrection in the Core Worlds' [[Jinata system]], where the planet [[Vardos]] was under the control of Jinata Security's leader, [[Protectorate (title)|Protectorate]] [[Gleb]].<ref name="Battlefront II">''[[Star Wars Battlefront II]]''</ref>

[[File:Bloodline-LiarPoster.png|thumb|right|260px|Senator Leia Organa was driven out of politics, and she formed the Resistance as the New Republic remained unmoved by her warnings.]]
Senator Leia Organa led politically unpopular campaigns for re-arming the Republic.<ref name="TFA VD" /> She uncovered that the crime lord [[Rinnrivin Di]], whose [[Rinnrivin Di's cartel|cartel]] raided trade lanes around Ryloth, was funded by the [[Amaxine warriors (New Republic Era)|Amaxine warriors]], who were in turn agents of the First Order. Frustrated by the Senate's unwillingness to take action against the increasing encroachments of the First Order and scandalized by the public revelation that her father was Darth Vader, Organa left politics in the Senate to set up a paramilitary force named the Resistance<ref name="Bloodline" /> in [[28 ABY]].<ref name="Timelines" />

Since the Chancellors who succeeded Mon Mothma lacked her charismatic power, the Chancellor remained a largely ceremonial post with little effective authority. The Senate continued suffering from political gridlock between the Centrist faction that wanted to be governed by a strong and centralized executive power and Organa's Populist faction.<ref name="Bloodline" /> In [[29 ABY]], a majority of systems aligned with the Centrist movement [[First Order secession|seceded]] from the New Republic and publicly joined the First Order,<ref name="TROS VD" /> having been disillusioned with the Republic government's lack of cohesion and the resulting collapse of essential services. Organa had tried to keep the Centrist systems within the Republic, if only to make sure they could be monitored, but unlike the Old Republic Senate under Palpatine's chancellorship, the New Republic Senate chose not to oppose the departure of the dissatisfied systems<ref name="TSWB" /> and welcomed the end of political gridlock.<ref name="Propaganda" />

====Cold War in the Borderlands====
{{Quote|The galaxy's hard-won peace is at risk. We may only get this one warning, this one chance to take action. I implore you to study my findings carefully, and with an open mind. What we're discovered should transcend petty political bickering, or your personal opinion of me. Unless we want another war&mdash;and surely, after the bloodshed that twenty years ago, nobody can want such a thing&mdash;we must be on guard. We must come together. We have to act.|Leia Organa's call on fellow senators to recognize the First Order threat|<ref name="Bloodline" />}}
[[Cold War]] tensions emerged as the First Order made incursions in the [[Borderlands]] between New Republic and First Order space,<ref name="Before">''[[Before the Awakening]]''</ref> While Leia Organa remained politically unpopular, the Senate tolerated the Resistance's activities,<ref name="TFA VD" /> which monitored the First Order while trying to avoid galactic conflict.<ref name="TSWB" /> The New Republic's [[Senate Intelligence Committee]] dismissed evidence of the First Order's activities as insignificant to warrant action.<ref name="Before" /> Furthermore, Vice Admiral Amilyn Holdo of the Resistance gathered evidence that the New Republic Defense Force was intentionally taking no action regarding the First Order's growth.<ref name="AoR Poe">[[Age of Resistance - Poe Dameron 1|''Age of Resistance - Poe Dameron'' 1]]</ref> In [[31 ABY]],<ref name="Poe date">According to ''[[Star Wars: The Rise of Skywalker: The Visual Dictionary]]'', [[Poe Dameron]] joined the [[Resistance]] three [[Standard year|years]] before the events of [[Star Wars: Episode VII The Force Awakens|''Star Wars'': Episode VII ''The Force Awakens'']], which ''[[Star Wars: Timelines]]'' date to [[34 ABY]].</ref> Poe Dameron and other [[New Republic pilot]]s of [[Rapier Squadron]] joined the Resistance, having been told by their [[Lonno Deso|superior officer]] that the First Order was not a serious threat following a failed [[Suraz engagement|attempt]] to stop the Order's hijacking of a [[Yissira Zyde|freighter]] in which they lost a [[Muran|fellow pilot]].<ref name="Before" />

[[File:Poe_Dameron_20_Textless.jpg|thumb|left|260px|Poe Dameron began learning to be a leader as General Organa tasked him with the search for Lor San Tekka.]]
Leia Organa later tasked Dameron and his colleagues with [[Operation: Sabre Strike|intercepting]] [[Erudo Ro-Kiintor]], a New Republic senator of the Centrist faction who had blocked motions to sanction the First Order and increase funding for New Republic forces. Indeed, the Resistance found in the Borderlands that the First Order had been transferring large sums of money to Ro-Kiintor via the Corporate Sector Authority and disguised through proxy companies.<ref name="Before" /> In [[32 ABY]],<ref name="32 ABY">''[[Star Wars: The Rise of Skywalker: The Visual Dictionary]]'' states that [[Black Squadron (Resistance)|Black Squadron]] was formed two [[Standard year|years]] before the [[Hosnian Cataclysm|Starkiller Incident]], which ''[[Star Wars: Timelines]]'' dates to [[34 ABY]], thereby corresponding to [[32 ABY]].</ref> Dameron led [[Black Squadron (Resistance)|Black Squadron]] on a [[Hunt for Lor San Tekka|search for Lor San Tekka]]&mdash;the explorer believed to be the lead on the whereabouts of Luke Skywalker&mdash;and found First Order forces attempting the same. Despite the Resistance's rules of engagement requiring members not to initiate a direct attack on First Order forces, Black Squadron pilot [[L'ulo L'ampar]] [[Mission to Ovanis|fired upon]] a [[First Order landing platform]] on [[Ovanis]]. Afterward, Dameron spoke with L'ampar about the incident, with the latter believing that hostile [[TIE/fo space superiority fighter|starfighters]] ought to be destroyed at every possible chance before the forthcoming war, but the [[Squad leader|squadron leader]] responded that the Resistance should not be accelerating the conflict as they were not yet ready, and their duty was to prepare while also helping the New Republic understand the First Order threat.<ref name="Poe 4">[[Poe Dameron 4|''Poe Dameron'' 4]]</ref>

When the First Order [[Blockade of the Otomok system|blockaded]] the [[Otomok system]], the New Republic simply voiced opposition and accepted the Order's claim that it was an internal affair.<ref name="Bomber">''[[The Last Jedi: Bomber Command]]''</ref> The Order freely extracted [[ore]] from [[Hays Minor]] of the Otomok system,<ref name="AoR Rose">[[Age of Resistance - Rose Tico 1|''Age of Resistance - Rose Tico'' 1]]</ref> tested weapons on the world, and stole its children to bolster the stormtrooper ranks. As for the Resistance, they violated New Republic regulations on military equipment that prevented newer [[Koensayr Manufacturing|Koensayr]] [[BTA-NR2 Y-wing starfighter|Y-wing]] models from carrying [[SW-9 ion cannon|ion cannons]] and bomber ordance launchers&mdash;the group had been acquiring the vessels and modifying them to better counter First Order forces<ref name="RSOWM">''[[Rebel Starfighters Owners' Workshop Manual]]''</ref> together with Slayn & Korpil [[MG-100 StarFortress SF-17 heavy bomber]]s.<ref name="Bomber" />

Two young women from Hays Minor, [[Paige Tico|Paige]] and [[Rose Tico]], fled their homeworld and joined the Resistance. The Tico sisters were among the crew of [[Crimson Squadron|two]] [[Cobalt Squadron|squadrons]] of those heavy bombers led by Vice Admiral Holdo in a [[Missions against pirates in the Cassander sector|campaign]] in the Borderlands' [[Cassander sector]] against [[Sheh Soahi's pirates|local pirates]] who were being supplied by the First Order without directly engaging the Order's forces<ref name="Bomber" /> in [[34 ABY]].<ref name="Timelines" /> Instead of heeding Leia Organa's warnings of First Order military build-up, Chancellor [[Lanever Villecham]] simply focused on promoting New Republic trade relations with the neutral systems of the Trans-Hydian Borderlands and was serving his second year as Chancellor<ref name="TFA VD" /> in the same year.<ref name="Villecham date">''[[Star Wars: Timelines]]'' dates the events of {{Film|VII}}, which include [[Lanever Villecham]] serving in his second [[Standard year|year]] as [[Chancellor (New Republic)|Chancellor]] of the [[New Republic]], according to ''[[Star Wars: The Force Awakens: The Visual Dictionary]]'', to [[34 ABY]].</ref>

====Complacency kills====
{{Quote|Not realising that the ideology that had once produced stormtroopers armor was still producing stormtroopers. Chancellor Lanever Villecham resolutely refused to heed the warnings from the likes of General Leia Organa as to what was about to happen.|Beaumont Kin, ''The Rise and Fall of the Galactic Empire''|<ref name="Kempshall" />}}
In that year,<ref name="Timelines" /> Resistance spy [[Vi Moradi]] investigated the new stormtrooper project and came into contact with "[[Archex|Cardinal]]," who supervised the training of young children. The stormtrooper leader believed that the Republican system of government was "doomed to fail" because sentient beings were incapable of pursuing rational long-term self-interest; he believed it was the First Order's mission to sort the Republic's "mess" by removing "diplomats and lobbyists who don't represent real people with real problems" and "bringing equality to all." Cardinal later became disillusioned with the Order after realizing the brutal and unforgiving nature of Captain Phasma and was rescued by Moradi.<ref name="Phasma">[[Phasma (novel)|''Phasma'']]</ref> The Resistance later placed him on a "deprogramming protocol" in the forests of [[Cerea]] that rehabilitated people who had been brainwashed by First Order indoctrination.<ref name="BlackSpire">''[[Galaxy's Edge: Black Spire]]''</ref>

New Republic Command<ref name="Recruit">{{Resistance|The Recruit}}</ref> later<ref name="Timelines" /> [[Mission to rendezvous with the Resistance|sent]] the pilots [[Kazuda Xiono]], [[Mia Gabon]], and [[Hugh Sion]] to deliver secretive intelligence to [[Commander]] Poe Dameron of the Resistance. They were directly attacked by [[Major]] [[Elrik Vonreg]] of the First Order, but Xiono was able to deliver the information to Dameron, and they and Leia Organa learned that the First Order was planning a massive attack on the Republic.<ref name="Recruit" /> General Organa sent her envoy, [[Korr Sella]], to warn the Republic Senate on Hosnian Prime, where she met Chancellor Villecham.<ref name="TFAn" />

[[File:Republic_City_Hosnian_Prime.png|thumb|right|360px|Warnings of the First Order's rise were ignored by the New Republic on Hosnian Prime&mdash;its last capital&mdash;until it was too late.]]
However, the entire [[Hosnian system]], home the New Republic capital and the bulk of its fleet, was [[Hosnian Cataclysm|obliterated]] by the First Order with a [[Starkiller crisis|single strike]] from their Unknown Regions superweapon, [[Starkiller Base]]. Although the Resistance [[Battle of Starkiller Base|destroyed]] Starkiller Base shortly after, the Order proceeded to conquer much of the galaxy<ref name="TFA VD" /> under a vanguard of zealous young commanders such as the technologist General [[Armitage Hux]].<ref name="TROS VD" /> They embraced [[Neo-Imperialism]],<ref name="Battles" /> [[Battle of Oetchi|whittling down]] the main [[Resistance navy|Resistance fleet]] to a single starship&mdash;the ''[[Millennium Falcon]]''&mdash;in a brutal [[First Order-Resistance War|war]],<ref name="Resistance Reborn">''[[Resistance Reborn]]''</ref> with ex-Imperial elders approving of the resurgence of fascism and militarism.<ref name="TROS VD" />

===Age of Resistance===
====Lighting the flame====
{{Quote|That's how we're gonna win. Not fighting what we hate, saving what we love.|Resistance fighter Rose Tico|audio=SavingWhatWeLove.flac|<ref name="TLJ">{{Film|VIII}}</ref>}}
[[File:Leia_Organa_Blaster_TLJ.png|thumb|left|220px|Leia Organa served as a fighter for democracy in both politics and the battlefield.]]
The Resistance comprised not only of Rebel Alliance veterans but those who found friendship among each other in face of the First Order's aggression&mdash;such as the aspiring Jedi [[Rey Skywalker|Rey]], the defected stormtrooper [[Finn]], the pilot leader Poe Dameron, and the [[Technician|mechanic]] [[Rose Tico]]. Although the Resistance sent pleas for help to their allies across the galaxy at their [[Battle of Oetchi|most desperate hour]], none responded. The galaxy's wealthiest industrialists tried to profiteer from the war while living luxuriously in resorts such as [[Canto Bight]] on the Corporate Sector planet [[Cantonica]], funding both the First Order and the Resistance's machines of war. Luke Skywalker had gone into exile on [[Ahch-To]], believing that the Jedi's legacy was hypocrisy, hubris, and failure in overseeing the rise of Darth Sidious, Vader, and [[Ben Solo|Kylo Ren]] of the First Order. However, Skywalker was spurred from apathy by a persistent Rey, heeding Yoda's advice to pass on his lessons as a Jedi. Kylo Ren continued to reject his family's love when Rey confronted him, thinking of himself as a "monster" of the dark side and fulfilling his ambition to replace Snoke as the First Order's [[Supreme Leader]]. Nevertheless, stories of heroism, such as Amilyn Holdo's [[Holdo maneuver|self-sacrifice]] against the [[Supremacy task force|First Order fleet]] and Luke Skywalker's [[Battle of Crait|standoff]] against the [[First Order Army]] on Crait began to inspire rebels with hope across the galaxy.<ref name="TLJ" />

[[Ikkrukk]] was among the hundreds of worlds invaded by the First Order. Although the Resistance [[Battle of Ikkrukk|defeated]] the First Order forces that were destroying [[Grail City]], Prime Minister [[Grist]] was unwilling to lend further support for fear of First Order retaliation, and some members of her government cabinet were even sympathetic to the new regime and hoped that occupation would benefit the city financially. Later, the Resistance was temporarily sheltered by the [[Ryloth Defense Authority]] militia, but Chancellor [[Drelomon]]'s [[Ryloth government|Rylothian government]] was worried that the Resistance's presence would draw the destructive wrath of the First Order. On [[Akiva]], which had been the first planet in the Outer Rim to [[Rebellion on Akiva|join]] the New Republic, voters removed the incumbent governor in an [[Emergency election on Akiva|emergency election]] in favor of a First Order&ndash;affiliated merchant.<ref name="Resistance Reborn" />

The First Order blockaded Ryloth to demand tithes anyway, oblivious to the Resistance's presence. General Organa promised the RDA leader, [[Yendor Brethen]], that she would not ask him to fight his relucatant government or people. However, the government betrayed the RDA, whose [[Ryloth Defense Authority base|base]] was attacked by the First Order, yet the Resistance had taken the time to re-gather its forces and evacuate. The group also [[Mission to Corellia|stole]] a list of rebel veterans from [[Occupation of Corellia|First Order–occupied]] Corellia, intending to recruit them to the cause.<ref name="Resistance Reborn" /> The [[Tah'Nuhna (species)|Tah'Nuhna]] civilization, who remained neutral in conflicts from the Hundred-Year Darkness to the rise and fall of the Empire, were later [[Destruction of Tah'Nuhna|destroyed]] by the First Order for briefly providing refuge for the Resistance.<ref name="Allegiance 1">[[Allegiance 1|''Allegiance'' 1]]</ref>

====Citizens of the galaxy====
{{Quote|Where did they get all these fighter craft? They have no navy.''"<br />"''It's not a navy, sir, it's just&hellip; people.|Allegiant General Pryde and Admiral [[Frantis Griss]], surprised by the Citizens' Fleet|<ref name="TROS">{{Film|IX}}</ref>}}
[[File:Allegiance-1-textless-Stelfreeze.png|thumb|right|250px|The Resistance was not only led by General Organa but bound by friendship and a sense of family among its fighters.]]
Spurred by the collective punishment of the Tah'Nuhna, the Resistance sought the support of the [[Mon Cala fleet]], [[Mission to Mon Cala|pleading]] King [[Ech-Char]] to support their cause. Ech-Char wanted to protect his people from First Order retaliation and refused to support the Resistance, and he also chaired the [[Court Of Justice|Mon Cala court]] with the support of isolationist Quarren advisors to order General Organa's group to leave the world.<ref name="Allegiance 3">[[Allegiance 3|''Allegiance'' 3]]</ref> When the First Order arrived, the veteran Quarren leader Nossor Ri sacrificed himself to allow the Resistance to leave the planet alongside [[Aftab Ackbar]]'s Mon Cala fleet.<ref name="Allegiance 4">[[Allegiance 4|''Allegiance'' 4]]</ref>

The Resistance subsequently relocated to the secluded world of [[Ajan Kloss]]. While Kylo Ren's First Order struggled to reconcile either its orderly militarism with mysticism or its youthful vanguard with its ex-Imperial veteran leaders, the tyrannical junta adhered to Palpatine's contingencies when the Emperor resurfaced<ref name="TROS VD" /> in [[35 ABY]].<ref name="Timelines" /> He had been resurrected due to the cloning efforts of his Sith cultists on Exegol, which also boasted hundreds of planet-destroying [[Xyston-class Star Destroyer|''Xyston''-class Star Destroyers]] and supporting forces made from resources secretly funneled by Sith loyalists in the executive boards of Sienar-Jaemus and Kuat-Entralla. Attempting to fuse his cultists' efforts with the First Order to create a "[[Final Order]]," the reborn Emperor received steadfast support from the "[[High Human]]" ideologue [[Allegiant General]] [[Enric Pryde]].<ref name="TROS VD" /> Kylo Ren [[Mission to Kijimi|revealed]] to Rey that she was a descendant of Palpatine, but as the Jedi student of Leia Organa and Luke Skywalker, she accepted that she was susceptible to the urges of the dark side while rejecting the burden of her blood&mdash;she instead re-affirmed her bond to her friends and the Skywalkers, including the "good" that she believed still existed in Kylo Ren and her duty as a Jedi.<ref name="TROS" />

Ren, [[Mission to Kef Bir|sensing]] the love of his dying mother and inspired by Rey, made the choice of embracing his Jedi self as Ben Solo. Poe Dameron also confronted his complicated past as a spice runner in Kijimi City, with his idealism convincing Zorii Bliss to help oppose the First Order. The Resistance additionally gained support from [[Company 77]] after Finn formed a rapport with the group&mdash;like him, they were former stormtroopers who had deserted the First Order after refusing to carry out an order to fire upon civilians and were willing to fight to stop the regime's oppression. The Resistance was impossibly outnumbered as it tried to stop the Sith Star Destroyers from launching on Exegol. However, Lando Calrissian assembled a [[Citizens' Fleet|fleet]] comprised of volunteer citizens from throughout the galaxy who heeded the call of the late Leia Organa to help the Resistance movement.<ref name="TROS" />

Among the Citizens' Fleet were unaffiliated [[fringer]]s and criminals from the anarchy of Kijimi City who championed their individual independence from politics or any authority and had taken the New Republic's limited ability to enforce interstellar law for granted; the draconian First Order roused them from apathy. Despite Kijimi's [[Occupation of Kijimi|occupation]] and [[Destruction of Kijimi|destruction]] under Pryde's command, the people of Kijimi were joined by people from other locales, including the royal-born [[Wilsa Teshlo]], a member of the matriarchal [[Veiled Sorority]] pirate fleet; the [[Caracara Squadron]] mercenaries, who lended defense services to star systems; [[Kid Malmash]] of the ''[[Nebular Kelpie]]'', transporter of political refugees;<ref name="TROS VD" /> and the residents of the ''[[Colossus]]'', a [[Supertanker fuel depot|supertanker]] home to the Resistance spy Kazuda Xiono and [[Hunt for the Colossus|hunted]] by the First Order.<ref name="TSWB" />

The people joined Rey at the [[Battle of Exegol]], and together, they defeated the Sith once and for all. The people of the galaxy, free at last, then led spontaneous [[Uprising against the First Order|uprisings]] across the galaxy, burning down the First Order with the hope of building a new future of harmony.<ref name="TROS" /> Resolving that the people of the galaxy could not afford risk another round of oppression in light of the New Republic's failure, Resistance member and historian Beaumont Kin then wrote ''[[The Rise and Fall of the Galactic Empire]]'', a book reflecting on the past decades of galactic tyranny.<ref name="Kempshall" />

==Behind the scenes==
===Love in the time of democracy===
{{Quote|We are both good and evil, and we have a choice.|George Lucas, on the premise of ''Star Wars''|<ref name="Cynical optimism">{{WebCite|url=https://www.nytimes.com/1999/03/21/movies/moviesspecial/george-lucas-im-a-cynic-who-has-hope-for-the-human-race.html|text=George Lucas: 'I'm a Cynic Who Has Hope for the Human Race'|author=[[Wikipedia:Orville Schell|Orville Schell]] (interviewer)|date=1999-03-21|work=[[Wikipedia:The New York Times|''The New York Times'']]|archivedate=20201109041642}}</ref>}}
[[File:DrStrangelove.jpg|thumb|left|300px|The 1964 political satire film ''Dr. Strangelove'' is among many cinematic influences for George Lucas.]]
''[[Star Wars]]'' was born of [[George Lucas]]' concern for democracy in the [[Wikipedia:United States|United States of America]].<ref name="Cannes 2024">{{YouTube|video=AHXLsHOdPiw|text=George Lucas talks STAR WARS, Coppola, gender/race + more from Cannes 2024|channel=@ThatShelf|name=That Shelf}}</ref> Nominally supported by the [[Wikipedia:United Nations|United Nations]] after its founding in [[1940s|1945]],<ref name="United Nations">{{WebCite|url=https://www.un.org/en/global-issues/democracy|text=Democracy|work=[[Wikipedia:United Nations|United Nations]]|archivedate=20220225183741}}</ref> democracy is commonly associated with [[Wikipedia:Liberal democracy|liberal democracies']] systems of governance that include respect for individual [[Wikipedia:Human rights|human rights]], the [[Wikipedia:Rule of law|rule of law]], the [[Wikipedia:Separation of powers|separation]] of executive, legislative, and judicial powers, and [[Wikipedia:Free and fair election|free and fair elections]].<ref name="Data">{{WebCite|url=https://ourworldindata.org/democracy|text=Democracy|work=[[Wikipedia:Our World in Data|Our World in Data]]|archivedate=20240604195004}}</ref> Released on [[May 25]], [[1977]] in the USA, [[Star Wars: Episode IV A New Hope|''Star Wars'': Episode IV ''A New Hope'']] was originally simply titled ''Star Wars'' and launches the [[original trilogy]] with a group of protagonists fighting an "evil Empire."<ref name="Archives" /> Lucas took advantage of the strong sense of good and evil in the aftermath of [[1930s|1939]]&ndash;1945's [[Wikipedia:World War II|World War II]], fought between the [[Wikipedia:Allies of World War II|Allied Powers]]&mdash;"good guys" including the USA who founded the United Nations after the war&mdash;and the [[Wikipedia:Axis powers|Axis Powers]] of [[Wikipedia:Nazi Germany|Nazi Germany]], [[Wikipedia:Fascist Italy|fascist Italy]], and [[Wikipedia:Empire of Japan|Imperial Japan]].<ref name="Wired">{{WebCite|url=https://www.wired.com/2005/05/lucasqa|text=George Lucas on ''Star Wars'', ''Fahrenheit 9/11'', and his own legacy|author=Steve Silberman|date=2005-05-01|work=[[Wikipedia:Wired (magazine)|''Wired'']]|archivedate=20240520202857}}</ref> While the Galactic Empire uses the visual motifs of Nazi Germany, the fictional regime was inspired by what Lucas terms the contemporary "[[Wikipedia:American imperialism|American Empire]]" that pursued imperialist domination behind the veneer of liberating more countries.<ref name="Archives">''[[The Star Wars Archives: Episodes I–III, 1999–2005]]''</ref> However, the director intended to avoid explicitly addressing culturally "incendiary" political and emotional issues about which people would have already formed a prejudice of opinion<ref name="Wired" />&mdash;Lucas considered this a unifying aspect of [[Wikipedia:Science fiction|science fiction]] and [[Wikipedia:Historical fiction|historical fiction]], though he preferred to call ''Star Wars'' a form of "fantasy," "[[Wikipedia:Space opera|space opera]]," or "mythology."<ref name="Fisher">{{YouTube|video=H84DLtS5ANg|text=Carrie Fisher interviews George Lucas - January 2002|channel=@blaked36|name=blaked36|date=2018-12-22}}</ref>

[[File:LucasPanavision-MOSW.png|thumb|right|350px|Lucas found himself passionate with and talented at filmmaking, which he considered to be a combination of his interests in art and anthropology.]]
While the original trilogy features the Jedi and rebels' struggle against the increasingly authoritarian Emperor Palpatine, his [[prequel trilogy]] features the fall of the Jedi and the transformation of the democratic Galactic Republic into the Empire. The conflict between good and evil is based on the mystical energy field known as the Force, which was Lucas' attempt to distill real-world religions.<ref name="Archives" /> Lucas believed that the essence of all religions is compassionate love and thus being able to "let go" rather than give in to greed,<ref name="Happiness interview">{{OfficialYouTube|video=2TdGd0MlmvI|text=George Lucas: Project Happiness Interview|channel=channel/UCOLrMxi9peCxmLA2qBUKK5w|name=Mount Madonna School Values in World Thought}}</ref> and individuals need to accept that they have both good and evil inside them, and every person in every day can choose to be compassionate and love what is good or give in to greed and make unhealthy attachments; when a person feels they have some form of power, they become afraid to lose it and may fail to let go. Lucas added that regardless of whichever belief a person has, "[[Wikipedia:First Epistle of John|God is love]]", and organized religions have a place in society to inspire people to be compassionate, but institutions can become corrupted, and "killing people in the name of God is wrong."<ref name="Bill Bradley">{{YouTube|video=S_JEkIPzl8A|text=George Lucas talks Star Wars and storytelling|channel=@BillBradley|name=Bill Bradley}}</ref> Lucas therefore used the Force, with the binary morality between good and evil, light and dark, as a narrative and symbolic tool in ''Star Wars'' as a "soap opera" about families at its heart, with wide-ranging societal repercussions in the choice between democracy and authoritarianism.<ref name="Charlie Rose full" />

===George Lucas' philosophy and American democracy===
====Evolution and education====
{{Quote|You try to keep people from being used or abused and listen to their grievances and try to do what is fair. Some people get upset because they want everything. Some people feel that they deserve to have everything, even though there's no reason for it. They just feel it's their right. That's one of the problems of a democracy. You get these individual voices that are very loud, and very dysfunctional. And if you cater to those voices, you end up with a very dysfunctional society.|George Lucas|<ref name="Cynical optimism" />}}
Defining humanity, Lucas said that human brains are capable of [[Wikipedia:Rational animal|rationality]] and could "exchange information" through, for example, narrative art. In his view, the matter of humanity's extinction or survival depends on whether such information is accurate, and ''Star Wars'' operates on the premise that "we are both good and evil, and we have a choice."<ref name="Cynical optimism" /> Lucas considered democracy a human choice<ref name="CBS 2012">{{WebCite|url=https://www.cbsnews.com/8301-505271_162-57362574/george-lucas-billionaire-down-on-capitalism|text=George Lucas: Billionaire down on capitalism|date=2012-01-20|work=[[Wikipedia:CBS|CBS]]|archivedate=20120121002534|nolive=1}}</ref> and deemed the [[Wikipedia:Adaptation|evolutionary adaptation]] of rationality to be God's blessing for humanity; for him, humans have a "duty" to improve education and use [[Wikipedia:Scientific method|scientific inquiry]] to make sense of reality&mdash;being personally interested in the potential for [[Wikipedia:Neuroanthropology|neuro-anthropology]] to study the development of social systems, belief systems, and the psychological motifs that he claimed have been embedded in human stories for millennia<ref name="Boston Lucas">{{WebCite|url=http://www.boston.com/ae/movies/lucas_interview|text=George Lucas Interview - Ty Burr - Star Wars - Museum of Science|author=[[Wikipedia:Ty Burr|Ty Burr]]|date=2006-08-16|work=[[Wikipedia:Museum of Science (Boston)|Museum of Science, Boston]]|archivedate=20130114022203}}</ref>&mdash;and the best way to live is to help each other, not just morally or emotionally for the "[[Wikipedia:Life, Liberty and the pursuit of Happiness|pursuit of happiness]]" but also economically and existentially because the survival of humanity is always stake, comparable to how [[Wikipedia:Hunter-gatherer|hunter-gatherer]] communities survived and then formed distinct societies and built cities.<ref name="AAA" /> Lucas lamented in 2012 that the dominance of capital and sensational mass media have been making American society more dysfunctional, antithetical to the values of democracy and compassion "that we supposedly stand for."<ref name="CBS 2012" /> When asked by his friend and journalist [[Wikipedia:Charlie Rose|Charlie Rose]] whether his ideal society of compassion is "democracy" or "[[Wikipedia:Socialism|socialism]]," Lucas called it "common sense."<ref name="Ideal society">{{OfficialYouTube|video=Ps8DDjjSRxQ|text=George Lucas details his ideal society|channel=channel/UC8p1vwvWtl6T73JiExfWs1g|name=CBS News}}</ref> Lucas believed that humanity's emotional underpinnings have not changed, though the intellectual contexts have.<ref name="Boston Lucas" /> According to Lucas, although his films are largely shaped by his experiences of society from the 1950s to 1970s, ''Star Wars'' could be seen as a form of "psychological archaeology" that explores from a humanist perspective the "ageless" and "universal" theme of conflict between humanity's intellectual-technological advancements and humanity's reliance on the "same" emotional driving points&mdash;aside from "the only other two universal themes" of violence and sex.<ref name="Cynical optimism" />

[[File:AmericanGraffitiLucas.jpg|thumb|left|350px|George Lucas filming ''American Graffiti'' (1973). His next project was ''Star Wars''.]]
Lucas opined in his conversation with [[Wikipedia:Sinologist|Sinologist]] [[Wikipedia:Orville Schell|Orville Schell]] in [[1999]] that the American media has a tendency to uncritically "devour" information&mdash;the filmmaker believed that one problem of democracy was its vulnerability to cater to loud, irrational, and greedy voices, which would prevent society from achieving consensus and render it dysfunctional. The director emphasized his belief that societies cannot exist without common faith. He also acknowledged that his notion that the media should be mindful of their [[Wikipedia:Social responsibility|social responsibilities]] has been taken to the extremity of censorship in Communist regimes such as the Soviet Union, which collapsed in [[1991]], and the People's Republic of China.<ref name="Cynical optimism" /> Speaking with Charlie Rose in [[2015]], Lucas nevertheless expressed appreciation that filmmakers he knew in Soviet Russia had creative freedom to make any film as long as it did not cross political redlines while an American filmmaker was bound by studios that wanted every production to be profitable&mdash;Lucas hated to be told what to do<ref name="Charlie Rose full" /> and greatly admired [[Wikipedia:Cinema of the Soviet Union#Art house/experimental|Soviet experimental cinema]] prioneered by [[Wikipedia:Sergei Eisenstein|Sergei Eisenstein]] in the 1920s. Speaking with journalist [[Wikipedia:Richard Corliss|Richard Corliss]] in [[2006]], Lucas said wanted to experiment with moving images after ''Star Wars'' and said that his "heart" was with the non-mainstream, European government&ndash;subsidized films of [[Wikipedia:Jean-Luc Godard|Jean-Luc Godard]] and [[Wikipedia:Federico Fellini|Federico Fellini]], and he thought of his ''Star Wars'' earnings as his "own little government subsidy."<ref name="Corliss" />

When speaking about the themes of ''Star Wars'' in light of [[2004]]'s [[Wikipedia:2004 United States presidential election|US presidential election]], Lucas said that the "current mess" was partly due to a lack of proper studying of the humanities and interdisciplinary learning, the "why" that storytellers like [[Wikipedia:William Shakespeare|William Shakespeare]] explore when inviting people to study the emotional and psychological motives behind conquerors, whether it be [[Wikipedia:Alexander the Great|Alexander the Great]], [[Wikipedia:Julius Caesar|Julius Caesar]], [[Wikipedia:Napoleon Bonaparte|Napoleon Bonaparte]], or [[Wikipedia:Adolf Hitler|Adolf Hitler]].
