While the essays were written from the perspectives of their respective authors, they were based on back-and-forth communication with Rinzler as an intermediary with Lucas, who listed various subjects that "became the nucleus for all the essays" and set aside some that were more concerned with political science for a potential future book.<ref name="SW History Rinzler">{{SW|url=news/star-wars-and-history|text=''Star Wars and History''}}</ref> A 263-page unpublished book attributed to Lucasfilm and the Wiley publishing house is listed under the title "Star Wars Politics" and dated [[April 15]], 2013 on [[Wikipedia:Google Books|Google Books]].<ref name="Star Wars Politics">{{WebCite|url=https://books.google.co.uk/books/about/Star_Wars_Politics.html?id=_V21mwEACAAJ|text=Star Wars Politics - Lucasfilm|work=[[Wikipedia:Google Books|Google Books]]|archivedate=20250219162946}}</ref>

[[File:BankingClanIntRate-POP.png|thumb|left|360px|Still of Senator Amidala urging Senator Organa to make a speech about galactic finance in "Pursuit of Peace," an episode that George Lucas wanted to address banking deregulation in light of the Global Financial Crisis.]]
In January 2013, the [[Wikipedia:Presidency of Barack Obama|Obama Administration]] rejected a 34,435-strong [[Wikipedia:We the People (petitioning system)|public petition]] for beginning construction on a Death Star because the administration was concerned with budget deficits and "does not support blowing up planets," instead listing the alternative US space-based science, technology, engineering, and math-related achievements and developments in both public and private sectors.<ref name="DS petition">{{WebCite|url=https://petitions.whitehouse.gov/petition/secure-resources-and-funding-and-begin-construction-death-star-2016/wlfKzFkN|text=Secure resources and funding, and begin construction of a Death Star by 2016.|work=[[Wikipedia:We the People (petitioning system)|We the People]]|archivedate=20140801173708}}</ref> The [[The Clone Wars: Season Three|Season Three]] episodes "[[Heroes on Both Sides]]" and "[[Pursuit of Peace]]" of ''The Clone Wars'' aired in 2010, addressing banking deregulation at George Lucas' behest<ref name="SWS 20200430">{{StarWarsShow|4NgPKSzR0LU|George Lucas and Dave Filoni Talk The Clone Wars, Plus Anthony Daniels Stops By!|sw_url=video/george-lucas-and-dave-filoni-talk-the-clone-wars-plus-anthony-daniels-stops-by}}</ref>&mdash;the production schedule of the television series meant they were written three years prior<ref name="ForceCast S4 Premiere">{{WebCite|url=http://forcecast.net/story/home/Special_Report_Clone_Wars_Season_4_Premiere_Part_II_140988.asp|text=ForceCast.net: Special Report: Clone Wars Season 4 Premiere, Part II|work=ForceCast.net|archivedate=20190419012740}}</ref>&mdash;in 2007, when the [[Wikipedia:2008 financial crisis|Global Financial Crisis]] occurred and laid the ground for democratic backsliding worldwide.<ref name="Empire of Democracy">{{BookCite|author=Reid-Henry, Simon|title=[[Wikipedia:Empire of Democracy|''Empire of Democracy'']]|publisher=John Murray|date=2019}}</ref>

George Lucas handpicked [[Kathleen Kennedy]], a film producer who had collaborated closely with him and Steven Spielberg, to lead Lucasfilm following his retirement. Kennedy Kennedy encouraged artists of ''Star Wars'' to make their work personal, noting the earnest authenticity of Lucas and Spielberg's films and particularly Lucas' "increasing need to use politics" in the ''Star Wars'' prequels to express his ideas in light of real-world events.<ref name="Kathleen TFA" /> Lucas' rough plan for the [[sequel trilogy]] was to take ideas from the aftermath of the Iraq War&mdash;"okay, you fought the war, you killed everybody, now what are you going to do?"&mdash;and how rebuilding is always harder than rebelling or fighting a war, with stormtrooper insurgents acting as "[[Wikipedia:Islamic terrorism|Saddam Hussein's Ba'athist fighters that joined ISIS and kept on fighting]]" in a corner of the galaxy while gangsters take advantage of the chaos and unite under Maul and [[Darth Talon#Behind the scenes|his apprentice]] to battle Leia Organa's New Republic and Luke Skywalker's Jedi.<ref name="Lucas sequels">{{WebCite|url=https://www.syfy.com/syfy-wire/george-lucas-third-star-wars-trilogy-darth-maul|text=George Lucas' third Star Wars trilogy would have made Darth Maul the big bad along with a 'new Darth Vader'|author=Josh Weiss|date=2020-11-23|work=[[Wikipedia:Syfy|Syfy.com]]|archivedate=20211102234112}}</ref>

Under Kennedy, the [[Lucasfilm Story Group]] was established to guide ''Star Wars'' storytellers in keeping to George Lucas' values and the thematic framework of ''Star Wars''.<ref name="Pablo interview">{{StarWarsShow|GvT2F1j-SvA|Pablo Hidalgo Extended Interview}}</ref> During an interview with Disney's CEO [[Bob Iger (chief executive officer)|Bob Iger]] and ''[[Star Wars: The Mandalorian]]'' creator [[Jon Favreau]] about Lucasfilm's franchise and parent company, Iger was pressed on America's political debate on whether "excessive wealth" is "corrosive to democracy" in 2019. Iger replied that big companies like Disney have a responsibility to alleviate the societal costs of income inequality and that it was for the country as a whole to review taxation policy.<ref name="Iger 2019">{{WebCite|url=https://www.vanityfair.com/video/watch/bob-iger-and-jon-favreau-talk-the-mandalorian-disney-and-george-lucas|text=Bob Iger and Jon Favreau Talk “The Mandalorian,” Disney+, and George Lucas|date=2019-10-22|work=[[Wikipedia:Vanity Fair (magazine)|''Vanity Fair'']]|archivedate=20210725170136}}</ref>

====Humanitarian efforts====
The [[501st Legion (fan organization)|501st Legion]] was founded in 1997 as a ''Star Wars'' fan and volunteer group in support of charitable work worldwide.<ref name="501st">{{SW|url=news/from-world-war-to-star-wars-stormtroopers|text=From World War to ''Star Wars'': Stormtroopers|nolive=1}}</ref> Other groups include the [[Rebel Legion]] and the [[Mandalorian Mercs]].<ref name="RebelLegion">{{SW|url=news/history-of-the-rebel-legion-part-1|text=Celebrating the Good Guys: The History of the Rebel Legion|nolive=1}}</ref> [[Lily Nyamwasa|Lily Meszaros]]&mdash;who played the Jedi Council member [[Stass Allie]] in 2005's ''Revenge of the Sith''&mdash;fled from Rwanda following the genocide in 1994 to [[Wikipedia:Australia|Australia]]. She set up the Rwandan Orphans Assistance Response to provide aid for children in Rwanda,<ref name="FT Rwanda">"Rwandan finds new hope in Australia after the genocide" by Sebag-Montefiore, Clarissa on [https://www.ft.com Financial Times] ([[November 7]], [[2014]]) {{C|subscription access only}}</ref> raising funds partly by signing ''Star Wars'' autographs.<ref name="Interview Rwanda">{{WebCite|url=https://starwarsinterviews.com/prequel-trilogy/episode-ii-attack-of-the-clones/lily-nyamwasa-stass-allie|text=Lily Nyamwasa (Stass Allie)|author=[[Dennis Pellegrom]]|date=2006|work=[[Star Wars Interviews]]|archivedate=20230205201431|nolive=1}}</ref>

In [[2014]], Disney and Lucasfilm created the ''Star Wars: Force for Change'' initiative in partnership with [[J.J. Abrams]]' [[Bad Robot Productions]] to support [[Wikipedia:UNICEF|UNICEF]], the United Nations Children's Fund, during the production of the 2015 sequel trilogy movie, [[Star Wars: Episode VII The Force Awakens|''Star Wars'': Episode VII ''The Force Awakens'']]. The campaign benefitted the UNICEF Innovation Fund, which was founded by Disney/Lucasfilm and the government of [[Wikipedia:Denmark|Denmark]] to support new Innovation Lab projects. Between [[May 21]] and [[July 25]], ''Star Wars'' fans were asked to contribute in exchange for a chance to appear in Episode VII, and the partnership raised over $4.2 million [[Wikipedia:United States dollar|USD]] from 125 countries in addition to Disney's donation of $1 million USD.<ref name="UNICEF 2014">{{WebCite|url=https://www.unicef.org/innovation/media/1396/file/UNICEF%20Innovation:%20%20Annual%20Report%202014.pdf|text=UNICEF Innovation: Annual Report 2014|work=[[Wikipedia:UNICEF|UNICEF]]|archivedate=20231204084657}}</ref> In 2016, UNICEF and ''Force for Change'' delivered the [[Wikipedia:Myanmar|Myanmar]] Social Innovation Lab<ref name="Myanmar">{{WebCite|url=https://www.unicef.org/innovation/stories/star-wars-force-change-supports-myanmar-innovation-lab|text=Star Wars: Force For Change Supports Myanmar Innovation Lab|date=2016-07-22|work=[[Wikipedia:UNICEF|UNICEF]]|archivedate=20230922062822}}</ref> and a digital care protection system in Cambodia.<ref name="Cambodia">{{WebCite|url=https://www.unicef.org/innovation/stories/star-wars-force-change-supports-digital-monitoring|text=Star Wars: Force For Change Supports Digital Monitoring|date=2016-10-05|work=[[Wikipedia:UNICEF|UNICEF]]|archivedate=20201125173329}}</ref>

In April 2015, Lucasfilm's ''Force for Change'' was announced as a presenting sponsor with [[Wikipedia:U.S. Fund for UNICEF|UNICEF USA]] for the UNICEF Kid Power program, which encouraged children in the US to be active and raise funds for nutrition programs for malnourished children around the world.<ref name="UNICEF Kid Power">{{WebCite|url=https://www.unicefusa.org/stories/force-change-lucasfilm-disney-work-fight-world-malnutrition|text=Force for Change: Lucasfilm & Disney Work to Fight World Malnutrition|author=Maryanne Buechner|date=2017-04-28|work=[[Wikipedia:U.S. Fund for UNICEF|UNICEF USA]]|archivedate=20230927182341}}</ref> [[Ashley Eckstein]]&mdash;who voiced Ahsoka Tano in the television series ''The Clone Wars'' and ''[[Star Wars Rebels]]''&mdash;took part in UNICEF's Kid Power program to share the group's work in Afghanistan, [[Wikipedia:Haiti|Haiti]], and the [[Wikipedia:Dominican Republic|Dominican Republic]] with exclusive content for [[Star Wars Rebels Season Three|''Rebels'' Season Three]].<ref name="Rebels Kid Power">{{WebCite|url=https://www.unicefkidpower.org/star-wars-force-for-change-mission2|text=Star Wars Force for Change Mission 2|work=[[Wikipedia:UNICEF|UNICEF]]|archivedate=20161202082232}}</ref><!-- https://www.unicef.org/innovation/stories/ban-ki-moon-visits-innovation --> In 2017, ''Force for Change'' raised funds for both UNICEF USA and [[Wikipedia:Starlight Children's Foundation|Starlight Children's Foundation]].<ref name="2017 fund">{{WebCite|url=https://www.unicefusa.org/stories/win-three-epic-star-wars-experiences-and-support-unicef/32130|text=Win Three Epic Star Wars Experiences and Support UNICEF!|date=2017-04-11|work=[[Wikipedia:U.S. Fund for UNICEF|UNICEF USA]]|archivedate=20200807001355}}</ref> In 2017, the Hong Kong Committee for UNICEF partnered with Disney for the city's first "''Star Wars'' Run" raising funds for children worldwide. In 2018, UNICEF Hong Kong likewise partnered with ''Force for Change'', the 501st Legion fan group, and local companies for a "''Star Wars'' Dress Up Day" on May 4, ''Star Wars'' Day with ''Star Wars'' merchandise in conjunction with marketing for the film ''[[Solo: A Star Wars Story]]'', which was released in [[May 24]].<ref name="2018 fund">{{WebCite|url=https://unicef.org.hk/en/news/latest_news/first-star-wars-dress-up-day-comes-to-hong-kong-on-may-4|text=First “Star Wars Dress Up Day” comes to Hong Kong on May 4　New “Star Wars” Limited-Edition Keepsakes to Help Raise funds for UNICEF　Dress Up to Celebrate May 4 ‧ For Every Child|author=[[Wikipedia:UNICEF|The Hong Kong Committee for UNICEF]]|date=2018-04-12|archivedate=20250220114230}}</ref>

During the [[Wikipedia:Polaris Dawn|Polaris Dawn]] spaceflight in September 2024, astronaut [[Wikipedia:Sarah Gillis|Sarah Gillis]] played John Williams' violin solo of "[[Rey's Theme]]" in Earth's orbit with the accompaniment of orchestras in [[Wikipedia:Brazil|Brazil]], Haiti, [[Wikipedia:Sweden|Sweden]], Uganda, [[Wikipedia:Boston|Boston]] and [[Wikipedia:Los Angeles|Los Angeles]] in the US, and [[Wikipedia:Venezuela|Venezuela]]. The event was a partnership between John Williams, [[Wikipedia:SpaceX|SpaceX's]] [[Wikipedia:Polaris program|Polaris Program]], [[Wikipedia:El Sistema|El Sistema]] music-education program, and [[Wikipedia:St. Jude Children's Research Hospital|St. Jude Children's Research Hospital]].<ref name="Polaris">{{Twitter|PolarisProgram|status/1834557770374296010|[[Wikipedia:Polaris program|Polaris]]|date=2024-09-13|archivedate=20240913151937}}</ref>

====A new era of storytelling====
{{Quote|And so if you make a timeless story like ''Star Wars'', then it's always going to feel relevant. ''[&hellip;]'' I just think that we are constantly accusing each other of that ''[corruption]'' and trying to overthrow them and become powerful ourselves. It's this never-ending thing.|Gareth Edwards|<ref name="Edwards">{{WebCite|url=https://www.ign.com/articles/2016/12/05/star-wars-director-gareth-edwards-on-the-politics-of-rogue-one|text=Star Wars: Director Gareth Edwards on the Politics of Rogue One|author=Jim Vejvoda|date=2016-12-05|work=[[Wikipedia:IGN|IGN]]|archivedate=20161206103132}}</ref>}}
[[File:First_Order_Military.jpg|thumb|left|350px|Neo-fascist stormtroopers salute in ''The Force Awakens'' with ''Star Wars''{{'}} return to the silver screen in 2015.]]
J.J. Abrams and Lawrence Kasdan wrote the story of ''The Force Awakens'' based on the existing ''Star Wars'' setting and characters as part of Lucas' tale of hope against the backdrop of cynicism following the Vietnam War. Abrams and Kasdan, the latter of whom also worked on the scripts of the original trilogy, therefore extended the story of "light versus dark" and good against evil rather than make real-world geopolitical or social allegories, though Abrams acknowledged that the rise of the First Order against a benevolent New Republic could be comparable to forces happening then in the real-world.<ref name="Abrams politics">{{OfficialYouTube|channel=@IGN|name=IGN|link=Wikipedia:IGN|video=NlPdkqeTbvM|text=Star Wars: The Force Awakens - J.J. Abrams Explains the Political State of The Force Awakens|date=2015-12-17}}</ref> The Order itself was borne of story conversations that included speculation on what might have happened if the Nazis had all [[Wikipedia:Ratlines (World War II)|fled]] to and regrouped in [[Wikipedia:Argentina|Argentina]].<ref name="Abrams Argentina">{{WebCite|url=http://www.empireonline.com/news/story.asp?NID=45203|text=JJ Abrams Spills Details On Kylo Ren|author=James Dyer|date=2015-08-25|work=[[Wikipedia:Empire (magazine)|''Empire Magazine'']]|archivedate=20151018205158}}</ref> [[Andy Serkis]], the actor of First Order Supreme Leader Snoke, described the character as a "totalitarian leader" in December 2017.<ref name="Totalitarian">{{WebCite|url=https://6abc.com/post/sharrie-williams-interviews-star-wars-villains/2785755|text=Sharrie Williams interviews Star Wars villains!|author=Sharrie Williams|date=2017-12-15|work=[[Wikipedia:WPVI-TV|WPVI-TV]]|archivedate=20250903181614}}</ref>

Commenting on the television series ''Star Wars Rebels'', which aired between 2014 and [[2018]] with himself as executive producer, Dave Filoni discussed politics and evil as a complex matter, comparing the real world's history of democracies collapsing into dictatorships with how a beautiful tree could rot from within and thus, in ''Star Wars'', making everyone a pawn of Palpatine "even without the Empire." Filoni said that the theme of ''Star Wars'' is in Luke Skywalker's message&mdash;to "not to be of yourself but to be selfless," finding enlightenment and then inspiring others to act too.<ref name="Filoni">{{WebCite|url=https://www.animationmagazine.net/2018/02/director-dave-filoni-on-star-wars-rebels-the-journey-and-the-legacy|text=Director Dave Filoni on 'Star Wars Rebels': The Journey and the Legacy|author=Cameron Koller|date=2018-02-23|work=[[Wikipedia:Animation Magazine|''Animation Magazine'']]|archivedate=20230203173532}}</ref> The first novel of the new canon continuity, [[A New Dawn (novel)|''A New Dawn'']], was written by [[John Jackson Miller]] and published in September 2014 as a prequel to ''Rebels'', which premiered in October. Miller likened the ''Star Wars'' galaxy's rapid increase of military production within a period of twenty years under the Empire to [[Wikipedia:Collectivization in the Soviet Union|collectivization in the Soviet Union]], which turned a predominantly agrarian [[Wikipedia:Russia|Russia]] and the nations it occupied into an industrial-military powerhouse at the cost of "over 20 million" lives in the interwar period. The author also drew from his experience in studying totalitarian governments states in [[Wikipedia:Comparative politics|comparative politics]] for how the Empire adopts and transforms pre-existing Republic institutions. Miller further compared the fact that the Empire's surveillance apparatus had developed before Palpatine's time to how "surveillance of various kinds has gone on for decades, for centuries," under many governments and commercial entities in the real world, made more apparent with the [[Wikipedia:2010s global surveillance disclosures|Edward Snowden leaks]] in 2013.<ref name="Dawn JJM">{{WebCite|url=https://www.dailydot.com/parsec/star-wars-author-john-jackson-miller-interview|text='Star Wars' author talks about writing the first novel in Disney's new canon|author=Eric Geller|date=2014-09-02|work=[[Wikipedia:The Daily Dot|''The Daily Dot'']]|archivedate=20160930115147}}</ref>

The 2015 comic miniseries ''[[Star Wars: Shattered Empire]]'' was written by [[Greg Rucka]] exploring the immediate aftermath of the Empire's defeat in ''Return of the Jedi'', and the author researched propaganda and political turmoil in the [[Wikipedia:Arab Spring|Arab Spring]] uprisings of the early 2010s, the [[Wikipedia:Eastern Bloc|Soviet Bloc]] that collapsed in a [[Wikipedia:Revolutions of 1989|wave of eastern European revolutions]] in [[1989]], and the totalitarian state of [[Wikipedia:North Korea|North Korea]].<ref name="Greg Rucka">{{SW|url=news/the-new-galaxy-of-star-wars-shattered-empire-an-interview-with-greg-rucka|text=The New Galaxy of ''Star Wars: Shattered Empire'': An Interview with Greg Rucka}}</ref> [[Adam Lance Garcia]] likewise wrote the [[2023]] short story "[[The Veteran]]," set during the riots following the Battle of Endor, with historical events such as the Arab Spring and the [[Wikipedia:1989 Tiananmen Square protests and massacre|Tiananmen Square protests and massacres]] of 1989 in mind.<ref name="Veteran">{{WebCite|url=https://castbox.fm/episode/The-Veteran---Adam-Lance-Garcia-Interview-id3760111-id632332420|text=The Veteran - Adam Lance Garcia Interview|author=[https://www.sithtyminutes.com Sithty Minutes]|work=[[Wikipedia:Castbox|Castbox]]|archivedate=20250226212537}}</ref>

The [[2016]] novel [[Bloodline (novel)|''Bloodline'']] by [[Claudia Gray]] explores the political debate between the Centrists and Populists toward the end of the New Republic Era, and Gray, having watched the musical [[Wikipedia:Hamilton (musical)|''Hamilton'']] while writing the novel, was inclined to agree with a reader's observation that ''Bloodline''{{'s}} politics echoes the political debate between [[Wikipedia:Alexander Hamilton|Alexander Hamilton's]] Federalists and [[Wikipedia:Anti-Federalism|Anti-Federalists]] over ratifying the [[Wikipedia:Constitution of the United States|US Constitution]] that clearly defined a [[Wikipedia:Federal government of the United States|federal government]] with certain central powers in 1787 and 1788.<ref name="Gray federalism">{{OfficialYouTube|video=1MQw7-ZCNpc|text=Jedi Council Interview With Claudia Gray (Author of Bloodline, Lost Stars)|channel=@ColliderLadiesNight|name=Collider Ladies Night|date=2016-05-05}}</ref> The 2016 reference title ''[[Star Wars Propaganda: A History of Persuasive Art in the Galaxy]]'' by [[Pablo Hidalgo]] includes the Declaration of Rebellion, a document written by Mon Mothma on behalf of the Rebel Alliance,<ref name="Propaganda" /> which originated in [[Paul Murphy]]'s 1990 ''Star Wars'' Legends roleplaying book ''[[The Rebel Alliance Sourcebook]]''.<ref name="TRAS">''[[The Rebel Alliance Sourcebook]]''</ref> The text borrows phrases from the [[Wikipedia:United States Declaration of Independence|United States Declaration of Independence]] of 1776.<ref name="US Declaration">{{WebCite|url=https://www.archives.gov/founding-docs/declaration-transcript|text=Declaration of Independence: A Transcription|work=[[Wikipedia:National Archives and Records Administration|National Archives (US)]]|archivedate=20250124221707}}</ref>

[[File:Rogue_One_AMC_IMAX_MiniPoster_-2.jpg|thumb|right|280px|The creators of ''Rogue One'' credited characters' relationships in face of the cycles of corruption with audiences' sense of resonance.]]
George Lucas and J. J. Abrams supported the [[Wikipedia:Hillary Clinton|Hillary Clinton]] campaign in the November 2016 [[Wikipedia:2016 United States presidential election|US presidential election]] that [[Wikipedia:Donald Trump|Donald Trump]] won.<ref name="2016USelection">{{WebCite|url=https://variety.com/2016/biz/news/hillary-clinton-donald-trump-hollywood-1201878938|text=Clinton vs. Trump in Hollywood: Who’s Giving|work=[[Wikipedia:Variety (magazine)|''Variety'']]|archivedate=20250713133226}}</ref> When asked whether the [[Star Wars Anthology Series|anthology]] film ''[[Rogue One: A Star Wars Story]]'', which was released in [[December]] that year, is significant to "recent political events," director [[Gareth Edwards]] replied that the movie was written in mid-2014 and echoed Lucas' belief that storytelling reflects a "human condition" that remains constant throghout human history; a "timeless" saga such as ''Star Wars'' would always resonate with contemporary events given Edwards' belief that humanity is a cycle of revolution against someone in power deemed to be corrupt.<ref name="Edwards" /> [[Felicity Jones]], who played the heroine Jyn Erso, stressed in an interview in the same month that the core of ''Star Wars'' films is rooted in characters' relationships&mdash;in ''Rogue One''{{'s}} case between father and daughter, where the filmmakers intended for viewers regardless of gender to want to be Jyn Erso as the main protagonist&mdash;which in turn make audiences want the characters to "succeed against the forces of evil."<ref name="Felicity Belfast">{{WebCite|url=https://www.belfasttelegraph.co.uk/entertainment/film-tv/felicity-jones-we-love-star-wars-because-were-fond-of-the-characters-and-want-them-to-succeed-against-forces-of-evil/35297932.html|text=Felicity Jones: 'We love Star Wars because we're fond of the characters and want them to succeed against forces of evil'|author=Gemma Dunn|date=2016-12-16|work=[[Wikipedia:Belfast Telegraph|Belfast Telegraph]]|archiveurl=https://archive.is/S6ANr}}</ref> Jones also appreciated that Jyn Erso's focus on her survival and mission rather than having a sexualized appearance fulfilled her perspective on feminism.<ref name="Felicity Glamour">{{WebCite|url=https://www.glamour.com/story/felicity-jones-glamour-january-cover-interview|text=Felicity Jones Reveals Why Her ''Rogue One: A Star Wars Story'' Character Won't Be Sexualized|author=Karen Valby|date=2016-11-29|work=[[Wikipedia:Glamour (magazine)|''Glamour'']]|archivedate=20181004154307}}</ref> ''Rogue One'' writer and second unit director [[Tony Gilroy]] recalled in 2025 that the film's post-production at [[Skywalker Sound]] took place in a sad atmosphere in the days following the [[Wikipedia:2016 United States elections|US election]] results in November 2016, and it was particularly surreal and sad for him to attend President [[Wikipedia:Barack Obama|Barack Obama's]] final [[Wikipedia:Christmas|Christmas]] party in the [[Wikipedia:White House|White House]], to which ''Rogue One'' crew members were invited.<ref name="Lovett Gilroy">{{YouTube|video=pWjbFeHkgoc|text=Tony Gilroy on What Andor Says About Trump’s Fragile Authoritarian Regime|channel=@lovettorleaveitpodcast|name=Lovett or Leave It|date=2025-08-24}}</ref>

===Escapism and nostalgia, or confronting oppression?===
====Rights in the West: democratic backsliding====
=====The diversity debate=====
In early November 2016, ''Rogue One'' writer [[Chris Weitz]] posted comments on [[Wikipedia:Twitter|Twitter]] against Donald Trump following his election victory and added that the Empire in ''Star Wars'' is a "[[Wikipedia:White supremacy|white supremacist]] (human) organization," prompting retaliatory online criticism. When Disney CEO Bob Iger was asked for comment at ''Rogue One''{{'s}} world premiere, he dismissed the media's "story" and asserted that the film makes no "political statements" whatsoever. Iger further said the company was proud of ''Rogue One''{{'s}} "diverse" casting, "and that is not a political statement, at all."<ref name="Iger RO">{{WebCite|url=https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/movies/movie-features/disneys-bob-iger-rogue-one-are-no-political-statements-955047|text=Disney's Bob Iger on 'Rogue One': "There Are No Political Statements In It"|author=Mia Galuppo|date=2016-12-12|work=[[Wikipedia:The Hollywood Reporter|The Hollywood Reporter]]|archivedate=20230612050710}}</ref>

When journalist [[Wikipedia:Steven Thrasher|Steven W. Thrasher]] interviewed [[Riz Ahmed]]&mdash;who played the Imperial-turned-rebel pilot [[Bodhi Rook]] in ''Rogue One''&mdash;earlier in July and asked him about the issue of diversity, the actor was happy that Lucasfilm was normalizing diversity in ''Star Wars'' yet, when asked about being known as the first [[Wikipedia:Cultural Muslims|Muslim]] ''Star Wars'' actor and the loud negative reaction against John Boyega's leading role as a "black stormtrooper," Ahmed described the situation as "strange"&mdash;"I can understand when John Boyega bristled at that kind of description, because it's putting you back in a pigeonhole." Ahmed said that much of life as an artist "is to transcend the labels people impose on you," but audiences could also feel a strong sense of resonance with another person's appearance, identity, and life experience. As to discussions on how art might be "political," the interviewer mentioned that the label was commonly applied to "[[Wikipedia:Person of color|artists of color]]," but Ahmed said that it has more to do with [[Wikipedia:Social class|class distinctions]] in the [[Wikipedia:British people|British]] context&mdash;he thought that the interviewer could be more focused on racial cleavages as an [[Wikipedia:Americans|American]] compared to himself, a Briton who is thus more aware of class cleavages.<ref name="Ahmed RO">{{WebCite|url=https://www.theguardian.com/culture/2016/jul/07/riz-ahmed-the-night-of-rogue-one-star-wars|text=Riz Ahmed: 'Life is about transcending the labels people impose on you'|author=[[Wikipedia:Steven Thrasher|Steven W. Thrasher]]|date=2016-07-07|work=[[Wikipedia:The Guardian|''The Guardian'']]|archivedate=20230413193425}}</ref> In February [[2017]], Ahmed raised funds in aid of [[Wikipedia:Syrian civil war|war-torn]] [[Wikipedia:Syria|Syria's]] refugees with signed ''Star Wars'' memorabilia in light of the [[Wikipedia:|fall of Aleppo]] to the [[Wikipedia:Ba'athist Syria|Assad regime]] in late 2016.<ref name="Syria appeal">{{WebCite|url=https://slate.com/culture/2017/02/riz-ahmed-and-the-night-of-costar-john-turturro-raise-money-for-syria.html|text=Riz Ahmed Recruits His Itchy The Night Of Lawyer John Turturro to Raise Money for Syrian Refugees|author=Marissa Martinelli|work=[[Wikipedia:Slate (magazine)|''Slate'']]|archivedate=20250211003234}}</ref>

=====Women's rights and LGBTQIA+ representation=====
{{Quote|I like that in the ''Star Wars'' world it's rather subtle, but you're seeing a woman as a leader who's not trying to lead as a man. ''[Rian Johnson is]'' saying something that's been a true challenge in feminism. Are we going to lead and be who we are as women in our femininity? Or are we going to dress up in a boy's clothes to do the boy's job? I think we're waking up to what we want feminism to look like.|Laura Dern, speaking about ''The Last Jedi'' in an interview in 2017|<ref name="Laura Dern VF" />}}
[[File:SWC17-Kelly-John-Daisy.jpg|thumb|left|320px|Kelly Marie Tran, John Boyega, and Daisy Ridley]]
Director [[Rian Johnson]] of the December 2017 sequel movie, [[Star Wars: Episode VIII The Last Jedi|''Star Wars'': Episode VIII ''The Last Jedi'']], was asked about whether it was influenced by nascent [[Wikipedia:Political polarization in the United States|political polarization]] under [[Wikipedia:Presidency of Donald Trump|Donald Trump's presidency]], and Johnson similarly spoke of ''Star Wars'' movies as never being consciously "conceived as a political allegory." He believed audiences apply ''Star Wars'' on political situations of the day because Lucas' stories have a universal and archetypal power;<ref name="Johnson">{{WebCite|url=https://www.fastcompany.com/40509282/the-last-jedi-director-rian-johnson-on-how-politics-influences-star-wars|text="The Last Jedi" director Rian Johnson on how politics influences "Star Wars"|author=Joe Berkowitz|date=2017-12-18|work=[[Wikipedia:Fast Company|''Fast Company'']]|archivedate=20171219020713}}</ref> [[Laura Dern]] said that when she and Johnson first discussed her role as Amilyn Holdo, the director spoke earnestly about the power of ''Star Wars'' to explore the complexity of being human, making sense of light and dark and struggles with family via characters and the "hero's journey." Dern was especially pleased that Holdo has a feminine physicality<ref name="Laura Dern hop">{{WebCite|url=https://asthebunnyhops.com/laura-dern|text=Laura Dern Discusses Her Role As Amilyn Holdo In Star Wars: The Last Jedi|work=As The Bunny Hops|archivedate=20181012071402}}</ref> and said that her character reflected "a true challenge in feminism. Are we going to lead and be who we are as women in our femininity? Or are we going to dress up in a boy's clothes to do the boy's job? I think we're waking up to what we want feminism to look like."<ref name="Laura Dern VF" />

Both Laura Dern and [[Oscar Isaac]], who played Poe Dameron, said in an interview that they had heard from Rian Johnson about their characters' depiction in ''Star Wars'' media beyond the movies and were vaguely aware about Holdo being a "very sexual character"&mdash;the September 2017 Claudia Gray novel ''[[Leia, Princess of Alderaan]]'' had mentioned that Holdo was attracted to species and genders beyond just humanoid humans<ref name="Leia" />&mdash;and fan enthusiasm over whether Dameron could be a [[w:c:lgbtqia:LGBTQIA+|LGBT]] character. Isaac was glad that "people can see themselves in a hero" from a broad range of personal identities including LGBT and [[Wikipedia:Hispanic and Latino Americans|Latino]], even if the story does not necessarily nail down any particular interpretation.<ref name="Dern Isaac">{{OfficialYouTube|video=VNWGSy2wrrI|text=Oscar Isaac & Laura Dern on 'Star Wars: The Last Jedi', LGBT Representation, and More|channel=@ColliderLadiesNight|name=Collider Ladies Night|date=2017-12-04}}</ref> Censors in [[Wikipedia:Singapore|Singapore]] and the [[Wikipedia:United Arab Emirates|United Arab Emirates]] cut a brief [[w:c:lgbtqia:Lesbian|lesbian]] kiss from local releases of [[Star Wars: Episode IX The Rise of Skywalker|''Star Wars'': Episode IX ''The Rise of Skywalker'']] in 2019.<ref name="TROS censor">{{WebCite|url=https://www.nbcnews.com/feature/nbc-out/fleeting-lesbian-kiss-star-wars-cut-singapore-dubai-releases-n1107516|text=Fleeting lesbian kiss in 'Star Wars' cut from Singapore, Dubai releases|author=Tim Fitzsimons|date=2019-12-26|work=[[Wikipedia:NBC|NBC]]|archivedate=20240526000255}}</ref>

=====Between the personal and the political=====
[[File:HerEyesOnYou.jpg|thumb|right|350px|The Women's March in January 2017, protesting the incoming Trump administration, photographed by Kevin Mazur.]]
[[Wikipedia:Rolling Stone|''Rolling Stone'']] magazine noted in its cover story of ''The Last Jedi'' that images and slogans associated with Princess Leia Organa were a common sight during the January 2017 [[Wikipedia:2017 Women's March|Women's March]], held less than a month after Carrie Fisher's death, in response to the [[Wikipedia:Misogyny|misogyny]] of the newly inaugurated President Trump. Interviewed by the magazine in late 2017, [[Daisy Ridley]] described the saga as a "wonderful mix" of what she thought was "somewhat a reflection of society" and "escapism, because there are creatures and there are people running around with fucking lasers and shit."<ref name="Rolling Stone 2017">{{WebCite|url=https://www.rollingstone.com/tv-movies/tv-movie-features/jedi-confidential-inside-the-dark-new-star-wars-movie-122234|text=Jedi Confidential: Inside the Dark New 'Star Wars' Movie|author=Brian Hiatt|date=2017-12-29|work=[[Wikipedia:Rolling Stone|''Rolling Stone'']]|archivedate=20220627032536}}</ref> Interviewed by [[StarWars.com]] in 2022, [[Kelly Marie Tran]] said that she felt her character Rose Tico, introduced in ''The Last Jedi'', is resonant with her own cultural and personal experience of growing up with parents who were "ripped from the place they lived"&mdash;Tran's parents survived the Vietnam War during their childhood and were among the [[Wikipedia:Vietnamese boat people|Vietnamese boat people]] who managed to settle in the United States after fleeing their home country at the end of the war, and they made personal sacrifices to raise their family, even initially disapproving Tran's efforts to become an actor as it seemed impossible.<ref name="Kelly SW.com">{{SW|url=news/kelly-marie-tran-interview|text="Imagine Yourself Doing Impossible Things": A Conversation with Kelly Marie Tran for AANHPI Heritage Month}}</ref>

Upon the publication of [[Christie Golden]]'s novel ''[[Battlefront II: Inferno Squad]]'' in July 2017, she expressed in an interview that ''Star Wars'' media had been influenced by the development of mass media and the Internet in the real-world, replying to a question about moving beyond the clear-cut morality of popular culture prompted by World War II. Golden's works explore characters across different factions and points of view, including the Empire's [[Inferno Squad]] as a [[Wikipedia:Covert operation|covert operation]] unit, and the author said that although society is "more polarized than ever," greater opportunities to reach out beyond one's world views meant that it is more difficult to "demonize people and make them one dimensional."<ref name="Golden Inferno">{{WebCite|url=https://www.theverge.com/2017/7/23/16015266/star-wars-battlefront-2-inferno-squad-christie-golden-sdcc-2017|text=Inferno Squad author Christie Golden on injecting moral ambiguity into Star Wars|author=Andrew Liptak|date=2017-07-23|work=[[Wikipedia:The Verge|''The Verge'']]|archivedate=20240223234935}}</ref>

[[File:Vote-SWTwitter.png|thumb|left|250px|A post from the official ''Star Wars'' Twitter account in the eve of the 2020 US elections]]
While promoting the final movie of the sequel trilogy, ''The Rise of Skywalker'', Lucasfilm's President Kathleen Kennedy similarly said in 2019 that the modern world has lost the "sense of innocence" of the 1970s and the "black-and-white" sensibility of World War II; contemporary storytelling would reflect the changed society of today. Director J.J. Abrams compared the First Order's origins to a hypothetical scenario of "the Argentine Nazis" actually banding together to continue their pursuit of fascism.<ref name="TROS Vanity" /> Slavery was not welcome in the otherwise criminal setting of Kijimi City, which was not only styled after Kurosawa films but also took inspiration from Nazi-occupied [[Wikipedia:Paris|Paris]] and Michael Curtiz's 1942 film [[Wikipedia:Casablanca (film)|''Casablanca'']] of the [[Wikipedia:French protectorate in Morocco|Moroccan]] colony under [[Wikipedia:Vichy France|Nazi-collaborationist]] French control.<ref name="Art of TROS">''[[The Art of Star Wars: The Rise of Skywalker]]''</ref> Abrams also took an approach different from the nostalgia of ''The Force Awakens'' and focused on how a new, young generation confronts the "debt that has come before," "less about restoring an old age" but "more about preserving a sense of freedom and not being one of the oppressed."<ref name="TROS Vanity" />

On [[October 27]], [[2020]], one week ahead of the [[November 3]] [[Wikipedia:2020 United States elections|United States elections]], the official ''Star Wars'' Twitter account posted an image of the word "VOTE"&mdash;styled as a blue hologram containing the [[Rebel Alliance starbird]] in place of the letter "O"&mdash;with the text "Make your voice heard."<ref name="2020 election">{{Twitter|starwars|status/1321169578219585536|[[Star Wars]]|archivefile=File:Vote-SWTwitter.png}}</ref> In 2023, Ian McDiarmid compared Palpatine to "a certain contemporary politician" because "the word loser" was likely the worst thing either could ever hear.<ref name="Sequel Villains">{{YouTube|channel=@DisneylandExperience|name=Disneyland Experience|video=A5Ie2E2Ra-Q|text=Villains of the Sequel Trilogy Panel Full – Star Wars Celebration 2023}}</ref>

=====Black Lives Matter=====
{{Quote|You are important. Your individual power, your individual right, is very, very important. We can all join together to make this a better world.|John Boyega, speaking at a Black Lives Matter rally in 2020|<ref name="Boyega speech">{{OfficialYouTube|video=GGXEB25WdyQ|text=John Boyega George Floyd protest London speech in full: Star Wars actor's powerful Hyde Park message|channel=@EveningStandard|name=Evening Standard|date=2020-06-03}}</ref>}}
[[File:JohnBoyega-BLM.jpg|thumb|right|350px|John Boyega speaking in London, June 3, 2020]]
The [[Wikipedia:COVID-19 pandemic|COVID-19 pandemic]] spread worldwide in 2020.<ref name="COVID">{{WebCite|url=https://apnews.com/article/covid-cia-trump-china-pandemic-lab-leak-9ab7e84c626fed68ca13c8d2e453dde1|text=CIA believes COVID likely originated from a lab, but has low confidence in its own finding|author=David Klepper|date=2025-01-25|work=[[Wikipedia:Associated Press|Associated Press]]|archivedate=20250125232709}}</ref> During the [[Wikipedia:Black Lives Matter|Black Lives Matter]] protest in his home city of London in the [[Wikipedia:United Kingdom|United Kingdom]] on [[June 3]], 2020, John Boyega&mdash;who played the stormtrooper&ndash;turned&ndash;Resistance fighter Finn in the sequel trilogy&mdash;spoke out against the racism that Black people face even in the democratic world.<ref name="Boyega speech" /> On the same day, Lucasfilm expressed solidarity with Boyega and his speech,<ref name="Boyega SW.com">{{SW|url=news/lucasfilm-stands-with-john-boyega|text=Lucasfilm Stands with John Boyega}}</ref> and the actor publicly revealed his frustration with Disney and the wider Hollywood industry's unfair treatment of people of color in an interview released three months later. Boyega highlighted the dissonance between the sequels' initial marketing and the relatively lessened prominence of the characters played by people of color in ''The Last Jedi'', and he added that he thought J.J. Abrams tried to salvage Disney's mistreatment with his unplanned return to directing ''The Rise of Skywalker''.<ref name="Boyega GQ">{{WebCite|url=https://www.gq-magazine.co.uk/culture/article/john-boyega-interview-2020|text=John Boyega: 'I'm the only cast member whose experience of Star Wars was based on their race'|author=Jimi Famurewa|date=2020-09-02|work=[[Wikipedia:GQ|British ''GQ'']]|archivedate=20200902140627}}</ref>

The game show ''[[Star Wars: Jedi Temple Challenge]]'' was scheduled to be released on June 3, 2020, but producer and lead actor [[Ahmed Best]] announced its delay to [[June 10]] given widespread [[Wikipedia:Reactions to the murder of George Floyd|civil and violent reactions to George Floyd's murder by police]] in [[Wikipedia:Minneapolis|Minneapolis]] in the US several days prior, on [[May 25]].<ref name="Sabered Hand">{{Instagram|username=bestahmed|url=p/CA-psPIgtBY|name=[[Ahmed Best]]|date=2020-06-03|quote=I’m so pumped to share #jeditemplechallenge with everyone. The fan response at Celebration last year was the beginning of my return to the galaxy I love so much. #KelleranBeq “The Sabered Hand” is the first Black number one lead of anything #StarWars and that is so very important to me. I’m also a #producer of the show and that makes me doubly excited to bring this thing to you. But as a black man, a black father, a black producer and black artist I have to consider all that is happening in the world right now. Our team has decided to push the premiere of #Jeditemplechallenge until next Wednesday June 10th. Hopefully we can be in a better headspace next week to enjoy this game show. I’ve been a part of this family for twenty years and I’ve felt your support. The excitement behind #KelleranBeq has me bursting with ideas of lore and backstory. Can't wait to share all of that with you. Star Wars fans, I appreciate us.|archiveurl=https://archive.is/9CAEm}}</ref>

====Might in the East: growing autocracy====
=====China, Taiwan, and Hong Kong=====
In 2015, Kennedy stated that she appreciated the global leverage provided by Disney for Lucasfilm, which was able to make official in-roads into China and Russia.<ref name="Kathleen Disney">{{DisneyCompany|url=the-force-finally-awakens-the-highly-anticipated-star-wars-the-force-awakens-is-now-open|text=Q&A with the Force Behind Lucasfilm: President/Producer Kathleen Kennedy|date=2015-12-18}}</ref> While British Hong Kong freely watched ''Star Wars'' and produced the first ''Star Wars'' action figures alongside Taiwan and Portuguese Macau, the original trilogy only entered mainland China in the aftermath of the Cultural Revolution in [[#Chinese trade and empire|various unofficial ways]], with official collaborations between Chinese companies and Lucasfilm finally being arranged with the sequel trilogy's release through Disney.<ref name="Cultural Revolution" /> Compared to the original American posters for ''The Force Awakens'' and also Hong Kong's bilingual [[Wikipedia:Hong Kong written Chinese|Chinese]]-[[Wikipedia:Hong Kong English|English]] posters,<ref name="HKTFA">{{WebCite|url=https://www.starwarsmovieposter.com/posters/asia/china/hong-kong-force-awakens-version-b-one-sheet|text=Hong Kong The Force Awakens Version "B" One-Sheet|work=[https://www.starwarsmovieposter.com StarWarsMoviePoster.com]|archivedate=20240730222726}}</ref> when the movie was released in mainland China in January 2016, the poster design there significantly diminishes John Boyega's character and entirely removes his Latino co-star Oscar Isaac as Poe Dameron and the non-human characters Chewbacca and [[Maz Kanata]].<ref name="China TFA">{{WebCite|url=https://variety.com/2015/film/news/star-wars-china-poster-controversy-john-boyega-1201653494|text='Star Wars' China Poster Sparks Controversy After Shrinking John Boyega’s Character|author=Maane Khatchatourian|date=2015-12-04|work=[[Wikipedia:Variety (magazine)|''Variety'']]|archivedate=20151204200926}}</ref> In a 2018 interview, Boyega said that he became aware of the racist changes via social media but did not focus on it, instead taking pride of the fact that ''The Force Awakens'' would be seen by large numbers of people and he would appear throughout the movie.<ref name="Boyega poster">{{Twitter|username=BBC|url=status/1057618453165563904|name=[[Wikipedia:BBC|BBC]]|date=2018-10-31|archivedate=20220826131248}}</ref> Its premiere inspirited ''Star Wars'' fans in mainland China, who have [[Wikipedia:Internet censorship in China|been able to]] build up sizeable online fan communities and kept up with US-based ''Star Wars'' news via websites such as the unofficial encyclopedia [[Wookieepedia]].<ref name="NYT China" />

[[File:TaiwanPresidentialOfficeMay4.jpg|thumb|left|330px|''Star Wars'' Day celebration at Taiwan's Presidential Office]]
When the cover of the ''TIME'' magazine in June 2015 featured Taiwan's [[Wikipedia:President of the Republic of China|presidential]] candidate [[Wikipedia:Tsai Ing-wen|Tsai Ing-wen]] with the words "She could lead the only Chinese democracy," mainland Chinese state media censored the words and compared her picture to a picture of Master Yoda.<ref name="Tsai Yoda">{{WebCite|url=https://hongkongfp.com/2015/06/22/mainland-media-remove-chinese-democracy-from-tsai-ing-wen-time-cover|text='Chinese democracy' removed from TIME cover|date=2015-06-22|work=[[Wikipedia:Hong Kong Free Press|Hong Kong Free Press]]|nobackup=1}}</ref> Tsai was elected President. In 2018, the [[Wikipedia:Presidential Office Building, Taipei|Taiwan Presidential Office]] held a [[May 4|May the Fourth]] welcome event with ''Star Wars'' fans where Vice President [[Wikipedia:Chen Chien-jen|Chen Chien-jen]] remarked: "If you all come in peace, I welcome you all as our guests. Today Master Yoda ''[President Tsai]'' is absent, but I quote one phrase from Yoda&mdash;'size matters not'. Taiwan is bigger than you think."<ref name="May 4th">{{WebCite|url=https://www.thejakartapost.com/news/2018/05/04/may-the-force-be-with-you-in-taiwans-presidential-office-.html|text='May the force be with you' in Taiwan's presidential office|author=[[Wikipedia:Agence France-Presse|Agence France-Presse]]|date=2018-05-04|work=[[Wikipedia:The Jakarta Post|''The Jakarta Post'']]|archivedate=20180504182500}}</ref>

Both former [[Wikipedia:Chief Secretary for Administration|Chief Secretary]] [[Wikipedia:Anson Chan|Anson Chan]] and prominent youth activist [[Wikipedia:Joshua Wong|Joshua Wong]] of Hong Kong compared the city's [[Wikipedia:Hong Kong Police Force|Police Force]] to the stormtroopers of ''Star Wars'' in their "indiscriminate brutality" against peaceful young demonstrators during the [[Wikipedia:2014 Hong Kong protests|2014 Umbrella Movement]] and [[Wikipedia:2019–2020 Hong Kong protests|2019–2020 pro-democracy protests]]. Chan claimed in 2014 that Hong Kong's tradition of peaceful protest dated back to the 1989 Tiananmen crackdown, and yet the police and the governments of Hong Kong and China have routinely failed to honor their responsibilities, opting to "rule by law" rather than uphold the rule of law as stipulated by the Sino-British Joint Declaration and the local constitution. Wong claimed in 2020 that a [[Wikipedia:Second Cold War|new cold war]] was brewing between China and "the democratic world" and Hong Kong, with the sometimes-violent clashes between protesters and police, was one of its first battles.<ref name="Anson Chan">{{WebCite|url=https://time.com/3447838/hong-kong-democracy-china-protests-anson-chan|text=Hong Kong Is Ready for Democracy, but China Isn't Ready for a Free Hong Kong|author=[[Wikipedia:Anson Chan|Anson Chan]]|date=2014-09-30|work=[[Wikipedia:Time (magazine)|TIME]]|archivedate=20140930112422}}</ref><ref name="Joshua Wong">{{WebCite|url=https://www.theguardian.com/books/2020/feb/22/unfree-speech-joshua-wong-jason-y-ng-review|text=Unfree Speech by Joshua Wong review – a young life of protest in Hong Kong|author=Julia Lovell|date=2020-02-22|work=[[Wikipedia:The Guardian|''The Guardian'']]|archivedate=20200326155356}}</ref> Artwork of the Hong Kong protest movement feature local imagery alongside Hollywood popular culture imagery, including ''Star Wars'', in what scholars describe as an attempt to reframe Hong Kong in good versus evil terms, especially for citizens who dually identify as localists and cosmopolitans, and also amplify international outreach.<ref name="Journal">{{WebCite|url=https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/full/10.1177/18681026231187002|text=Hong Kong's Networked Agitprop: Popular Nationalism in the Wake of the 2019 Anti-Extradition Protests|author=Milan Ismangil and Florian Schneider|date=2023|work=Journal of Current Chinese Affairs|nobackup=1}}</ref> Similar ''Star Wars'' artwork were made during the [[Wikipedia:2020–2021 Thai protests|pro-democracy protests in Thailand]] in 2020.<ref name="Thai rubber ducks">{{WebCite|url=https://globalvoices.org/2020/11/25/rubber-ducks-in-thai-protests-inspire-solidarity-and-memes|text=Rubber ducks in Thai protests inspire solidarity and memes|date=2020-11-25|work=Global Voices|archivedate=20201125153827}}</ref>

[[File:HongKong1997Skyline.jpg|thumb|right|300px|Hong Kong in 1997, with a ''Star Wars'' logo from a commemorative edition of action figures]]
[[Wikipedia:Benny Tai|Benny Tai]], a legal scholar and one of the main figures of the Umbrella Movement, wrote opinion pieces published by [[Wikipedia:Apple Daily|''Apple Daily'']] following the screenings of ''The Last Jedi'' and ''The Rise of Skywalker'', linking specific parts of the films to specific plans and hopes for Hong Kong's democratic movement, including the [[Wikipedia:2019 Hong Kong local elections|2019 local elections]] and the [[Wikipedia:Postponement of the 2020 Hong Kong legislative election|2020 legislative election]].<ref name="Benny Tai 2018">{{WebCite|url=https://collection.news/appledaily/articles/E2LJU7I5WWMSTWROGMPGR6664U|text=星球大戰與香港民主運動<br />（港大法律系副教授　戴耀廷） - 戴耀廷|author=[[Wikipedia:Benny Tai|Benny Tai]]|date=2018-01-30|work=[https://collection.news 聞庫]|language=[[Wikipedia:Hong Kong written Chinese|Hong Kong written Chinese]]|nobackup=1}} {{C|original link on censored news site [[Wikipedia:Apple Daily|''Apple Daily'']] irretrievable}}</ref><ref name="Benny Tai 2019">{{WebCite|url=https://collection.news/appledaily/articles/R2U2TVE37BG2J2P3NJMZK6K34E|text=星球大戰的啟示 - 戴耀廷|author=[[Wikipedia:Benny Tai|Benny Tai]]|date=2019-12-24|work=[https://collection.news 聞庫]|language=[[Wikipedia:Hong Kong written Chinese|Hong Kong written Chinese]]|nobackup=1}} {{C|original link on censored news site [[Wikipedia:Apple Daily|''Apple Daily'']] irretrievable}}</ref> The Chinese government used COVID-19 to [[Wikipedia:Postponement of the 2020 Hong Kong legislative election|postpone]] the legislative election and added the [[Wikipedia:2020 Hong Kong national security law|national security law]] to the local constitution before [[Wikipedia:Apple Daily raids and arrests|shutting down]] ''Apple Daily'' and arresting the "[[Wikipedia:Hong Kong 47|Hong Kong 47]]" group of pro-democracy figures including Tai in 2021, which was condemned by the [[Wikipedia:G7|G7]] and [[Wikipedia:European Union|European Union]] and prompted the UK government to declare that the People's Republic of China is in a state of "ongoing non-compliance" of the Sino-British Joint Declaration, a UN-registered international law, following three breaches.<ref name="Joint Declaration">{{WebCite|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/robertolsen/2021/03/14/uk-says-china-breached-hong-kong-handover-treaty-for-third-time|text=U.K. Says China Breached Hong Kong Handover Treaty Yet Again|author=Robert Olsen|work=[[Wikipedia:Forbes|''Forbes'']]|archivedate=20211214010420}}</ref> During Benny Tai's trial in 2024, a government-appointed national security judge characterised the [[Wikipedia:2020 Hong Kong pro-democracy primaries|2020 pro-democracy primary election]] candidates as "stormtroopers" for planning to win a majority of seats in the legislative election and to force the local government's resignation by voting down the government budget, and the judge added that the court intended to find their "Darth Vader." Tai later pleaded guilty and the court stated he was the "mastermind" of the primaries.<ref name="Benny Tai">{{WebCite|url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250312002537/https://channelchk.com/即時熱話/47人案-辯方指-國安法-生效後戴耀廷已退出-重申不能控制他人取態非-主腦-|text=47人案｜辯方指《國安法》生效後戴耀廷已退出　重申不能控制他人取態非「主腦」|date=2024-06-26|work=[[Wikipedia:Channel C|''Channel C'']]|nobackup=1}} {{C|original link on the now-censored news site [[Wikipedia:Channel C|''Channel C'']]}}</ref>

The ''Star Wars: Galactic Conflict'' video game was developed by China's [[Wikipedia:Yoozoo Games|Yoozoo Games]] and approved by Chinese authorities between 2019 and 2020,<ref name="More legal">{{WebCite|url=https://youxiputao.com/article/24204|text=游族诉迪士尼，要求赔偿232万；游戏涉黄，联运方被罚3万|date=2022-10-31|work=[https://youxiputao.com Youxi Putao]|archivedate=20230322175545}}</ref> and a Taiwanese company would have handled distribution outside the mainland via a subsidiary in Hong Kong that was founded in response to the [[Wikipedia:China–United States trade war|trade war]] between China and the United States<ref name="Gravity AP19">{{WebCite|url=https://www.gravitytw.com/file/download/2c9b80827d92ced0017d98c56fd9013a|text=2019AnnualReport|work=[https://www.gravitytw.com Gravity]|language=[[Wikipedia:Traditional Chinese characters|Traditional Chinese]]|archivedate=20240311074306}}</ref>&mdash;the game was never released amidst [[Star Wars: Galactic Conflict#Development|various irregularities]].<ref name="Legal">{{WebCite|url=http://www.gamelook.com.cn/2022/10/501226|text=合作告吹游戏停摆，游族与迪士尼因《星球大战》IP授权取消起冲突|date=2022-10-28|work=[http://www.gamelook.com.cn GameLook]|archivedate=20230115142719}}</ref> The official Chinese web-novel ''The Vow of Silver Dawn''<ref name="Cultural Revolution" /> was released between 2020 and 2021<ref name="Webnovel">{{WebCite|url=https://www.qidian.com/book/1015950219|text=星球大战：白银誓约(国王陛下)小说在线阅读|language=[[Wikipedia:Simplified Chinese characters|Simplified Chinese]]|archivedate=20260514162024}}</ref> to explore the ''Star Wars'' galaxy [[The Vow of Silver Dawn#Plot summary|from a Chinese cultural context]].<ref name="Cultural Revolution" /> Among the web-novel's characteristics is the setting of Aakaash system, ruled by a corrupt corporation that isolated itself from the Republic, brainwashed citizens in news media, enforced disappearances, and suppressed the dragon-like Loongren ethnic group.<ref name="Vow" /> For ''Star Wars'' Day on May 4, 2021, the [[Wikipedia:Green Party Taiwan|Green Party of Taiwan's]] press release included a picture of [[Wikipedia:Censorship of Winnie-the-Pooh in China|Winnie the Pooh]] with a Darth Vader mask and stated that Taiwan is on the front line against the Galactic Empire of the real world&mdash;the Chinese imperialism shown in [[Wikipedia:Xinjiang|Xinjiang]], [[Wikipedia:Tibet|Tibet]], and Hong Kong&mdash;and that Taiwanese people would stand strong for freedom and democracy alongside the rest of the "free world" while calling on Europe to take a firm stance on China amidst its infiltration and information warfare.<ref name="Taiwan Greens">{{WebCite|url=https://web.greenparty.org.tw/posts/news/20210504|text=【世界星戰日 帝國威脅仍在 綠黨不會退縮】|date=2021-05-04|work=[[Wikipedia:Green Party Taiwan|Green Party Taiwan]]|language=[[Wikipedia:Traditional Chinese characters|Traditional Chinese (Taiwan)]]|archivedate=20210518132313}}</ref>

=====Russia, information war, and Ukraine=====
{{Quote|War and high stakes and hearing air raid sirens and being in a highly militarized environment&mdash;none of that was abstract for me. It was all very real. ''[&hellip;]'' The nature of the show, but also the franchise in general, is that it has the room, the breadth, and depth to be able to absorb a lot of different historical events. [''Star Wars''] can transpose them in ways that provide a certain kind of dramatic distance. ''[It can]'' allow us to understand those events better because they’re put through this filter.|Beau Willimon, on writing for ''Andor'' while in Ukraine|<ref name="Willimon Ukraine">{{WebCite|url=https://www.indiewire.com/awards/consider-this/andor-season-2-saw-gerrera-speech-oral-history-1235133299|text=‘Andor’ Writer Beau Willimon Breaks Down Saw Gerrera’s ‘Absolutely Wackadoodle’ Rhydo Speech|author=Ben Travers|date=2025-06-20|work=[[Wikipedia:IndieWire|''IndieWire'']]|archivedate=20250621101126}}</ref>}}
[[File:Resist-U24.jpg|thumb|left|300px|''Resist'', by Justin Aram, with Mark Hamill's autograph. The poster raised over $1.5 million for drones to support Ukrainian defenders.]]
Information war by the Russian Federation against the democratic world involved the ''Star Wars'' fandom directly. A study published in the [[Wikipedia:First Monday (journal)|''First Monday'']] journal in 2018 reported that a significant number of Twitter posts harassing Rian Johnson over ''The Last Jedi'' are characteristic of Russian troll accounts, which manipulate and exploit the politicization of popular culture discourse, particularly misogynistic, anti-[[w:c:lgbtqia:Gay|gay]], and [[Wikipedia:Alt-right|alt-right]] beliefs, akin to 2014's [[Wikipedia:Gamergate (harassment campaign)|Gamergate]] harassment campaign and Russia's electoral interference targeting the United States.<ref name="Russian trolls">{{WebCite|url=https://www.researchgate.net/publication/328820777_Weaponizing_the_haters_The_Last_Jedi_and_the_strategic_politicization_of_pop_culture_through_social_media_manipulation|text=Weaponizing the haters: The Last Jedi and the strategic politicization of pop culture through social media manipulation|author=Morten Bay|date=2018-11-15|work=[[Wikipedia:ResearchGate|ResearchGate]]|archivedate=20191225070859}}</ref>

According to an [[Wikipedia:English language|English-language]] article by the Russian government's [[Wikipedia:Russia Beyond|''Russia Beyond'']] media outlet in 2015, the ''Zebra'' youth television program in [[Wikipedia:Saint Petersburg|Leningrad]] (Saint Petersburg) illegally broadcast dubbed clips of ''Star Wars'' in 1989, but the [[Wikipedia:Consulate General of the United States, Saint Petersburg|US Consulate General]] allowed ''Zebra'' to continue doing so. ''Star Wars'' was finally released in the Soviet Union in the summer of 1990. The Soviet Union was succeeded by the Russian Federation following its collapse in 1991. The ''Russia Beyond'' article goes on to mention that a 2001 episode of the satirical television series [[Wikipedia:Puppets (TV series)|''Kukly'']] had a puppet of the "recently elected president [[Wikipedia:Vladimir Putin|Vladimir Putin]]" play as Luke Skywalker while a puppet of ex-president [[Wikipedia:Boris Yeltsin|Boris Yeltsin]] played as Obi-Wan Kenobi.<ref name="Russia Beyond">{{WebCite|url=https://www.rbth.com/longreads/star_wars|text=Star Wars in USSR|date=2015|work=[[Wikipedia:Russia Beyond|''Russia Beyond'']]|archivedate=20230207052824}}</ref> The article neglects to mention that Putin's regime took over the broadcaster that year.<ref name="RFE">{{WebCite|url=https://www.rferl.org/a/russia_independent_ntv_fell_silent/3557594.html|text=Ten Years Ago, Russia's Independent NTV, The Talk Of The Nation, Fell Silent|date=2011-04-14|work=[[Wikipedia:Radio Free Europe/Radio Liberty|Radio Free Europe/Radio Liberty]]|archivedate=20180130155650}}</ref>

Following [[Wikipedia:Ukraine|Ukraine's]] pro-democracy and pro-European [[Wikipedia:Revolution of Dignity|Revolution of Dignity]] in 2014, Putin launched Russia's [[Wikipedia:Russo-Ukrainian war|war of aggression against Ukraine]].<ref name="VF">{{WebCite|url=https://www.vanityfair.com/hollywood/2022/09/darth-vaders-voice-emanated-from-war-torn-ukraine|text=Darth Vader's Voice Emanated From War-Torn Ukraine|author=Breznican, Anthony|date=2022-09-23|work=[[Wikipedia:Vanity Fair (magazine)|''Vanity Fair'']]|archivedate=20220923151721}}</ref> In 2015, the Ukrainian city of [[Wikipedia:Odesa|Odesa]] invited sculptor [[Wikipedia:Oleksandr Milov|Oleksandr Milov]] to transform a [[Wikipedia:Statue of Darth Vader|statue]] of the Soviet Union founder [[Wikipedia:Vladimir Lenin|Vladimir Lenin]] into Darth Vader. The city also planned to install a [[Wikipedia:Wi-Fi|Wi-Fi]] router in the dictator's head so that he could communicate with other Sith.<ref name="Vader of Odesa">{{WebCite|url=https://www.dw.com/en/darth-vader-replaces-lenin-statue-in-odessa-ukraine/a-18802474|text=Darth Vader replaces Lenin in Ukraine|author=[[Wikipedia:Agence France-Presse|Agence France-Presse]] and [[Wikipedia:Deutsche Presse-Agentur|Deutsche Presse-Agentur]]|date=2015-10-23|work=[[Wikipedia:Deutsche Welle|Deutsche Welle]]|archivedate=20151024013544}}</ref>

[[File:Respeecher-DarthHungary.png|thumb|right|320px|Work on ''Star Wars'' continued in Ukraine, taking advantage of AI technology.]]
Russia's [[Wikipedia:2022 Russian invasion of Ukraine|full-scale invasion of Ukraine]] in February 2022 disrupted the Ukrainian speech-synthesis start-up [[Respeecher]]'s work on the ''[[Star Wars: Obi-Wan Kenobi]]'' television series. As Ukraine defended itself and the democratic world from Russian advances, Respeecher employees finished replicating [[James Earl Jones]]' voice as Darth Vader via [[Wikipedia:Artificial intelligence|artificial intelligence]] with the actor's consent, having previously replicated actor [[Mark Hamill]]'s voice as a young Luke Skywalker for ''Star Wars: The Mandalorian'' and ''[[Star Wars: The Book of Boba Fett]]''.<ref name="VF" /> In September 2022, Hamill joined Ukraine's [[Wikipedia:United24|United24]] fundraising campaign as an ambassador to acquire an "Army of [[Wikipedia:Unmanned aerial vehicle|Drones]]," with [[Wikipedia:President of Ukraine|Ukrainian President]] [[Wikipedia:Volodymyr Zelenskyy|Volodymyr Zelenskyy]] thanking Hamill's support and echoing ''Star Wars''{{'}} theme that "good will triumph over evil and light will overcome darkness."<ref name="U24">{{WebCite|url=https://apnews.com/article/mark-hamill-ukraine-drones-46cfb2c53d43e7e1b4e270d0292a5f32|text=Mark Hamill talks fundraising for Ukraine, love of politics|author=Martina Rebecca Inchingolo|date=2022-12-14|work=[[Wikipedia:Associated Press|Associated Press]]|archivedate=20221214175707}}</ref> Over 500 drones were acquired via Hamill's campaign by October.<ref name="500drones">{{WebCite|url=https://www.foxnews.com/entertainment/star-wars-actor-mark-hamill-donated-500-drones-ukraine-forces-charity|text='Star Wars' actor Mark Hamill donated 500 drones to Ukraine forces through charity|author=Peter Aitken|date=2022-10-21|work=[[Wikipedia:Fox News|Fox News]]|archivedate=20221104152507}}</ref> From [[February 17]] to [[March 24]], 2023, United24 and Hamill ran a raffle for ten autographed copies of ''Resist'', a poster by Justin Aram depicting Ukraine as one X-wing facing a Russian fleet of Imperial Star Destroyers. The partnership raised over $1.5 million USD from ''Star Wars'' fans, which bought RQ-35 Heidrun reconnaissance drones for Ukrainian defenders.<ref name="ResistPosterPortfolio">{{WebCite|url=https://justinaram.com/portfolio/resist|text=Resist Poster|work=[https://justinaram.com Portfolio of Justin Aram]|archivedate=20241211090752}}</ref><ref name="ResistPosterU24">{{WebCite|url=https://u24.gov.ua/news/hamill_posters|text=Mark Hamill Raffles Autographed Star Wars Posters in Exchange for a Donation to Support Ukraine|date=2023-02-17|work=[[Wikipedia:United24|United24]]|archivedate=20230219065228}}</ref>

Ukrainian actress [[Ivanna Sakhno]], who played [[Shin Hati]] in the 2023 television series ''[[Star Wars: Ahsoka]]'', is also an ambassador for United24 and the [[Wikipedia:International Rescue Committee|International Rescue Committee]] and advocating for her country, which is "fighting for democratic values around the world."<ref name="Sakhno Forbes">{{YouTube|video=TGmg0m7qzRM|text=Ahsoka Star Ivanna Sakhno Wants To Make Sure The World 'Doesn't Look Away' From Ukraine|channel=@Forbes|name=[[Wikipedia:Forbes|''Forbes'']]|date=2024-03-08}}</ref> Russian air raids continued targeting Ukrainian civilians, and in March 2023, Mark Hamill provided his voice for the Ukrainian "Air Alert" app's English-language version.<ref name="Hamill air alert">{{WebCite|url=https://apnews.com/article/russia-ukraine-star-wars-luke-hamill-app-08ec03bf1a2c9c0378857090079f00f9|text=Mark Hamill lends 'Star Wars' voice to Ukrainian air-raid app|author=John Leicester|date=2023-03-28|work=[[Wikipedia:Associated Press|Associated Press]]|archivedate=20260226000941}}</ref> In August 2024, Hamill and historian [[Wikipedia:Timothy Snyder|Timothy Snyder]] helped United24 launch the "Safe Terrain" fundraiser for thirty Zmiy Ground Demining Complexes, UGV SIRKO-S1 Remote Controlled Platforms, and KNLR-E Multi-purpose Tracked Platforms to neutralize Russian mines&mdash;"these are the robots you are raising money for."<ref name="Safe Terrain">{{WebCite|url=https://u24.gov.ua/safeterrain|text=Safe Terrain|work=[[Wikipedia:United24|United24]]|archivedate=20251218060032}}</ref> In March 2022, [[Wikipedia:Propstore|Prop Store]] auctioned an original ''Star Wars'' poster by the [[Wikipedia:Brothers Hildebrandt|Brothers Hildebrandt]] for £13,000 [[Wikipedia:Pound sterling|GBP]], which were donated to the [[Wikipedia:Disasters Emergency Committee|Disasters Emergency Committee's]] humanitarian appeal for Ukraine.<ref name="Hildebrandt Ukraine">{{WebCite|url=https://www.independent.co.uk/news/uk/ukraine-american-luke-skywalker-disasters-emergency-committee-james-bond-b2043958.html|text=Rare Star Wars poster exceeds its top estimate in sale for Ukraine|date=2022-03-25|work=[[Wikipedia:The Independent|''The Independent'']]|archivedate=20220412093443}}</ref>

For [[Andor Season 2|Season 2]] of the television series ''[[Star Wars: Andor]]'', American writer [[Beau Willimon]] was contributing from Ukraine during the early months of the full-scale invasion, from February 2022.<ref name="Willimon Ukraine" /> On [[November 7]], 2022, [[Wikipedia:Simon Coveney|Simon Coveney]], as the Chair of the [[Wikipedia:Committee of Ministers of the Council of Europe|Committee of Ministers of the Council of Europe]] and [[Wikipedia:Ireland|Ireland's]] Minister for Foreign Affairs, addressed the [[Wikipedia:World Forum for Democracy|World Forum for Democracy]] with references to ''Star Wars''&mdash;the sequel trilogy's Ahch-To scenes were filmed in Ireland, and though reality is more complex than the dichotomous framing of "democrats as Jedi and autocrats as Empire," Russia is clearly a tyranny whereas Ukraine is on the front line of democracy, and democracy could only endure if the world fights for it in the same vein as Yoda's words: "Try not. Do, or do not. There is no try."<ref name="Coveney 2022">{{WebCite|url=https://rm.coe.int/world-forum-for-democracy-address-minister-simon-coveney/1680a91a52|text=6 - Address for WfD|work=[[Wikipedia:Council of Europe|Council of Europe]]|archivedate=20250814074634}}</ref> [[Wikipedia:NATO Review|''NATO Review'']] published an essay by Ukrainian journalist and soldier [[Wikipedia:Pavlo Kazarin|Pavlo Kazarin]] on [[February 16]], 2023, that concluded the genuine, united spirit of ordinary Ukrainian people is defining the world of the [[Wikipedia:21st century|21st century]] akin to the underdogs "blowing up the Death Star" in fictional epics like ''Star Wars''.<ref name="NATO Review">{{WebCite|url=http://nato.int/docu/review/articles/2023/02/16/a-year-ago-i-volunteered-as-a-soldier-in-the-ukrainian-army/index.html|text=A year ago I volunteered as a soldier in the Ukrainian army|author=[[Wikipedia:Pavlo Kazarin|Pavlo Kazarin]]|date=2023-02-16|work=[[Wikipedia:NATO Review|''NATO Review'']]|archivedate=20230224044203|nolive=1}}</ref>

On [[September 25]], 2025, France's Minister for Europe and Foreign Affairs, [[Wikipedia:Jean-Noël Barrot|Jean-Noël Barrot]], spoke at the US' [[Wikipedia:Harvard Kennedy School|Harvard Kennedy School]] about Western democratic backsliding in the face of Russia and China with references to ''Star Wars''. Barrot likened Vladimir Putin to Darth Sidious, using the classic authoritarian playbook of identifying proxies and fostering a fake separatist threat to launch "colonial wars" against [[Wikipedia:Georgia (country)|Georgia]] from 2008 and Ukraine from 2014, as well as [[Wikipedia:Russian sabotage operations in Europe|directly attacking]] the European Union covertly and attempting to manipulate elections in Germany, [[Wikipedia:Romania|Romania]], and [[Wikipedia:Moldova|Moldova]]. Given that reality is not the fiction of ''Star Wars'', Barrot urged his audience not to wait for "the return of the Jedi" but to stand up for active citizenship, alongside those risking their lives for freedom around the world, and focus on "fixing democracy."<ref name="Barrot 2025">{{WebCite|url=https://www.diplomatie.gouv.fr/en/the-ministry-and-its-network/news/article/speech-by-jean-noel-barrot-minister-for-europe-and-foreign-affairs-at-harvard|text=Speech by Jean-Noël Barrot, Minister for Europe and Foreign Affairs at Harvard Kennedy School (25 septembre 2025)|date=2025-09-25|work=[[Wikipedia:Ministry for Europe and Foreign Affairs (France)|Ministère de l'Europe et des Affaires étrangères]]|archivedate=20251005162219}}</ref>

===Evolution of culture, geopolitics, and AI&mdash;2020s===
====''The High Republic====
Launched on [[January 5]], 2021 following several months of delay in light of the COVID-19 pandemic, ''[[Star Wars: The High Republic]]'' is a multimedia project set during a renaissance era of the Jedi and the Galactic Republic, beginning with works primarily by authors [[Cavan Scott]], [[Claudia Gray]], [[Charles Soule]], [[Daniel José Older]], and [[Justina Ireland]].<ref name="Revised Dates">{{SW|url=news/star-wars-the-high-republic-sees-revised-release-dates|text=''Star Wars: The High Republic'' Sees Revised Release Dates}}</ref> Starlight Beacon is a central setting of the project's [[Phase I: Light of the Jedi|Phase I]] and is "very closely tied" to New York's [[Wikipedia:World Trade Center (1973–2001)|World Trade Center]] and the Twin Towers' collapse in the September 11 attacks of 2001.<ref name="THR Ireland">{{WebCite|url=https://www.laughingplace.com/w/disney-entertainment/interview-star-wars-high-republic-tessa-gratton-justina-ireland|text=Interview &#8211; &#8220;Star Wars: The High Republic&#8221; Authors Tessa Gratton and Justina Ireland Discuss Their Work at SDCC 2024|author=Mike Celestino|date=2024-07-30|work=Laughing Place|archivedate=20240731012129}}</ref> [[Phase II: Quest of the Jedi|Phase II]] features the Eiram and E'ronoh War, for which author [[Zoraida Córdova]] researched the World Wars and the Cold War about the cyclical nature of human conflict.<ref name="Convergence SWNN">{{WebCite|url=https://www.starwarsnewsnet.com/2022/11/interview-author-zoraida-cordova-talks-about-the-hope-she-brings-to-the-high-republic-in-convergence.html|text=Interview: Author Zoraida Córdova Talks About the Hope She Brings to the High Republic in 'Convergence'|author=Kyle Larson|date=2022-11-25|work=[https://www.starwarsnewsnet.com Star Wars News Net]|archivedate=20221125141304}}</ref>

[[Phase III: Trials of the Jedi|Phase III]] features the Stormwall, which was similarly influenced by the Berlin Wall; Justina Ireland was serving in the US army during 9/11, and as a child, she had happy childhood memories of news about the [[Wikipedia:Fall of the Berlin Wall|fall of the Berlin Wall]] and the supposed end of communism in November 1989. Ireland also said that her fellow authors had also lived through such "touchstone historical moments" that could influence their writing.<ref name="THR Ireland" /> [[Tessa Gratton]] wrote the 2024 novel ''[[The High Republic: Temptation of the Force]]'' focusing on love and politics, having struggled with the seeming contradiction of a Jedi's duty to uphold justice and dismissal of "love" as simply attachment in ''Star Wars'' while also appreciating that politics in practice is messy, yet the setting has "a lot of really clear-cut lines" that allow ideas to be clearly expressed via politics. The author thought that a person could not truly believe in justice if they do not love something, and the time of the High Republic allowed Yoda to support Elzar Mann emotionally unlike Anakin Skywalker in the prequel trilogy because the Jedi were not yet deeply entangled with the Republic's politics.<ref name="Gratton love">{{WebCite|url=https://www.starwarsnewsnet.com/2024/07/swnn-interview-temptation-of-the-force-author-tessa-gratton-on-connecting-the-high-republic-attachment-in-star-wars-and-more.html|text=SWNN Interview: ‘Temptation of the Force’ Author Tessa Gratton on Connecting the High Republic, Attachment in ‘Star Wars’, and More|author=Tyler Bradshaw and Nate Manning|date=2024-07-08|work=[https://www.starwarsnewsnet.com Star Wars News Net]|archivedate=20240710141522}}</ref>

[[File:Starlight_Beacon_High_Republic_Trailer.jpg|thumb|left|340px|Starlight Beacon, a centerpiece in ''The High Republic'' storytelling]]
In 2024, [[Daniel José Older]] retweeted a [[November 21]] Twitter post from a reader of his [[Star Wars: The High Republic Adventures (2023)|''Star Wars: The High Republic Adventures'']] comic series expressing that the Valo story arc, spanning [[The High Republic Adventures (2023) 11|issues 11]] and [[The High Republic Adventures (2023) 12|12]] published in the preceding days, felt "like a commentary on current events, specifically about Palestine."<ref name="Palestine">{{Twitter|username=aDillonDev|url=status/1859701605219877086|name=James Coruscanti Diner III|date=2024-11-21|quote=The Valo arc in The High Republic Adventures strongly feels like a commentary on current events, specifically about Palestine. Not sure when these issues were written, but some of Ram's internal dialogue rings really true.|archiveurl=https://archive.is/f8lqU}} &mdash; [https://archive.is/q0apP retweeted] by [[Daniel José Older]]</ref> Amidst [[Wikipedia:Middle Eastern crisis (2023–present)|heightened regional armed conflict]] involving the [[Wikipedia:Iran|Islamic Republic of Iran]] and State of Israel's authoritarian governments and their allies and proxies since the [[Wikipedia:October 7 attacks|October 7 terrorist attacks]] on Israel in 2023, international controversy surrounds Israeli forces' retaliatory violence against Palestinians, especially in the [[Wikipedia:Gaza Strip|Gaza Strip]].<ref name="Gaza">{{WebCite|url=https://lordslibrary.parliament.uk/humanitarian-situation-in-gaza|text=Humanitarian situation in Gaza|author=James Tobin|date=2024-02-05|work=[[Wikipedia:House of Lords Library|House of Lords Library]]|archivedate=20250121232408}}</ref><ref name="7/10">{{WebCite|url=https://static1.squarespace.com/static/67bf0490d422da027d74c55c/t/67d80d04b3bde77ec3ac2b66/1742212374048/The+7+October+Parliamentary+Commission+Report+-+The+Roberts+Report+-+APPG+UK-Israel.pdf|text=The Roberts Report|author=All-Party Parliamentary Group on UK-Israel|date=2025|work=[https://www.7octparliamentarycommission.co.uk 7 October Parliamentary Commission]|archivedate=20250318205950}}</ref> Older later quoted an excerpt of a review of the twelfth issue on Twitter: "In ''Star Wars The High Republic Adventures'' #12 Daniel José Older reminds us of a fundamental premise behind ''Star Wars'': Resistance is Perseverance. Standing up for freedom in the midst of colonization and apartheid lies in persistence of hope and persistence of action."<ref name="Perseverance">{{Twitter|username=djolder|url=status/1860502204269940816|name=Daniel José Older|date=2024-11-24|quote=In ''Star Wars The High Republic Adventures'' #12 Daniel José Older reminds us of a fundamental premise behind ''Star Wars'': Resistance is Perseverance. Standing up for freedom in the midst of colonization and apartheid lies in persistence of hope and persistence of action.|archivedate=20241124015426}}</ref>

====''Andor'' Season One====
{{Quote|Our world is exploding in different places right now, people's rights are disappearing, and ''Andor'' reflects that. ''[In the show]'' the Empire is taking over, and it feels like the same thing is happening in reality, too. I was impressed by Tony's social-realist intentions. He's created a whole new morality. It's very deep and humane&mdash;there is grief, mourning, hope, fear. It's not just primary colours here.|Fiona Shaw, in a 2022 interview on Tony Gilroy's ''Andor'' series|<ref name="Trumpian world">{{WebCite|url=https://www.empireonline.com/tv/news/andor-star-wars-take-trumpian-world-fiona-shaw-exclusive|text=Andor Is Star Wars' 'Scurrilous Take On The Trumpian World,' Says Fiona Shaw|author=Ben Travis|date=2022-08-02|work=[[Wikipedia:Empire (magazine)|''Empire Magazine'']]|archivedate=20221123200912}}</ref>}}
[[File:Andor-NewsSlider.jpg|thumb|right|340px|''Andor'' drew from a plethora of revolutionary histories.]]
Showrunner Tony Gilroy characterized his work on ''Andor'' as an amateur historian and [[Wikipedia:Late bloomer|late bloomer]] who learnt traditional ways of storytelling from writers like [[Wikipedia:William Goldman|William Goldman]] and had a golden opportunity from Disney and Lucasfilm President Kathleen Kennedy to turn 1500 pages on revolution and the rise of authoritarianism into a drama series.<ref name="Horowitz Gilroy">{{YouTube|video=4Hk3w9W0ZjA|text=Tony Gilroy talks ANDOR, STAR WARS, BOURNE, MICHAEL CLAYTON &#124; Happy Sad Confused|channel=@JoshHorowitz|name=Josh Horowitz|date=2025-05-19}}</ref> When Gilroy was asked about political resonances in 2022's [[Andor Season 1|''Andor'' Season 1]] in November of that year, he stated that the ''Andor'' was written without contemporary politics in mind, drawing on historical revolutions simply as spontaneous events to drive the characters' adventures&mdash;"whatever contemporary resonance it has is usually in the eye of the beholder. Oppression is oppression."<ref name="Oppression">{{WebCite|url=https://www.indiewire.com/features/general/andor-not-political-disney-plus-tony-gilroy-interview-1234780620|text='Andor': Tony Gilroy's 'Star Wars' Series Wasn't Meant to be Political|author=Proma Khosla|date=2022-11-09|work=[[Wikipedia:IndieWire|''IndieWire'']]|archivedate=20230518200016}}</ref> Gilroy emphasized that his personal politics are "absolutely not important" to the series' themes and that ''Star Wars'' allows plentiful comparisons to history. As to the "age old question" of "who's a terrorist and who's a freedom fighter"&mdash;which has asked of [[Wikipedia:Thomas Jefferson|Thomas Jefferson]] with the American Revolution and its [[Wikipedia:Continental Congress|Continental Congress]], [[Wikipedia:Nelson Mandela|Nelson Mandela]] with the [[Wikipedia:African National Congress|African National Congress']] anti-apartheid armed resistance in South Africa, the nation-building of Israel with the [[Wikipedia:King David Hotel bombing|Irgun bombings]], the Haitian Revolution, the French Revolution with the [[Wikipedia:The Mountain|Montagnard]] behind the original "Terror,"<ref name="Themes">{{WebCite|url=https://nerdist.com/article/andor-showrunner-tony-gilroy-interview-season-one-themes-hope-community-cassian-future|text=ANDOR Showrunner Tony Gilroy on Season 1's Powerful Themes and the Future|author=Michael Walsh|date=2022-11-23|work=[[Wikipedia:Nerdist|Nerdist]]|archivedate=20221123110025}}</ref><ref name="Andor finale Deadline">{{WebCite|url=https://deadline.com/2022/11/andor-season-one-finale-tony-gilroy-interview-season-two-spoilers-1235181298|text=‘Andor’: Tony Gilroy On Cassian’s Season One Finale Ultimatum;
